class BatchReadCodesThread(QThread):
    """
    Scans multiple PDF and PostScript files for barcodes and QR codes in a background thread.
    """
    progress_updated = pyqtSignal(int, int, str)  # current, total, current_filename
    code_found = pyqtSignal(dict)
    scan_finished = pyqtSignal(str)

    def __init__(self, file_paths, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.file_paths = file_paths
        self.is_running = True
        # NEW: Initialize the PostScript converter within the thread
        try:
            self.ps_converter = PostScriptConverter()
        except Exception as e:
            self.ps_converter = None
            print(f"Could not initialize PostScript converter: {e}")

    def run(self):
        total_files = len(self.file_paths)
        found_any = False
        
        for i, file_path in enumerate(self.file_paths):
            if not self.is_running:
                break
            
            self.progress_updated.emit(i + 1, total_files, os.path.basename(file_path))
            
            doc = None
            is_temp_file = False
            path_to_scan = file_path

            try:
                # MODIFICATION: Check file type and convert if it's PostScript
                if file_path.lower().endswith(('.ps', '.eps')):
                    if not self.ps_converter:
                        print(f"Skipping PostScript file because converter is not available: {os.path.basename(file_path)}")
                        continue
                    # Convert PS to a temporary PDF
                    temp_pdf_path = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=".pdf")
                    self.ps_converter.ps_to_pdf(file_path, temp_pdf_path)
                    path_to_scan = temp_pdf_path
                    is_temp_file = True

                # Now, process the file (either original PDF or temporary PDF)
                doc = fitz.open(path_to_scan)
                if doc.is_encrypted:
                    print(f"File {os.path.basename(file_path)} is encrypted, skipping scan.")
                    continue

                for page_num in range(doc.page_count):
                    if not self.is_running:
                        break
                    
                    page = doc.load_page(page_num)
                    pix = page.get_pixmap(dpi=200)
                    img = Image.frombytes("RGB", [pix.width, pix.height], pix.samples)
                    decoded_objects = pyzbar.decode(img)
                    
                    if decoded_objects:
                        found_any = True
                        for obj in decoded_objects:
                            try:
                                data = obj.data.decode('utf-8')
                            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                                try: data = obj.data.decode('big5')
                                except: data = str(obj.data)

                            self.code_found.emit({
                                'filename': os.path.basename(file_path), # Report the original filename
                                'path': file_path,
                                'page': page_num + 1,
                                'type': obj.type,
                                'data': data
                            })
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error processing file {os.path.basename(file_path)}: {e}")
            finally:
                if doc:
                    doc.close()
                # NEW: Clean up temporary PDF file if one was created
                if is_temp_file and os.path.exists(path_to_scan):
                    try:
                        os.remove(path_to_scan)
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"Could not remove temp file {path_to_scan}: {e}")


        summary = "Scan complete." if found_any else "Scan complete. No barcodes or QR codes were found in any files."
        self.scan_finished.emit(summary)

    def stop(self):
        self.is_running = False

class BatchReadCodesDialog(QDialog):
    """
    Dialog for batch reading of barcodes and QR codes from multiple PDF and PostScript files.
    """
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.parent_window = parent
        self.thread = None
        self.file_paths = []
        
        self.setWindowTitle("Batch Barcode / QR Code Reader")
        self.setMinimumSize(1200, 800)
        self.setup_ui()

    # setup_ui, remove_selected_files, clear_all_files, start_scan, stop_scan,
    # update_progress, add_result_to_table, on_scan_finished, export_results,
    # export_as_csv, export_as_excel, and closeEvent methods remain UNCHANGED from the previous version.
    # The only changes are in add_files and add_folder.

    def setup_ui(self):
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        splitter = QSplitter(Qt.Horizontal)
        left_panel = QFrame()
        left_layout = QVBoxLayout(left_panel)
        file_group = QGroupBox("Source Files")
        file_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.file_list_widget = QListWidget()
        self.file_list_widget.setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView.ExtendedSelection)
        file_layout.addWidget(self.file_list_widget)
        file_buttons_layout = QGridLayout()
        self.add_files_button = QPushButton("Add Files")
        self.add_folder_button = QPushButton("Add Folder")
        self.remove_button = QPushButton("Remove Selected")
        self.clear_button = QPushButton("Clear All")
        file_buttons_layout.addWidget(self.add_files_button, 0, 0)
        file_buttons_layout.addWidget(self.add_folder_button, 0, 1)
        file_buttons_layout.addWidget(self.remove_button, 1, 0)
        file_buttons_layout.addWidget(self.clear_button, 1, 1)
        file_layout.addLayout(file_buttons_layout)
        file_group.setLayout(file_layout)
        left_layout.addWidget(file_group)
        splitter.addWidget(left_panel)
        right_panel = QFrame()
        right_layout = QVBoxLayout(right_panel)
        results_group = QGroupBox("Scan Results & Control")
        results_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        control_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.start_button = QPushButton("Start Scan")
        self.stop_button = QPushButton("Stop Scan")
        self.stop_button.setEnabled(False)
        control_layout.addWidget(self.start_button)
        control_layout.addWidget(self.stop_button)
        results_layout.addLayout(control_layout)
        status_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.progress_bar = QProgressBar()
        self.status_label = QLabel("Ready")
        status_layout.addWidget(self.progress_bar)
        status_layout.addWidget(self.status_label, 1)
        results_layout.addLayout(status_layout)
        self.results_table = QTableWidget()
        self.results_table.setColumnCount(4)
        self.results_table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["Filename", "Page", "Type", "Decoded Data"])
        self.results_table.setSelectionBehavior(QAbstractItemView.SelectRows)
        self.results_table.setEditTriggers(QAbstractItemView.NoEditTriggers)
        self.results_table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView.Stretch)
        self.results_table.setSortingEnabled(True)
        results_layout.addWidget(self.results_table)
        results_group.setLayout(results_layout)
        right_layout.addWidget(results_group)
        splitter.addWidget(right_panel)
        main_layout.addWidget(splitter)
        splitter.setSizes([400, 800])
        bottom_buttons_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.export_button = QPushButton("Export Results")
        self.close_button = QPushButton("Close")
        bottom_buttons_layout.addStretch()
        bottom_buttons_layout.addWidget(self.export_button)
        bottom_buttons_layout.addWidget(self.close_button)
        main_layout.addLayout(bottom_buttons_layout)
        self.add_files_button.clicked.connect(self.add_files)
        self.add_folder_button.clicked.connect(self.add_folder)
        self.remove_button.clicked.connect(self.remove_selected_files)
        self.clear_button.clicked.connect(self.clear_all_files)
        self.start_button.clicked.connect(self.start_scan)
        self.stop_button.clicked.connect(self.stop_scan)
        self.export_button.clicked.connect(self.export_results)
        self.close_button.clicked.connect(self.close)
        
    def add_files(self):
        # MODIFICATION: Update file filter to include PostScript files
        paths, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileNames(self, "Select PDF or PostScript Files", "", "Supported Files (*.pdf *.ps *.eps);;PDF Files (*.pdf);;PostScript Files (*.ps *.eps)")
        if paths:
            for path in paths:
                if path not in self.file_paths:
                    self.file_paths.append(path)
                    self.file_list_widget.addItem(os.path.basename(path))

    def add_folder(self):
        folder = QFileDialog.getExistingDirectory(self, "Select Folder")
        if folder:
            for root, _, files in os.walk(folder):
                for file in files:
                    # MODIFICATION: Check for multiple supported extensions
                    if file.lower().endswith((".pdf", ".ps", ".eps")):
                        path = os.path.join(root, file)
                        if path not in self.file_paths:
                            self.file_paths.append(path)
                            self.file_list_widget.addItem(os.path.basename(path))

    def remove_selected_files(self):
        selected_items = self.file_list_widget.selectedItems()
        if not selected_items: return
        for item in reversed(selected_items):
            row = self.file_list_widget.row(item)
            self.file_list_widget.takeItem(row)
            del self.file_paths[row]

    def clear_all_files(self):
        self.file_list_widget.clear()
        self.file_paths.clear()

    def start_scan(self):
        if not self.file_paths:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", "Please add at least one file first.")
            return
        self.results_table.setRowCount(0)
        self.start_button.setEnabled(False)
        self.stop_button.setEnabled(True)
        self.status_label.setText("Preparing to scan...")
        self.thread = BatchReadCodesThread(self.file_paths)
        self.thread.progress_updated.connect(self.update_progress)
        self.thread.code_found.connect(self.add_result_to_table)
        self.thread.scan_finished.connect(self.on_scan_finished)
        self.thread.start()

    def stop_scan(self):
        if self.thread and self.thread.isRunning():
            self.thread.stop()
            self.status_label.setText("Stopping...")

    def update_progress(self, current, total, filename):
        self.progress_bar.setMaximum(total)
        self.progress_bar.setValue(current)
        self.status_label.setText(f"({current}/{total}) Scanning: {filename}")

    def add_result_to_table(self, result):
        row_position = self.results_table.rowCount()
        self.results_table.insertRow(row_position)
        self.results_table.setItem(row_position, 0, QTableWidgetItem(result['filename']))
        self.results_table.setItem(row_position, 1, QTableWidgetItem(str(result['page'])))
        self.results_table.setItem(row_position, 2, QTableWidgetItem(result['type']))
        self.results_table.setItem(row_position, 3, QTableWidgetItem(result['data']))

    def on_scan_finished(self, summary):
        self.status_label.setText(summary)
        self.start_button.setEnabled(True)
        self.stop_button.setEnabled(False)
        self.thread = None
        if self.progress_bar.maximum() > 0:
            self.progress_bar.setValue(self.progress_bar.maximum())

    def export_results(self):
        if self.results_table.rowCount() == 0:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Info", "There are no results to export.")
            return
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Export Results", "", "Excel Files (*.xlsx);;CSV Files (*.csv)")
        if not path: return
        try:
            if path.lower().endswith('.xlsx'): self.export_as_excel(path)
            elif path.lower().endswith('.csv'): self.export_as_csv(path)
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Export failed: {e}")

    def export_as_csv(self, path):
        with open(path, 'w', newline='', encoding='utf-8-sig') as file:
            writer = csv.writer(file)
            headers = [self.results_table.horizontalHeaderItem(i).text() for i in range(self.results_table.columnCount())]
            writer.writerow(headers)
            for row in range(self.results_table.rowCount()):
                row_data = [self.results_table.item(row, col).text() for col in range(self.results_table.columnCount())]
                writer.writerow(row_data)
        self.status_label.setText(f"Results successfully exported to {os.path.basename(path)}")

    def export_as_excel(self, path):
        wb = Workbook()
        ws = wb.active
        ws.title = "Scan Results"
        headers = [self.results_table.horizontalHeaderItem(i).text() for i in range(self.results_table.columnCount())]
        ws.append(headers)
        for row in range(self.results_table.rowCount()):
            row_data = [self.results_table.item(row, col).text() for col in range(self.results_table.columnCount())]
            ws.append(row_data)
        for i, header_cell in enumerate(ws[1]):
            column_letter = get_column_letter(i + 1)
            ws.column_dimensions[column_letter].auto_size = True
        ws_summary = wb.create_sheet("Summary Report")
        summary_data = [
            ("Total Files Scanned", len(self.file_paths)),
            ("Files with Barcodes", len(set(self.results_table.item(row, 0).text() for row in range(self.results_table.rowCount())))),
            ("Total Barcodes Found", self.results_table.rowCount()),
            ("Report Generated On", datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"))
        ]
        for row_data in summary_data:
            ws_summary.append(row_data)
        ws_summary.column_dimensions['A'].width = 25
        ws_summary.column_dimensions['B'].width = 25
        wb.save(path)
        self.status_label.setText(f"Results and summary successfully exported to {os.path.basename(path)}")
        
    def closeEvent(self, event):
        self.stop_scan()
        super().closeEvent(event)

class PDFImageLabel(QLabel):
    """
    一個自訂的 QLabel，專門用於顯示 PDF 頁面並處理滑鼠框選事件。
    (已重寫滑鼠事件以確保全向選取功能)
    """
    # 定義一個自訂信號，當選取完成時發出，參數為選取的 QRect
    selection_finished = pyqtSignal(QRect)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.selecting = False
        self.origin_point = QPoint()
        self.selection_rect = QRect()
        self.setMouseTracking(True) # 啟用滑鼠追蹤

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        """滑鼠按下事件：記錄起點並開始選取。"""
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            self.selecting = True
            self.origin_point = event.pos()
            self.selection_rect = QRect(self.origin_point, QSize())
            self.update()
        super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event):
        """滑鼠移動事件：手動計算標準化矩形並更新。"""
        if self.selecting:
            # 手動計算矩形的左上角和右下角，確保座標永遠是有效的
            x1 = self.origin_point.x()
            y1 = self.origin_point.y()
            x2 = event.pos().x()
            y2 = event.pos().y()

            # 計算標準化的左上角、寬度和高度
            left = min(x1, x2)
            top = min(y1, y2)
            width = abs(x1 - x2)
            height = abs(y1 - y2)
            
            # 創建一個新的、永遠有效的矩形
            self.selection_rect = QRect(left, top, width, height)
            self.update()
        super().mouseMoveEvent(event)

    def mouseReleaseEvent(self, event):
        """滑鼠釋放事件：結束選取並發出信號。"""
        if self.selecting and event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            self.selecting = False
            # 只有當選取框有實際大小時才發出信號
            if self.selection_rect.width() > 5 and self.selection_rect.height() > 5:
                self.selection_finished.emit(self.selection_rect)
            self.update()
        super().mouseReleaseEvent(event)

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        """繪圖事件：先繪製原始內容，再繪製選取框。"""
        super().paintEvent(event)
        if self.selecting and self.selection_rect.isValid():
            painter = QPainter(self)
            # 使用更清晰的顏色和樣式
            pen = QPen(QColor(0, 120, 215, 200), 2, Qt.SolidLine)
            painter.setPen(pen)
            brush = QBrush(QColor(0, 120, 215, 70))
            painter.setBrush(brush)
            painter.drawRect(self.selection_rect)
            painter.end()

class ReadCodesThread(QThread):
    """Scans for barcodes and QR codes on PDF pages in a background thread."""
    code_found = pyqtSignal(dict)
    progress_updated = pyqtSignal(int, int)
    scan_finished = pyqtSignal(str)

    def __init__(self, doc, pages_to_scan, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.doc = doc
        self.pages_to_scan = pages_to_scan
        self.is_running = True

    def run(self):
        total_pages = len(self.pages_to_scan)
        found_any = False
        
        for i, page_num in enumerate(self.pages_to_scan):
            if not self.is_running:
                break
            
            self.progress_updated.emit(i + 1, total_pages)
            
            try:
                page = self.doc.load_page(page_num)
                # Use a higher DPI to improve recognition success rate
                pix = page.get_pixmap(dpi=300)
                
                # Convert the pixmap to a Pillow Image object
                img = Image.frombytes("RGB", [pix.width, pix.height], pix.samples)
                
                # Decode using pyzbar
                decoded_objects = pyzbar.decode(img)
                
                if decoded_objects:
                    found_any = True
                    for obj in decoded_objects:
                        try:
                            # Decode the data from bytes to a utf-8 string
                            data = obj.data.decode('utf-8')
                        except UnicodeDecodeError:
                            # If utf-8 decoding fails, try using another encoding or just display the raw bytes
                            try:
                                data = obj.data.decode('latin1') # 'latin1' or 'big5' could be alternatives
                            except:
                                data = str(obj.data)

                        self.code_found.emit({
                            'page': page_num + 1,
                            'type': obj.type,
                            'data': data,
                            'rect': obj.rect # (left, top, width, height)
                        })
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error processing page {page_num + 1}: {e}")

        summary = "Scan complete." if found_any else "Scan complete. No barcodes or QR codes were found."
        self.scan_finished.emit(summary)

    def stop(self):
        self.is_running = False

class ReadCodesDialog(QDialog):
    """A dialog for reading barcodes and QR codes from a PDF."""
    jump_to_code_requested = pyqtSignal(int, object)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.parent_window = parent
        self.doc = parent.doc
        self.thread = None
        
        self.setWindowTitle("Read Barcodes / QR Codes")
        self.setMinimumSize(800, 600)
        self.setup_ui()

    def setup_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        # 1. Scan Options
        options_group = QGroupBox("Scan Options")
        options_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        
        self.scan_all_radio = QRadioButton("Scan All Pages")
        self.scan_current_radio = QRadioButton("Scan Current Page Only")
        self.scan_range_radio = QRadioButton("Scan Specific Pages")
        self.scan_range_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.scan_range_edit.setPlaceholderText("e.g., 1, 3, 5-8")
        self.scan_range_edit.setEnabled(False)
        
        self.scan_all_radio.setChecked(True)
        self.scan_range_radio.toggled.connect(self.scan_range_edit.setEnabled)

        options_layout.addWidget(self.scan_all_radio)
        options_layout.addWidget(self.scan_current_radio)
        options_layout.addWidget(self.scan_range_radio)
        options_layout.addWidget(self.scan_range_edit)
        options_group.setLayout(options_layout)
        layout.addWidget(options_group)

        # 2. Control Buttons
        control_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.start_button = QPushButton("Start Scan")
        self.start_button.clicked.connect(self.start_scan)
        self.stop_button = QPushButton("Stop Scan")
        self.stop_button.setEnabled(False)
        self.stop_button.clicked.connect(self.stop_scan)
        control_layout.addWidget(self.start_button)
        control_layout.addWidget(self.stop_button)
        layout.addLayout(control_layout)

        # 3. Progress and Status
        status_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.progress_bar = QProgressBar()
        self.status_label = QLabel("Ready")
        status_layout.addWidget(self.progress_bar)
        status_layout.addWidget(self.status_label, 1)
        layout.addLayout(status_layout)

        # 4. Results Table
        self.results_table = QTableWidget()
        self.results_table.setColumnCount(3)
        self.results_table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["Page", "Type", "Decoded Data"])
        self.results_table.setSelectionBehavior(QAbstractItemView.SelectRows)
        self.results_table.setEditTriggers(QAbstractItemView.NoEditTriggers)
        self.results_table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(2, QHeaderView.Stretch)
        self.results_table.itemDoubleClicked.connect(self.jump_to_code)
        layout.addWidget(self.results_table)

        # 5. Action Buttons
        action_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.copy_button = QPushButton("Copy Selected Data")
        self.copy_button.clicked.connect(self.copy_selected_data)
        # --- MODIFICATION START ---
        self.export_button = QPushButton("Export Results") # Changed from "Export Results (CSV)"
        # --- MODIFICATION END ---
        self.export_button.clicked.connect(self.export_results)
        self.close_button = QPushButton("Close")
        self.close_button.clicked.connect(self.close)
        
        action_layout.addStretch()
        action_layout.addWidget(self.copy_button)
        action_layout.addWidget(self.export_button)
        action_layout.addWidget(self.close_button)
        layout.addLayout(action_layout)

    def start_scan(self):
        if not self.doc:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", "Please open a PDF file first.")
            return

        try:
            pages_to_scan = []
            if self.scan_all_radio.isChecked():
                pages_to_scan = list(range(self.doc.page_count))
            elif self.scan_current_radio.isChecked():
                pages_to_scan = [self.parent_window.current_page]
            elif self.scan_range_radio.isChecked():
                pages_to_scan = self.parent_window.parse_page_range(self.scan_range_edit.text())
                if pages_to_scan is None:
                    # parse_page_range will show an error message
                    return 

            if not pages_to_scan:
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", "No valid pages to scan.")
                return

        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Page number parsing failed: {e}")
            return
            
        self.results_table.setRowCount(0)
        self.start_button.setEnabled(False)
        self.stop_button.setEnabled(True)
        self.status_label.setText("Scanning...")

        self.thread = ReadCodesThread(self.doc, pages_to_scan)
        self.thread.code_found.connect(self.add_result_to_table)
        self.thread.progress_updated.connect(self.update_progress)
        self.thread.scan_finished.connect(self.on_scan_finished)
        self.thread.start()

    def stop_scan(self):
        if self.thread and self.thread.isRunning():
            self.thread.stop()
            self.status_label.setText("Stopping...")

    def update_progress(self, current, total):
        self.progress_bar.setMaximum(total)
        self.progress_bar.setValue(current)
        self.status_label.setText(f"Scanning page {current} of {total}...")

    def add_result_to_table(self, result):
        row_position = self.results_table.rowCount()
        self.results_table.insertRow(row_position)
        
        page_item = QTableWidgetItem(str(result['page']))
        type_item = QTableWidgetItem(result['type'])
        data_item = QTableWidgetItem(result['data'])
        
        # Store the original data in the item for later use
        page_item.setData(Qt.UserRole, result)
        
        self.results_table.setItem(row_position, 0, page_item)
        self.results_table.setItem(row_position, 1, type_item)
        self.results_table.setItem(row_position, 2, data_item)
        
        self.results_table.resizeRowsToContents()

    def on_scan_finished(self, summary):
        self.status_label.setText(summary)
        self.start_button.setEnabled(True)
        self.stop_button.setEnabled(False)
        self.thread = None
        if self.progress_bar.maximum() > 0:
            self.progress_bar.setValue(self.progress_bar.maximum())

    def jump_to_code(self, item):
        row = item.row()
        result_item = self.results_table.item(row, 0)
        if result_item:
            result_data = result_item.data(Qt.UserRole)
            page_num_zero_based = result_data['page'] - 1
            
            # Use a PyMuPDF Rect object
            l, t, w, h = result_data['rect']
            # Convert the barcode position from image coordinates back to PDF page coordinates
            page = self.doc.load_page(page_num_zero_based)
            # Assuming DPI is 300
            mat = fitz.Matrix(300/72, 300/72) 
            # Inverse transformation matrix
            inv_mat = ~mat
            rect = fitz.Rect(l, t, l+w, t+h) * inv_mat
            
            self.parent_window.jump_to_page_with_highlight_rect(page_num_zero_based, rect)
            self.activateWindow()

    def copy_selected_data(self):
        selected_items = self.results_table.selectedItems()
        if not selected_items:
            return
            
        # We only copy the "Decoded Data" column
        row = selected_items[0].row()
        data_item = self.results_table.item(row, 2)
        if data_item:
            clipboard = QApplication.clipboard()
            clipboard.setText(data_item.text())
            self.status_label.setText("Copied to clipboard!")
            QTimer.singleShot(2000, lambda: self.status_label.setText("Ready"))

    def export_results(self):
        if self.results_table.rowCount() == 0:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Information", "There are no results to export.")
            return

        # Allow saving as both CSV and TXT
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "Export Results", "", "CSV Files (*.csv);;Text Files (*.txt)")
        if not path:
            return

        try:
            # Check which file format was chosen
            if path.lower().endswith('.txt'):
                # Export only barcode data to a TXT file
                with open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as file:
                    for row in range(self.results_table.rowCount()):
                        # Column 2 is "Decoded Data"
                        data_item = self.results_table.item(row, 2)
                        if data_item:
                            file.write(data_item.text() + '\n')
                self.status_label.setText(f"Barcode data exported to {os.path.basename(path)}")
            
            elif path.lower().endswith('.csv'):
                # Original CSV export logic
                with open(path, 'w', newline='', encoding='utf-8-sig') as file:
                    writer = csv.writer(file)
                    # Write header
                    writer.writerow([self.results_table.horizontalHeaderItem(i).text() for i in range(self.results_table.columnCount())])
                    # Write data
                    for row in range(self.results_table.rowCount()):
                        row_data = [self.results_table.item(row, col).text() for col in range(self.results_table.columnCount())]
                        writer.writerow(row_data)
                self.status_label.setText(f"Results exported to {os.path.basename(path)}")

        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Export failed: {e}")


    def closeEvent(self, event):
        self.stop_scan()
        super().closeEvent(event)


class CompressPDFThread(QThread):
    """Runs PDF compression in a background thread to avoid freezing the GUI."""
    status_message = pyqtSignal(str)
    progress_updated = pyqtSignal(int)
    progress_range_updated = pyqtSignal(int)
    compression_finished = pyqtSignal(str)

    def __init__(self, files, settings, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.files = files
        self.settings = settings
        self.is_running = True

    def run(self):
        total_files = len(self.files)
        self.progress_range_updated.emit(total_files)
        total_original_size = 0
        total_compressed_size = 0

        for i, file_path in enumerate(self.files):
            if not self.is_running:
                break

            self.status_message.emit(f"({i+1}/{total_files}) Starting to process: {os.path.basename(file_path)}...")
            
            try:
                # Determine output path
                if self.settings['output_folder']:
                    output_dir = self.settings['output_folder']
                    base_name = os.path.basename(file_path)
                    name, ext = os.path.splitext(base_name)
                    output_path = os.path.join(output_dir, f"{name}{self.settings['suffix']}{ext}")
                else:
                    name, ext = os.path.splitext(file_path)
                    output_path = f"{name}{self.settings['suffix']}{ext}"
                
                if os.path.exists(output_path) and not self.settings['overwrite']:
                    self.status_message.emit(f" -> Warning: File already exists, skipping '{os.path.basename(output_path)}'")
                    self.progress_updated.emit(i + 1)
                    continue
                
                original_size = os.path.getsize(file_path)
                total_original_size += original_size

                doc = fitz.open(file_path)

                if doc.is_encrypted:
                    self.status_message.emit(f" -> Warning: File is encrypted, skipping '{os.path.basename(file_path)}'")
                    doc.close()
                    self.progress_updated.emit(i + 1)
                    continue
                
                # Prepare save arguments
                save_args = {
                    'garbage': self.settings['garbage'],
                    'clean': self.settings['clean'],
                    'deflate': self.settings['deflate'],
                    'deflate_images': self.settings['deflate_images'],
                    'deflate_fonts': self.settings['deflate_fonts'],
                    'linear': self.settings['linear'],
                    'pretty': False
                }

                doc.save(output_path, **save_args)
                doc.close()

                compressed_size = os.path.getsize(output_path)
                total_compressed_size += compressed_size
                reduction = 100 * (1 - compressed_size / original_size) if original_size > 0 else 0
                
                self.status_message.emit(f" -> Success: Compression complete. Size reduced from {original_size/1024:.1f} KB to {compressed_size/1024:.1f} KB ({reduction:.1f}% reduction)")

            except Exception as e:
                self.status_message.emit(f" -> Error: An error occurred while processing '{os.path.basename(file_path)}': {e}")
            
            self.progress_updated.emit(i + 1)

        # Final report
        if total_original_size > 0:
            total_reduction = 100 * (1 - total_compressed_size / total_original_size)
            summary = (f"\nCompression Finished!\n"
                       f"Processed a total of {total_files} files.\n"
                       f"Total size reduced from {total_original_size / (1024*1024):.2f} MB to {total_compressed_size / (1024*1024):.2f} MB.\n"
                       f"Overall size reduction of {total_reduction:.2f}%.")
        else:
            summary = "\nCompression task finished (no files were processed)."
            
        self.compression_finished.emit(summary)

    def stop(self):
        self.is_running = False

class CompressPDFDialog(QDialog):
    """Dialog for detailed PDF compression options."""
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.parent_window = parent
        self.setWindowTitle("Compress PDF Files")
        self.setMinimumSize(650, 700)
        self.thread = None
        self.setup_ui()

    def setup_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        # 1. Source Selection
        source_group = QGroupBox("Select Source")
        source_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.source_current_radio = QRadioButton("Compress the currently open file")
        self.source_single_radio = QRadioButton("Select a single file")
        self.source_batch_radio = QRadioButton("Select a folder (batch compression)")
        self.source_current_radio.setChecked(True)
        
        # Check if a file is open
        if not self.parent_window or not self.parent_window.doc:
            self.source_current_radio.setEnabled(False)
            self.source_single_radio.setChecked(True)

        source_layout.addWidget(self.source_current_radio)
        source_layout.addWidget(self.source_single_radio)
        source_layout.addWidget(self.source_batch_radio)
        source_group.setLayout(source_layout)
        layout.addWidget(source_group)
        
        # 2. Output Settings
        output_group = QGroupBox("Output Settings")
        output_form = QFormLayout()
        
        self.output_path_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.output_path_button = QPushButton("Browse...")
        self.output_path_button.clicked.connect(self.browse_for_source)
        output_path_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        output_path_layout.addWidget(self.output_path_edit)
        output_path_layout.addWidget(self.output_path_button)
        output_form.addRow("Source Location:", output_path_layout)
        
        self.suffix_edit = QLineEdit("_compressed")
        output_form.addRow("Filename Suffix:", self.suffix_edit)

        self.overwrite_check = QCheckBox("Overwrite existing files")
        output_form.addRow(self.overwrite_check)
        
        output_group.setLayout(output_form)
        layout.addWidget(output_group)

        # Link source selection to UI state changes
        self.source_current_radio.toggled.connect(self.update_ui_state)
        self.source_single_radio.toggled.connect(self.update_ui_state)
        self.source_batch_radio.toggled.connect(self.update_ui_state)

        # 3. Compression Level
        level_group = QGroupBox("Compression Level")
        level_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.level_combo = QComboBox()
        self.level_combo.addItems([
            "Low Compression (Fastest)", 
            "Medium Compression (Balanced)", 
            "High Compression (Better)", 
            "Maximum Compression (Cleanest, Slowest)"
        ])
        self.level_combo.setCurrentIndex(1)
        level_layout.addWidget(self.level_combo)
        level_group.setLayout(level_layout)
        layout.addWidget(level_group)

        # 4. Advanced Options
        advanced_group = QGroupBox("Advanced Options")
        advanced_layout = QGridLayout()
        self.clean_check = QCheckBox("Clean and rewrite PDF structure (clean)")
        self.clean_check.setToolTip("Removes syntax errors and beautifies the PDF structure. May slightly increase file size but improves stability.")
        self.clean_check.setChecked(True)
        
        self.deflate_check = QCheckBox("Compress uncompressed streams (deflate)")
        self.deflate_check.setToolTip("Apply generic compression to all uncompressed parts of the PDF.")
        self.deflate_check.setChecked(True)
        
        self.deflate_images_check = QCheckBox("Compress images (deflate images)")
        self.deflate_images_check.setToolTip("Compress uncompressed images in the PDF.")
        self.deflate_images_check.setChecked(True)

        self.deflate_fonts_check = QCheckBox("Compress fonts (deflate fonts)")
        self.deflate_fonts_check.setToolTip("Compress unembedded fonts in the PDF.")
        self.deflate_fonts_check.setChecked(True)

        self.linear_check = QCheckBox("Optimize for Fast Web View (linearize)")
        self.linear_check.setToolTip("Linearize the PDF to allow the first page to be displayed faster on the web.")
        
        advanced_layout.addWidget(self.clean_check, 0, 0)
        advanced_layout.addWidget(self.deflate_check, 1, 0)
        advanced_layout.addWidget(self.deflate_images_check, 0, 1)
        advanced_layout.addWidget(self.deflate_fonts_check, 1, 1)
        advanced_layout.addWidget(self.linear_check, 2, 0)
        advanced_group.setLayout(advanced_layout)
        layout.addWidget(advanced_group)
        
        # 5. Status and Log
        status_group = QGroupBox("Status and Log")
        status_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.status_log = QTextEdit()
        self.status_log.setReadOnly(True)
        self.status_log.setLineWrapMode(QTextEdit.NoWrap)
        self.progress_bar = QProgressBar()
        status_layout.addWidget(self.progress_bar)
        status_layout.addWidget(self.status_log)
        status_group.setLayout(status_layout)
        layout.addWidget(status_group)

        # 6. Buttons
        button_box = QDialogButtonBox()
        self.start_button = button_box.addButton("Start Compression", QDialogButtonBox.AcceptRole)
        self.open_output_button = button_box.addButton("Open Output Folder", QDialogButtonBox.ActionRole)
        self.close_button = button_box.addButton("Close", QDialogButtonBox.RejectRole)
        self.open_output_button.setEnabled(False)

        self.start_button.clicked.connect(self.start_compression)
        self.open_output_button.clicked.connect(self.open_output_folder)
        self.close_button.clicked.connect(self.close)
        layout.addWidget(button_box)
        
        self.update_ui_state()

    def update_ui_state(self):
        """Updates the state of UI components based on the source selection."""
        if self.source_current_radio.isChecked():
            self.output_path_edit.setPlaceholderText("Will be saved in the same folder as the original file")
            self.output_path_edit.setText("")
            self.output_path_edit.setEnabled(False)
            self.output_path_button.setEnabled(False)
        elif self.source_single_radio.isChecked():
            self.output_path_edit.setPlaceholderText("Select the PDF file to compress...")
            self.output_path_edit.setText("")
            self.output_path_edit.setEnabled(True)
            self.output_path_button.setEnabled(True)
        elif self.source_batch_radio.isChecked():
            self.output_path_edit.setPlaceholderText("Select the source folder containing PDFs...")
            self.output_path_edit.setText("")
            self.output_path_edit.setEnabled(True)
            self.output_path_button.setEnabled(True)

    def browse_for_source(self):
        """Selects a file or folder based on the current mode."""
        if self.source_single_radio.isChecked():
            path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "Select PDF File to Compress", "", "PDF files (*.pdf)")
            if path:
                self.output_path_edit.setText(path)
        elif self.source_batch_radio.isChecked():
            path = QFileDialog.getExistingDirectory(self, "Select Source Folder Containing PDFs")
            if path:
                self.output_path_edit.setText(path)

    def start_compression(self):
        files_to_process = []
        output_folder = None

        # Validate and collect files
        if self.source_current_radio.isChecked():
            if self.parent_window and self.parent_window.original_pdf_path:
                files_to_process.append(self.parent_window.original_pdf_path)
            else:
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", "No open file available for compression.")
                return
        elif self.source_single_radio.isChecked():
            file_path = self.output_path_edit.text()
            if not file_path or not os.path.isfile(file_path):
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", "Please select a valid PDF file.")
                return
            files_to_process.append(file_path)
        elif self.source_batch_radio.isChecked():
            folder_path = self.output_path_edit.text()
            if not folder_path or not os.path.isdir(folder_path):
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", "Please select a valid source folder.")
                return
            # Let the user choose the output folder
            output_folder = QFileDialog.getExistingDirectory(self, "Select Output Folder")
            if not output_folder:
                return # User cancelled
            for fname in os.listdir(folder_path):
                if fname.lower().endswith('.pdf'):
                    files_to_process.append(os.path.join(folder_path, fname))
            if not files_to_process:
                QMessageBox.information(self, "Information", "No PDF files found in the source folder.")
                return

        settings = {
            'garbage': self.level_combo.currentIndex() + 1,
            'clean': self.clean_check.isChecked(),
            'deflate': self.deflate_check.isChecked(),
            'deflate_images': self.deflate_images_check.isChecked(),
            'deflate_fonts': self.deflate_fonts_check.isChecked(),
            'linear': self.linear_check.isChecked(),
            'overwrite': self.overwrite_check.isChecked(),
            'suffix': self.suffix_edit.text(),
            'output_folder': output_folder
        }
        
        self.start_button.setEnabled(False)
        self.close_button.setText("Cancel")
        self.open_output_button.setEnabled(False)
        self.status_log.clear()
        
        self.thread = CompressPDFThread(files_to_process, settings)
        self.thread.status_message.connect(self.status_log.append)
        self.thread.progress_updated.connect(self.progress_bar.setValue)
        self.thread.progress_range_updated.connect(self.progress_bar.setMaximum)
        self.thread.compression_finished.connect(self.on_compression_finished)
        self.thread.start()

    def on_compression_finished(self, summary):
        self.status_log.append(summary)
        self.start_button.setEnabled(True)
        self.close_button.setText("Close")
        
        # Enable the 'Open Folder' button
        # Store the last used output directory to be opened
        last_output_dir = None
        if self.source_current_radio.isChecked() and self.parent_window.original_pdf_path:
            last_output_dir = os.path.dirname(self.parent_window.original_pdf_path)
        elif self.source_single_radio.isChecked():
            last_output_dir = os.path.dirname(self.output_path_edit.text())
        elif self.source_batch_radio.isChecked():
            # The output folder was set in start_compression
            if self.thread and self.thread.settings['output_folder']:
                 last_output_dir = self.thread.settings['output_folder']
        
        if last_output_dir and os.path.isdir(last_output_dir):
            self._last_output_dir = last_output_dir
            self.open_output_button.setEnabled(True)
        
        self.thread = None

    def open_output_folder(self):
        if hasattr(self, '_last_output_dir') and os.path.isdir(self._last_output_dir):
            try:
                # This works on Windows, for cross-platform a better solution might be needed
                os.startfile(self._last_output_dir)
            except Exception as e:
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", f"Could not open folder: {e}")

    def closeEvent(self, event):
        if self.thread and self.thread.isRunning():
            reply = QMessageBox.question(self, 'Confirm Close',
                                       "Compression is in progress. Are you sure you want to stop it and close the window?",
                                       QMessageBox.Yes | QMessageBox.No, QMessageBox.No)
            if reply == QMessageBox.Yes:
                self.thread.stop()
                self.thread.wait() # Wait for the thread to finish cleanly
                event.accept()
            else:
                event.ignore()
        else:
            event.accept()


class ReorderWorker(QThread):
    """後台重排序工作線程 - 修復版本"""
    progress = pyqtSignal(str)
    finished = pyqtSignal(int, int, str)
    error = pyqtSignal(str)
    
    def __init__(self, file_path, source_page, target_position, doc_info):
        super().__init__()
        self.file_path = file_path
        self.source_page = source_page
        self.target_position = target_position
        self.doc_info = doc_info
        
    def run(self):
        try:
            self.progress.emit("正在準備重排序...")
            
            doc = self.doc_info['doc']
            total_pages = doc.page_count
            
            if self.source_page >= total_pages or self.target_position >= total_pages:
                self.error.emit("頁面索引超出範圍")
                return
            
            # 直接在記憶體中重排序
            self.progress.emit("正在重新組織頁面...")
            
            # 計算新的頁面順序
            page_order = list(range(total_pages))
            moved_page = page_order.pop(self.source_page)
            page_order.insert(self.target_position, moved_page)
            
            # 創建新文檔並插入頁面
            new_doc = fitz.open()
            
            for i, page_num in enumerate(page_order):
                try:
                    new_doc.insert_pdf(doc, from_page=page_num, to_page=page_num)
                    
                    # 每處理10頁更新一次進度
                    if i % 10 == 0:
                        progress_percent = int((i / total_pages) * 100)
                        self.progress.emit(f"正在處理頁面 {i+1}/{total_pages} ({progress_percent}%)")
                        
                except Exception as e:
                    self.error.emit(f"處理頁面 {page_num} 時發生錯誤: {str(e)}")
                    new_doc.close()
                    return
            
            self.progress.emit("正在保存文檔...")
            
            # 使用更安全的保存方式
            try:
                # 創建一個安全的臨時文件
                import uuid
                temp_filename = f"{self.file_path}.tmp_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
                
                new_doc.save(temp_filename)
                new_doc.close()
                
                # 驗證保存的文件
                test_doc = fitz.open(temp_filename)
                if test_doc.page_count != total_pages:
                    test_doc.close()
                    os.remove(temp_filename)
                    self.error.emit("保存的文檔頁數不匹配")
                    return
                test_doc.close()
                
                # 原子性替換
                if os.path.exists(self.file_path):
                    backup_path = f"{self.file_path}.backup"
                    if os.path.exists(backup_path):
                        os.remove(backup_path)
                    os.rename(self.file_path, backup_path)
                
                os.rename(temp_filename, self.file_path)
                
                # 清理備份文件
                if os.path.exists(backup_path):
                    os.remove(backup_path)
                
                self.progress.emit("重排序完成")
                self.finished.emit(self.source_page, self.target_position, self.file_path)
                
            except Exception as e:
                new_doc.close()
                if os.path.exists(temp_filename):
                    os.remove(temp_filename)
                self.error.emit(f"保存文檔時發生錯誤: {str(e)}")
                
        except Exception as e:
            self.error.emit(f"重排序過程中發生未知錯誤: {str(e)}")

class ThumbnailGenerator(QThread):
    thumbnailReady = pyqtSignal(str, int, object)
    progressUpdate = pyqtSignal(int, int)
    
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.task_queue = queue.Queue()
        self.running = True
        
    def add_task(self, file_path, doc, page_num):
        self.task_queue.put((file_path, doc, page_num))
        
    def run(self):
        while self.running:
            try:
                file_path, doc, page_num = self.task_queue.get(timeout=1)
                
                page = doc.load_page(page_num)
                matrix = fitz.Matrix(0.3, 0.3)  # 降低解析度
                pixmap = page.get_pixmap(matrix=matrix)
                
                img = QImage(pixmap.samples, pixmap.width, pixmap.height, 
                           pixmap.stride, QImage.Format_RGB888)
                qpixmap = QPixmap.fromImage(img)
                
                self.thumbnailReady.emit(file_path, page_num, qpixmap)
                self.task_queue.task_done()
                
            except queue.Empty:
                continue
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"縮略圖生成錯誤: {e}")
                
    def stop(self):
        self.running = False

class DropInsertIndicator(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent):
        super().__init__(parent)
        # 根據父類的縮放大小動態設置指示器高度
        if hasattr(parent, 'parent') and hasattr(parent.parent(), 'thumbnail_size'):
            height = parent.parent().thumbnail_size
        else:
            height = 150
        self.setFixedSize(4, height)
        self.setStyleSheet("background-color: #4a86e8; border-radius: 2px;")

class DraggablePageWidget(QLabel):
    def __init__(self, file_path, page_num, pixmap, parent, size):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.file_path = file_path
        self.page_num = page_num
        self.parent_dialog = parent
        self.selected = False
        self.thumbnail_size = size
        self.original_pixmap = pixmap  # 保存原始縮略圖
        
        self.setup_widget(pixmap)
        
    def setup_widget(self, pixmap):
        scaled_pixmap = pixmap.scaled(
            self.thumbnail_size, self.thumbnail_size, 
            Qt.KeepAspectRatio, Qt.SmoothTransformation
        )
        self.setPixmap(scaled_pixmap)
        self.setFixedSize(self.thumbnail_size + 20, self.thumbnail_size + 60)
        self.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.setAcceptDrops(True)
        
        # 啟用右鍵菜單
        self.setContextMenuPolicy(Qt.CustomContextMenu)
        self.customContextMenuRequested.connect(self.show_context_menu)
        
        self.update_style()
        
        # 創建頁碼標籤
        self.create_page_label()
        
    def create_page_label(self):
        """創建或更新頁碼標籤"""
        # 如果已經有頁碼標籤，先移除
        for child in self.children():
            if isinstance(child, QLabel) and child != self:
                child.deleteLater()
        
        # 創建新的頁碼標籤
        page_label = QLabel(f"Page {self.page_num + 1}", self)
        page_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        page_label.setStyleSheet("""
            color: white; 
            background-color: rgba(0, 0, 0, 120); 
            padding: 4px;
            font-size: 13px;
            font-weight: bold;
            border-radius: 3px;
        """)
        page_label.setGeometry(5, self.thumbnail_size + 25, self.thumbnail_size + 10, 25)
        page_label.show()
        
    def update_thumbnail_size(self, new_size):
        """更新縮略圖大小"""
        self.thumbnail_size = new_size
        
        # 重新縮放原始圖像
        scaled_pixmap = self.original_pixmap.scaled(
            self.thumbnail_size, self.thumbnail_size, 
            Qt.KeepAspectRatio, Qt.SmoothTransformation
        )
        self.setPixmap(scaled_pixmap)
        
        # 更新組件大小
        self.setFixedSize(self.thumbnail_size + 20, self.thumbnail_size + 60)
        
        # 重新創建頁碼標籤
        self.create_page_label()
        
        # 更新樣式
        self.update_style()
    
    def update_style(self):
        if self.selected:
            self.setStyleSheet("""
                QLabel {
                    border: 3px solid #4a86e8;
                    background-color: rgba(74, 134, 232, 50);
                    border-radius: 5px;
                    padding: 5px;
                }
            """)
        else:
            self.setStyleSheet("""
                QLabel {
                    border: 2px solid #555555;
                    background-color: #404040;
                    border-radius: 5px;
                    padding: 5px;
                }
                QLabel:hover {
                    border: 2px solid #777777;
                    background-color: #505050;
                }
            """)
    
    def show_context_menu(self, pos):
        """顯示右鍵菜單"""
        if hasattr(self.parent_dialog, 'show_context_menu'):
            # 將本地坐標轉換為全局坐標
            global_pos = self.mapToGlobal(pos)
            self.parent_dialog.show_context_menu(self, global_pos)
    
    def contextMenuEvent(self, event):
        """處理右鍵菜單事件（備用方案）"""
        if hasattr(self.parent_dialog, 'show_context_menu'):
            self.parent_dialog.show_context_menu(self, event.globalPos())
        else:
            super().contextMenuEvent(event)
    
    def mouseDoubleClickEvent(self, event):
        """處理雙擊事件"""
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            # 調用預覽功能
            if hasattr(self.parent_dialog, 'preview_page'):
                self.parent_dialog.preview_page(self.file_path, self.page_num)
            elif hasattr(self.parent_dialog, 'page_double_clicked'):
                self.parent_dialog.page_double_clicked(self, event)
        else:
            super().mouseDoubleClickEvent(event)
    
    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        """處理鼠標按下事件"""
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            self.drag_start_position = event.pos()
            
            # 立即處理單擊選擇
            if hasattr(self.parent_dialog, 'page_clicked'):
                self.parent_dialog.page_clicked(self, event)
                
        elif event.button() == Qt.RightButton:
            # 右鍵點擊 - 先選中當前頁面，然後顯示菜單
            if hasattr(self.parent_dialog, 'page_clicked'):
                self.parent_dialog.page_clicked(self, event)
            
            # 顯示右鍵菜單
            if hasattr(self.parent_dialog, 'show_context_menu'):
                self.parent_dialog.show_context_menu(self, event.globalPos())
        else:
            super().mousePressEvent(event)
    
    def mouseMoveEvent(self, event):
        """處理鼠標移動事件以啟動拖拽"""
        if (event.buttons() & Qt.LeftButton and 
            hasattr(self.parent_dialog, 'is_reordering') and 
            not self.parent_dialog.is_reordering):
            
            # 檢查是否移動了足夠的距離才開始拖拽
            if not hasattr(self, 'drag_start_position'):
                return
                
            if ((event.pos() - self.drag_start_position).manhattanLength() < 
                QApplication.startDragDistance()):
                return
            
            drag = QDrag(self)
            mimeData = QMimeData()
            
            # 設置拖拽數據
            drag_data = f"{self.file_path}|||{self.page_num}"
            mimeData.setText(drag_data)
            drag.setMimeData(mimeData)
            
            # 創建拖拽圖像
            try:
                pixmap = self.grab().scaled(100, 100, Qt.KeepAspectRatio, Qt.FastTransformation)
                
                # 添加半透明效果
                painter = QPainter(pixmap)
                painter.setCompositionMode(QPainter.CompositionMode_DestinationIn)
                painter.fillRect(pixmap.rect(), QColor(0, 0, 0, 160))
                painter.end()
                
                drag.setPixmap(pixmap)
                drag.setHotSpot(QPoint(50, 50))  # 設置為圖像中心
                
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error creating drag pixmap: {e}")
            
            # 執行拖拽
            drag.exec_(Qt.MoveAction)

class ZoomControlWidget(QWidget):
    """嵌入式縮放控制組件"""
    def __init__(self, parent):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setup_ui()
        
    def setup_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(10, 5, 10, 5)
        layout.setSpacing(8)
        
        # 縮放標籤
        zoom_label = QLabel("Zoom:")
        zoom_label.setStyleSheet("color: white; font-weight: bold;")
        layout.addWidget(zoom_label)
        
        # 縮小按鈕
        zoom_out_btn = QPushButton("−")
        zoom_out_btn.setFixedSize(30, 30)
        zoom_out_btn.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                background-color: #404040;
                border: 1px solid #555555;
                border-radius: 12px;
                color: white;
                font-weight: bold;
                font-size: 14px;
            }
            QPushButton:hover { background-color: #505050; }
            QPushButton:pressed { background-color: #606060; }
        """)
        
        # 縮放滑桿
        self.zoom_slider = QSlider(Qt.Horizontal)
        self.zoom_slider.setMinimum(100)
        self.zoom_slider.setMaximum(300)
        self.zoom_slider.setValue(150)
        self.zoom_slider.setFixedWidth(120)
        self.zoom_slider.setStyleSheet("""
            QSlider::groove:horizontal {
                border: 1px solid #555555;
                height: 6px;
                background: #333333;
                border-radius: 3px;
            }
            QSlider::handle:horizontal {
                background: #4a86e8;
                border: 1px solid #555555;
                width: 16px;
                height: 16px;
                margin: -5px 0;
                border-radius: 8px;
            }
            QSlider::handle:horizontal:hover {
                background: #5a96f8;
            }
        """)
        
        # 放大按鈕
        zoom_in_btn = QPushButton("+")
        zoom_in_btn.setFixedSize(30, 30)
        zoom_in_btn.setStyleSheet(zoom_out_btn.styleSheet())
        
        # 縮放值標籤
        self.zoom_value_label = QLabel("150")
        self.zoom_value_label.setFixedWidth(35)
        self.zoom_value_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.zoom_value_label.setStyleSheet("color: white; font-size: 12px; font-weight: bold;")
        
        # 連接信號
        zoom_out_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: self.adjust_zoom(-25))
        zoom_in_btn.clicked.connect(lambda: self.adjust_zoom(25))
        
        layout.addWidget(zoom_out_btn)
        layout.addWidget(self.zoom_slider)
        layout.addWidget(zoom_in_btn)
        layout.addWidget(self.zoom_value_label)
        layout.addStretch()  # 推到右邊
        
    def adjust_zoom(self, delta):
        current_value = self.zoom_slider.value()
        new_value = max(100, min(300, current_value + delta))
        self.zoom_slider.setValue(new_value)

class DropZoneFileList(QListWidget):
    """支援拖放功能的文件列表"""
    filesDropped = pyqtSignal(list)
    
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setAcceptDrops(True)
        self.setDragDropMode(QAbstractItemView.DropOnly)
        
        # 添加拖放提示
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QListWidget {
                background-color: #333333;
                border: 2px dashed #555555;
                color: white;
                border-radius: 8px;
                padding: 10px;
            }
            QListWidget::item {
                padding: 12px 8px;
                border-bottom: 1px solid #444444;
                border-radius: 4px;
                margin: 2px 0;
            }
            QListWidget::item:selected {
                background-color: #4a86e8;
                color: white;
                border: none;
            }
            QListWidget::item:hover {
                background-color: #505050;
                border: 1px solid #666666;
            }
        """)
        
        # 當列表為空時顯示提示文字
        self.update_empty_state()
        
    def update_empty_state(self):
        """更新空狀態顯示"""
        if self.count() == 0:
            self.setStyleSheet(self.styleSheet() + """
                QListWidget {
                    background-image: url();
                    background-position: center;
                    background-repeat: no-repeat;
                }
            """)
            # 可以在這裡添加佔位符提示
    
    def dragEnterEvent(self, event):
        if event.mimeData().hasUrls():
            # 檢查是否包含PDF文件
            pdf_files = [url.toLocalFile() for url in event.mimeData().urls() 
                        if url.toLocalFile().lower().endswith('.pdf')]
            if pdf_files:
                event.acceptProposedAction()
                self.setStyleSheet(self.styleSheet().replace(
                    "border: 2px dashed #555555", 
                    "border: 2px dashed #4a86e8"
                ))
        else:
            event.ignore()
    
    def dragMoveEvent(self, event):
        if event.mimeData().hasUrls():
            event.acceptProposedAction()
    
    def dragLeaveEvent(self, event):
        # 恢復原始邊框樣式
        self.setStyleSheet(self.styleSheet().replace(
            "border: 2px dashed #4a86e8", 
            "border: 2px dashed #555555"
        ))
    
    def dropEvent(self, event):
        # 恢復原始邊框樣式
        self.setStyleSheet(self.styleSheet().replace(
            "border: 2px dashed #4a86e8", 
            "border: 2px dashed #555555"
        ))
        
        if event.mimeData().hasUrls():
            pdf_files = []
            for url in event.mimeData().urls():
                file_path = url.toLocalFile()
                if file_path.lower().endswith('.pdf') and os.path.exists(file_path):
                    pdf_files.append(file_path)
            
            if pdf_files:
                self.filesDropped.emit(pdf_files)
                event.acceptProposedAction()
            else:
                event.ignore()

        toolbar.addAction(merge_action)

    def create_icon(self, symbol, color):
        """創建美化的圖示"""
        pixmap = QPixmap(36, 36)
        pixmap.fill(Qt.transparent)
        
        painter = QPainter(pixmap)
        painter.setRenderHint(QPainter.Antialiasing)
        
        # 創建漸層效果
        gradient = QLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, 36)
        base_color = QColor(color)
        gradient.setColorAt(0, base_color.lighter(120))
        gradient.setColorAt(1, base_color.darker(120))
        
        # 繪製背景圓形
        painter.setBrush(QBrush(gradient))
        painter.setPen(QPen(QColor(color).darker(150), 2))
        painter.drawEllipse(3, 3, 30, 30)
        
        # 繪製符號
        painter.setPen(QColor('white'))
        painter.setFont(QFont('Arial', 16, QFont.Bold))
        painter.drawText(pixmap.rect(), Qt.AlignCenter, symbol)
        
        painter.end()
        
        return QIcon(pixmap)

    def create_file_list(self, parent):
        """創建美化的文件列表面板"""
        file_widget = QWidget()
        file_widget.setStyleSheet("""
            QWidget {
                background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:0, y2:1,
                    stop:0 #404040, stop:1 #333333);
                border-right: 2px solid #555555;
            }
        """)
        file_layout = QVBoxLayout(file_widget)
        file_layout.setContentsMargins(12, 12, 12, 12)
        file_layout.setSpacing(10)
        
        # 文件區域標題
        title_widget = QWidget()
        title_layout = QHBoxLayout(title_widget)
        title_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        
        file_label = QLabel("📁 PDF Files")
        file_label.setStyleSheet("""
            font-weight: bold; 
            color: white; 
            padding: 8px;
            background-color: rgba(74, 134, 232, 100);
            border-radius: 6px;
        """)
        title_layout.addWidget(file_label)
        file_layout.addWidget(title_widget)
        
        # 文件拖放提示
        drop_hint = QLabel("💡 Drag PDF files here or use Open button")
        drop_hint.setStyleSheet("""
            color: #cccccc;
            padding: 6px;
            background-color: rgba(255, 255, 255, 20);
            border-radius: 4px;
            border: 1px dashed #666666;
        """)
        drop_hint.setWordWrap(True)
        file_layout.addWidget(drop_hint)
        
        # 使用支援拖放的文件列表
        self.file_list = DropZoneFileList()
        self.file_list.itemClicked.connect(self.file_selected)
        self.file_list.filesDropped.connect(self.handle_dropped_files)
        file_layout.addWidget(self.file_list, 1)  # 讓文件列表佔用剩餘空間
        
        # 文件操作按鈕
        file_buttons_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        file_buttons_layout.setSpacing(8)
        
        open_btn = QPushButton("📂 Open PDF")
        open_btn.clicked.connect(self.open_pdf)
        open_btn.setStyleSheet("""
            QPushButton {
                background-color: #4a86e8;
                color: white;
                border: none;
                padding: 8px 16px;
                border-radius: 4px;
                min-width: 100px;
                font-weight: bold;
            }
            QPushButton:hover {
                background-color: #3a76d8;
            }
            QPushButton:pressed {
                background-color: #2a66c8;
            }
            QPushButton:disabled {
                background-color: #555555;
                color: #999999;
            }
        """)
        file_buttons_layout.addWidget(open_btn)
        
        close_btn = QPushButton("❌ Close File")
        close_btn.clicked.connect(self.close_current_file)
        close_btn.setStyleSheet(open_btn.styleSheet())
        file_buttons_layout.addWidget(close_btn)
        
        file_layout.addLayout(file_buttons_layout)
        
        parent.addWidget(file_widget)

    def handle_dropped_files(self, file_paths):
        """處理拖放的文件"""
        for file_path in file_paths:
            try:
                self.load_pdf_file(file_path)
            except Exception as e:
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "Load Error", f"Failed to load {os.path.basename(file_path)}: {str(e)}")

    def create_page_view(self, parent):
        """創建美化的頁面預覽區域"""
        page_widget = QWidget()
        page_widget.setStyleSheet("""
            QWidget {
                background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:0, y2:1,
                    stop:0 #333333, stop:1 #2b2b2b);
            }
        """)
        
        page_layout = QVBoxLayout(page_widget)
        page_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        page_layout.setSpacing(0)
        
        # 頁面區域標題欄
        page_header = QWidget()
        page_header.setFixedHeight(50)
        page_header.setStyleSheet("""
            QWidget {
                background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:0, y2:1,
                    stop:0 #404040, stop:1 #333333);
                border-bottom: 2px solid #555555;
            }
        """)
        
        header_layout = QHBoxLayout(page_header)
        header_layout.setContentsMargins(20, 10, 20, 10)
        
        page_title = QLabel("📄 Page Preview")
        page_title.setStyleSheet("""
            font-weight: bold; 
            color: white; 
        """)
        header_layout.addWidget(page_title)
        header_layout.addStretch()
        
        # 添加進度條（初始隱藏）
        from PyQt5.QtWidgets import QProgressBar
        self.progress_bar = QProgressBar()
        self.progress_bar.setVisible(False)
        self.progress_bar.setStyleSheet("""
            QProgressBar {
                border: 2px solid #555555;
                border-radius: 5px;
                text-align: center;
                background-color: #333333;
                color: white;
            }
            QProgressBar::chunk {
                background-color: #4a86e8;
                border-radius: 3px;
            }
        """)
        header_layout.addWidget(self.progress_bar)
        
        page_layout.addWidget(page_header)
        
        self.scroll_area = QScrollArea()
        self.scroll_area.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self.scroll_area.setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarAsNeeded)
        self.scroll_area.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarAsNeeded)
        self.scroll_area.setAcceptDrops(True)
        
        self.page_container = QWidget()
        self.page_container.setAcceptDrops(True)
        
        self.scroll_area.setWidget(self.page_container)
        self.scroll_area.installEventFilter(self)
        
        self.scroll_area.verticalScrollBar().valueChanged.connect(self.on_scroll_changed)
        self.scroll_area.horizontalScrollBar().valueChanged.connect(self.on_scroll_changed)
        
        self.page_container.dragEnterEvent = self.container_drag_enter_event
        self.page_container.dragMoveEvent = self.container_drag_move_event
        self.page_container.dropEvent = self.container_drop_event
        self.page_container.dragLeaveEvent = self.container_drag_leave_event
        
        page_layout.addWidget(self.scroll_area)
        parent.addWidget(page_widget)

    def eventFilter(self, source, event):
        """事件過濾器 - 處理 Ctrl+滾輪縮放"""
        if (source == self.scroll_area and 
            event.type() == QEvent.Wheel and 
            event.modifiers() & Qt.ControlModifier):
            
            delta = event.angleDelta().y()
            if delta > 0:
                # 向上滾動 - 放大
                new_value = min(300, self.zoom_widget.zoom_slider.value() + 25)
            else:
                # 向下滾動 - 縮小
                new_value = max(100, self.zoom_widget.zoom_slider.value() - 25)
            
            self.zoom_widget.zoom_slider.setValue(new_value)
            return True
            
        return super().eventFilter(source, event)

    def on_zoom_changed(self, value):
        """縮放值改變時的處理 - 帶動畫效果"""
        old_size = self.thumbnail_size
        self.thumbnail_size = value
        self.zoom_widget.zoom_value_label.setText(str(value))
        
        if self.current_file_path and abs(old_size - value) > 5:  # 只有當變化足夠大時才更新
            # 使用定時器避免過於頻繁的更新
            if hasattr(self, 'zoom_update_timer'):
                self.zoom_update_timer.stop()
            else:
                self.zoom_update_timer = QTimer()
                self.zoom_update_timer.timeout.connect(self.apply_zoom_change)
                self.zoom_update_timer.setSingleShot(True)
            
            self.zoom_update_timer.start(50)  # 50ms延遲


    def apply_zoom_change(self):
        """應用縮放變化"""
        if self.current_file_path:
            # 保存當前滾動位置的比例
            v_scrollbar = self.scroll_area.verticalScrollBar()
            h_scrollbar = self.scroll_area.horizontalScrollBar()
            
            v_ratio = v_scrollbar.value() / max(v_scrollbar.maximum(), 1) if v_scrollbar.maximum() > 0 else 0
            h_ratio = h_scrollbar.value() / max(h_scrollbar.maximum(), 1) if h_scrollbar.maximum() > 0 else 0
            
            # 立即更新所有可見的組件大小
            self.update_visible_widgets_size()
            
            # 重新計算佈局
            self.auto_arrange_thumbnails()
            
            # 恢復滾動位置比例
            QTimer.singleShot(10, lambda: self.restore_scroll_position(v_ratio, h_ratio))

    def restore_scroll_position(self, v_ratio, h_ratio):
        """恢復滾動位置"""
        v_scrollbar = self.scroll_area.verticalScrollBar()
        h_scrollbar = self.scroll_area.horizontalScrollBar()
        
        if v_scrollbar.maximum() > 0:
            v_scrollbar.setValue(int(v_scrollbar.maximum() * v_ratio))
        if h_scrollbar.maximum() > 0:
            h_scrollbar.setValue(int(h_scrollbar.maximum() * h_ratio))

    def update_visible_widgets_size(self):
        """更新所有可見組件的大小"""
        for page_num, widget in self.visible_page_widgets.items():
            # 更新組件大小
            widget.update_thumbnail_size(self.thumbnail_size)
            
            # 重新計算位置
            row = page_num // self.columns
            col = page_num % self.columns
            x = col * (self.thumbnail_size + 40) + 20
            y = row * (self.thumbnail_size + 80) + 20
            widget.move(x, y)

    def auto_arrange_thumbnails(self):
        """自動排列縮略圖 - 優化版本"""
        if not self.current_file_path:
            return
        
        # 根據容器寬度計算最佳列數
        container_width = self.scroll_area.viewport().width() - 60
        widget_width = self.thumbnail_size + 40
        
        old_columns = self.columns
        self.columns = max(1, container_width // widget_width)
        
        # 重新計算容器大小
        self.update_container_size()
        
        # 如果列數改變了，需要重新排列所有可見組件
        if old_columns != self.columns:
            self.reposition_all_visible_widgets()
        else:
            # 只是大小改變，更新位置即可
            self.update_visible_widgets_positions()

    def reposition_all_visible_widgets(self):
        """重新排列所有可見組件的位置"""
        for page_num, widget in self.visible_page_widgets.items():
            row = page_num // self.columns
            col = page_num % self.columns
            x = col * (self.thumbnail_size + 40) + 20
            y = row * (self.thumbnail_size + 80) + 20
            widget.move(x, y)

    def update_visible_widgets_positions(self):
        """只更新可見組件的位置（不改變大小）"""
        for page_num, widget in self.visible_page_widgets.items():
            row = page_num // self.columns
            col = page_num % self.columns
            x = col * (self.thumbnail_size + 40) + 20
            y = row * (self.thumbnail_size + 80) + 20
            widget.move(x, y)

    def resizeEvent(self, event):
        """窗口大小改變事件"""
        super().resizeEvent(event)
        # 自動重新排列縮略圖
        QTimer.singleShot(100, self.auto_arrange_thumbnails)

    def on_scroll_changed(self):
        """滾動事件處理 - 延遲更新以避免頻繁刷新"""
        self.virtual_scroll_timer.stop()
        self.virtual_scroll_timer.start(50)  # 50ms延遲

    def update_visible_pages(self):
        """更新可見頁面範圍 - 核心虛擬化邏輯"""
        if not self.current_file_path:
            return
            
        # 計算可見範圍
        viewport_top = self.scroll_area.verticalScrollBar().value()
        viewport_height = self.scroll_area.viewport().height()
        
        row_height = self.thumbnail_size + 80
        start_row = max(0, viewport_top // row_height - 1)
        end_row = min(self.get_total_rows(), (viewport_top + viewport_height) // row_height + 2)
        
        start_page = start_row * self.columns
        end_page = min(self.get_total_pages(), end_row * self.columns)
        
        # 移除不可見的widgets
        self.cleanup_invisible_widgets(start_page, end_page)
        
        # 創建新的可見widgets
        self.create_visible_widgets(start_page, end_page)

    def cleanup_invisible_widgets(self, start_page, end_page):
        """清理不可見的widgets"""
        to_remove = []
        for page_num, widget in self.visible_page_widgets.items():
            if page_num < start_page or page_num >= end_page:
                widget.setParent(None)
                widget.deleteLater()
                to_remove.append(page_num)
        
        for page_num in to_remove:
            del self.visible_page_widgets[page_num]

    def create_visible_widgets(self, start_page, end_page):
        """創建可見範圍內的widgets"""
        for page_num in range(start_page, end_page):
            if (page_num >= self.get_total_pages() or 
                page_num in self.visible_page_widgets):
                continue
                
            pixmap = self.page_thumbnails.get((self.current_file_path, page_num))
            if pixmap:
                self.create_page_widget(page_num, pixmap)

    def create_page_widget(self, page_num, pixmap):

        row = page_num // self.columns
        col = page_num % self.columns
        
        page_widget = DraggablePageWidget(
            self.current_file_path, page_num, pixmap, self, self.thumbnail_size
        )
        
        # 手動設置位置
        x = col * (self.thumbnail_size + 40) + 20
        y = row * (self.thumbnail_size + 80) + 20
        page_widget.move(x, y)
        page_widget.setParent(self.page_container)
        page_widget.show()
        
        self.visible_page_widgets[page_num] = page_widget

    def get_total_pages(self):
        """獲取總頁數"""
        if self.current_file_path:
            return self.pdf_docs[self.current_file_path]['doc'].page_count
        return 0

    def get_total_rows(self):
        """獲取總行數"""
        total_pages = self.get_total_pages()
        return (total_pages + self.columns - 1) // self.columns if total_pages > 0 else 0

    def update_container_size(self):
        """更新容器大小"""
        if not self.current_file_path:
            return
            
        total_rows = self.get_total_rows()
        virtual_height = total_rows * (self.thumbnail_size + 80) + 40
        virtual_width = self.columns * (self.thumbnail_size + 40) + 40
        
        # 設置最小大小以確保滾動區域正確更新
        self.page_container.setMinimumSize(virtual_width, virtual_height)
        
        # 強制更新滾動區域
        self.scroll_area.updateGeometry()
        QApplication.processEvents()

    def generate_thumbnails(self, file_path, doc):
        """異步生成縮略圖"""
        total_pages = doc.page_count
        self.progress_bar.setVisible(True)
        self.progress_bar.setMaximum(total_pages)
        self.progress_bar.setValue(0)
        self.statusBar().showMessage(f"正在生成縮略圖... 0/{total_pages}")
        
        # 批量添加任務到後台線程
        for page_num in range(total_pages):
            self.thumbnail_generator.add_task(file_path, doc, page_num)

    def on_thumbnail_ready(self, file_path, page_num, pixmap):
        """縮略圖生成完成回調"""
        self.page_thumbnails[(file_path, page_num)] = pixmap
        
        # 更新進度
        if file_path in self.pdf_docs:
            doc = self.pdf_docs[file_path]['doc']
            current_count = len([k for k in self.page_thumbnails.keys() if k[0] == file_path])
            
            self.progress_bar.setValue(current_count)
            self.statusBar().showMessage(f"正在生成縮略圖... {current_count}/{doc.page_count}")
            
            # 完成時隱藏進度條
            if current_count >= doc.page_count:
                self.progress_bar.setVisible(False)
                self.statusBar().showMessage("Ready")
            
            # 如果是當前顯示的文件且在可見範圍內，立即更新顯示
            if (file_path == self.current_file_path and 
                page_num in self.visible_page_widgets):
                widget = self.visible_page_widgets[page_num]
                scaled_pixmap = pixmap.scaled(
                    self.thumbnail_size, self.thumbnail_size, 
                    Qt.KeepAspectRatio, Qt.SmoothTransformation
                )
                widget.setPixmap(scaled_pixmap)
            elif file_path == self.current_file_path:
                self.virtual_scroll_timer.start(10)

    def display_pages(self, file_path):
        """優化的頁面顯示 - 只顯示可見頁面"""
        self.clear_page_display()
        self.current_file_path = file_path
        
        doc_info = self.pdf_docs[file_path]
        
        # 設置虛擬容器大小
        self.update_container_size()
        
        # 只創建可見範圍的widgets
        self.update_visible_pages()
