        self.update_file_list_display()

    def clear_page_display(self):
        """清除當前頁面顯示"""
        for widget in self.visible_page_widgets.values():
            widget.setParent(None)
            widget.deleteLater()
        self.visible_page_widgets.clear()
        self.page_widgets.clear()

    def refresh_current_display(self):
        """優化的刷新顯示"""
        if self.current_file_path:
            # 清理現有的可見widgets
            self.clear_page_display()
            
            # 重新生成縮略圖
            doc_info = self.pdf_docs[self.current_file_path]
            
            # 清除舊的縮略圖
            keys_to_remove = [k for k in self.page_thumbnails.keys() if k[0] == self.current_file_path]
            for key in keys_to_remove:
                del self.page_thumbnails[key]
            
            # 重新生成
            self.generate_thumbnails(self.current_file_path, doc_info['doc'])
            
            # 重新顯示
            self.display_pages(self.current_file_path)
            self.update_file_list_display()

    def container_drag_enter_event(self, event):
        """容器拖放進入事件"""
        if event.mimeData().hasText():
            drag_data = event.mimeData().text()
            if "|||" in drag_data:
                event.acceptProposedAction()
                self.update_drop_indicators(event.pos())

    def container_drag_move_event(self, event):
        """容器拖放移動事件"""
        if event.mimeData().hasText():
            drag_data = event.mimeData().text()
            if "|||" in drag_data:
                self.update_drop_indicators(event.pos())
                event.acceptProposedAction()

    def container_drop_event(self, event):
        """優化的容器拖放放下事件"""
        try:
            if event.mimeData().hasText():
                drag_data = event.mimeData().text()
                
                if "|||" in drag_data:
                    parts = drag_data.rsplit("|||", 1)
                    if len(parts) == 2:
                        source_file = parts[0]
                        source_page_str = parts[1]
                        
                        try:
                            source_page = int(source_page_str)
                            insert_position = self.calculate_drop_position(event.pos())
                            
                            if source_file == self.current_file_path and not self.is_reordering:
                                # 立即給用戶反饋
                                self.statusBar().showMessage("正在準備重排序操作...")
                                
                                # 很短的延遲讓用戶看到反饋
                                QTimer.singleShot(10, lambda: self.reorder_pages(source_page, insert_position))
                                event.acceptProposedAction()
                            elif self.is_reordering:
                                self.statusBar().showMessage("正在處理中，請稍候...")
                                
                        except ValueError as e:
                            print(f"Error parsing page number: {source_page_str}, Error: {e}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error in container_drop_event: {e}")
        finally:
            self.hide_drop_indicators()

    def container_drag_leave_event(self, event):
        """容器拖放離開事件"""
        self.hide_drop_indicators()

    def calculate_drop_position(self, pos):
        """計算拖放插入位置"""
        if not self.current_file_path:
            return 0
        
        total_pages = self.get_total_pages()
        if total_pages == 0:
            return 0
        
        row = pos.y() // (self.thumbnail_size + 80)
        col = pos.x() // (self.thumbnail_size + 40)
        
        insert_pos = row * self.columns + col
        return min(insert_pos, total_pages)

    def update_drop_indicators(self, pos):
        """更新拖放指示器位置"""
        self.hide_drop_indicators()
        
        if not self.visible_page_widgets:
            return
        
        insert_pos = self.calculate_drop_position(pos)
        
        indicator = DropInsertIndicator(self.page_container)
        
        if insert_pos >= self.get_total_pages():
            # 放在最後
            if self.visible_page_widgets:
                last_visible_page = max(self.visible_page_widgets.keys())
                if last_visible_page in self.visible_page_widgets:
                    last_widget = self.visible_page_widgets[last_visible_page]
                    x = last_widget.x() + last_widget.width() + 5
                    y = last_widget.y()
                else:
                    x = 10
                    y = 10
            else:
                x = 10
                y = 10
        else:
            # 找到對應位置
            if insert_pos in self.visible_page_widgets:
                target_widget = self.visible_page_widgets[insert_pos]
                x = target_widget.x() - 10
                y = target_widget.y()
            else:
                # 計算虛擬位置
                row = insert_pos // self.columns
                col = insert_pos % self.columns
                x = col * (self.thumbnail_size + 40) + 10
                y = row * (self.thumbnail_size + 80) + 20
        
        indicator.move(x, y)
        indicator.show()
        self.drop_indicators = [indicator]

    def hide_drop_indicators(self):
        """隱藏所有拖放指示器"""
        for indicator in self.drop_indicators:
            indicator.hide()
            indicator.deleteLater()
        self.drop_indicators.clear()

    def open_pdf(self):
        file_path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self, "Open PDF", "", "PDF files (*.pdf)"
        )
        if file_path:
            self.load_pdf_file(file_path)

    def load_pdf_file(self, file_path):
        try:
            temp_dir = tempfile.mkdtemp()
            temp_file_path = os.path.join(temp_dir, os.path.basename(file_path))
            shutil.copy2(file_path, temp_file_path)
            
            doc = fitz.open(temp_file_path)
            
            self.pdf_docs[temp_file_path] = {
                'doc': doc,
                'original_path': file_path,
                'temp_path': temp_file_path,
                'modified': False
            }
            
            file_name = os.path.basename(file_path)
            list_item = f"📄 {file_name} ({doc.page_count} pages)"
            self.file_list.addItem(list_item)
            
            item = self.file_list.item(self.file_list.count() - 1)
            item.setData(Qt.UserRole, temp_file_path)
            
            self.generate_thumbnails(temp_file_path, doc)
            
            if len(self.pdf_docs) == 1:
                self.file_list.setCurrentRow(0)
                self.display_pages(temp_file_path)
                
            self.statusBar().showMessage(f"✅ Loaded: {file_name}")
            
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Failed to load PDF: {str(e)}")

    def file_selected(self, item):
        """文件被選中時的處理"""
        file_path = item.data(Qt.UserRole)
        if file_path in self.pdf_docs:
            self.display_pages(file_path)

    def reorder_pages(self, source_page, target_position):
        """重新排列頁面順序 - 簡化同步版本"""
        if not self.current_file_path or self.is_reordering:
            return
            
        self.is_reordering = True
        
        try:
            doc_info = self.pdf_docs[self.current_file_path]
            doc = doc_info['doc']
            
            target_position = max(0, min(target_position, doc.page_count - 1))
            
            if source_page == target_position:
                self.is_reordering = False
                return
            
            # 顯示進度
            self.progress_bar.setVisible(True)
            self.progress_bar.setRange(0, 0)  # 不確定進度
            self.statusBar().showMessage("正在重新排列頁面...")
            
            # 強制更新UI
            QApplication.processEvents()
            
            # 先重新排列縮略圖索引
            self.reorder_thumbnails(source_page, target_position)
            
            # 在後台執行文檔重排序
            QTimer.singleShot(100, lambda: self.perform_document_reorder(source_page, target_position))
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.reorder_error(f"重排序初始化失敗: {str(e)}")

    def perform_document_reorder(self, source_page, target_position):
        """執行實際的文檔重排序"""
        try:
            doc_info = self.pdf_docs[self.current_file_path]
            doc = doc_info['doc']
            total_pages = doc.page_count
            
            # 計算新的頁面順序
            page_order = list(range(total_pages))
            moved_page = page_order.pop(source_page)
            page_order.insert(target_position, moved_page)
            
            # 創建新文檔
            new_doc = fitz.open()
            
            # 批量插入頁面
            for i, page_num in enumerate(page_order):
                new_doc.insert_pdf(doc, from_page=page_num, to_page=page_num)
                
                # 每處理10頁處理一次事件
                if i % 10 == 0:
                    QApplication.processEvents()
            
            # 關閉舊文檔
            doc.close()
            
            # 保存新文檔
            new_doc.save(self.current_file_path)
            new_doc.close()
            
            # 重新打開文檔
            doc_info['doc'] = fitz.open(self.current_file_path)
            doc_info['modified'] = True
            self.modified_files.add(self.current_file_path)
            
            # 完成
            self.reorder_completed(source_page, target_position)
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.reorder_error(f"文檔重排序失敗: {str(e)}")

    def reorder_completed(self, source_page, target_position):
        """重排序完成處理"""
        try:
            # 隱藏進度條
            self.progress_bar.setVisible(False)
            
            # 強制刷新顯示
            self.force_refresh_display()
            
            self.save_action.setEnabled(True)
            self.statusBar().showMessage(f"✅ 頁面 {source_page + 1} 已移動到位置 {target_position + 1}")
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.reorder_error(f"完成重排序時發生錯誤: {str(e)}")
        finally:
            self.is_reordering = False

    def reorder_error(self, error_message):
        """重排序錯誤處理"""
        self.progress_bar.setVisible(False)
        self.statusBar().showMessage("重排序操作失敗")
        print(f"重排序錯誤: {error_message}")  # 輸出到控制台以便調試
        self.is_reordering = False

    def force_refresh_display(self):
        """強制刷新顯示"""
        if not self.current_file_path:
            return
        
        try:
            # 清除當前可見的widgets
            self.clear_page_display()
            
            # 重新計算容器大小
            self.update_container_size()
            
            # 立即更新可見頁面
            self.update_visible_pages()
            
            # 強制重繪
            self.page_container.update()
            self.scroll_area.update()
            
            # 更新文件列表顯示
            self.update_file_list_display()
            
            # 確保UI更新
            QApplication.processEvents()
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"刷新顯示時發生錯誤: {str(e)}")

    def reorder_thumbnails(self, source_page, target_position):
        """重新排列縮略圖索引而不是重新生成 - 增強錯誤處理"""
        if not self.current_file_path:
            return
        
        try:
            # 獲取當前文件的所有縮略圖
            current_thumbnails = {}
            keys_to_remove = []
            
            for (file_path, page_num), pixmap in self.page_thumbnails.items():
                if file_path == self.current_file_path:
                    current_thumbnails[page_num] = pixmap
                    keys_to_remove.append((file_path, page_num))
            
            if not current_thumbnails:
                print("警告: 沒有找到當前文件的縮略圖")
                return
            
            # 批量移除舊的縮略圖索引
            for key in keys_to_remove:
                del self.page_thumbnails[key]
            
            # 重新計算頁面順序
            total_pages = len(current_thumbnails)
            if total_pages == 0:
                return
                
            page_order = list(range(total_pages))
            
            # 驗證索引範圍
            if not (0 <= source_page < len(page_order) and 0 <= target_position < len(page_order)):
                print(f"警告: 頁面索引超出範圍 source:{source_page}, target:{target_position}, total:{total_pages}")
                return
            
            moved_page = page_order.pop(source_page)
            page_order.insert(target_position, moved_page)
            
            # 批量重新分配縮略圖
            new_thumbnails = {}
            for new_index, old_index in enumerate(page_order):
                if old_index in current_thumbnails:
                    new_thumbnails[(self.current_file_path, new_index)] = current_thumbnails[old_index]
            
            # 批量更新
            self.page_thumbnails.update(new_thumbnails)
            
            print(f"成功重排序縮略圖: {source_page} -> {target_position}")
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"重排序縮略圖時發生錯誤: {str(e)}")


    def page_clicked(self, widget, event):
        """頁面被點擊時的處理"""
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            if event.modifiers() & Qt.ControlModifier:
                self.toggle_page_selection(widget)
            elif event.modifiers() & Qt.ShiftModifier:
                self.range_select_pages(widget)
            else:
                self.select_single_page(widget)

    def page_double_clicked(self, widget, event):
        """頁面被雙擊時的處理"""
        self.preview_page(widget.file_path, widget.page_num)

    def toggle_page_selection(self, widget):
        """切換頁面選擇狀態"""
        widget.selected = not widget.selected
        widget.update_style()
        
        page_info = (widget.file_path, widget.page_num)
        if widget.selected:
            if page_info not in self.selected_pages:
                self.selected_pages.append(page_info)
        else:
            if page_info in self.selected_pages:
                self.selected_pages.remove(page_info)

    def select_single_page(self, widget):
        """選中單個頁面"""
        self.clear_all_selections()
        
        widget.selected = True
        self.selected_pages = [(widget.file_path, widget.page_num)]
        widget.update_style()

    def range_select_pages(self, widget):
        """範圍選擇頁面"""
        if not self.selected_pages:
            self.select_single_page(widget)
            return
            
        last_selected = self.selected_pages[-1]
        current_page = (widget.file_path, widget.page_num)
        
        if last_selected[0] == current_page[0]:
            start_page = min(last_selected[1], current_page[1])
            end_page = max(last_selected[1], current_page[1])
            
            for page_num in range(start_page, end_page + 1):
                page_info = (widget.file_path, page_num)
                if page_info not in self.selected_pages:
                    self.selected_pages.append(page_info)
                    
            self.update_all_page_styles()

    def clear_all_selections(self):
        """清除所有選擇"""
        self.selected_pages.clear()
        for widget in self.visible_page_widgets.values():
            widget.selected = False
            widget.update_style()

    def update_all_page_styles(self):
        """更新所有頁面的選擇樣式"""
        for widget in self.visible_page_widgets.values():
            page_info = (widget.file_path, widget.page_num)
            widget.selected = page_info in self.selected_pages
            widget.update_style()

    def show_context_menu(self, widget, pos):
        """顯示右鍵菜單"""
        menu = QMenu(self)
        menu.setStyleSheet("""
            QMenu {
                background-color: #272727;
                color: white;
                border: 1px solid #666666;
                border-radius: 10px;
                padding: 4px;
            }
            QMenu::item {
                padding: 8px 25px;
                margin: 2px 4px;
                border-radius: 5px;
            }
            QMenu::item:selected {
                background-color: #02F78E;
                color: black;
            }
            QMenu::separator {
                height: 1px;
                background-color: #666666;
                margin-left: 10px;
                margin-right: 10px;
            }
        """)
        
        rotate_left_action = menu.addAction("Rotate Left")
        rotate_right_action = menu.addAction("Rotate Right")
        menu.addSeparator()
        delete_action = menu.addAction("Delete")
        extract_action = menu.addAction("Extract")
        menu.addSeparator()
        preview_action = menu.addAction("Preview")
        
        action = menu.exec_(pos)
        
        if action == rotate_left_action:
            self.rotate_selected_pages(-90)
        elif action == rotate_right_action:
            self.rotate_selected_pages(90)
        elif action == delete_action:
            self.delete_selected_pages()
        elif action == extract_action:
            self.extract_selected_pages()
        elif action == preview_action:
            self.preview_page(widget.file_path, widget.page_num)

    def rotate_selected_pages(self, angle):
        """旋轉選中的頁面 - 優化版本，只重新生成改變的縮略圖"""
        if not self.selected_pages:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Warning", "No pages selected.")
            return
        
        # 創建進度對話框
        progress_dialog = QProgressDialog("Rotating pages...", "Cancel", 0, len(self.selected_pages), self)
        progress_dialog.setWindowModality(Qt.WindowModal)
        progress_dialog.setMinimumDuration(0)
        progress_dialog.setValue(0)
        progress_dialog.setWindowTitle("Rotation Progress")
        
        rotated_pages = []  # 記錄實際被旋轉的頁面
        
        try:
            for i, (file_path, page_num) in enumerate(self.selected_pages):
                if progress_dialog.wasCanceled():
                    break
                    
                progress_dialog.setValue(i)
                progress_dialog.setLabelText(f"Rotating page {page_num + 1}...")
                QApplication.processEvents()
                
                doc_info = self.pdf_docs[file_path]
                doc = doc_info['doc']
                page = doc.load_page(page_num)
                
                # 檢查是否真的需要旋轉（有時候頁面已經是目標角度）
                current_rotation = page.rotation
                new_rotation = (current_rotation + angle) % 360
                
                if current_rotation != new_rotation:
                    page.set_rotation(new_rotation)
                    doc_info['modified'] = True
                    self.modified_files.add(file_path)
                    rotated_pages.append((file_path, page_num, page))
            
            progress_dialog.close()
            
            if not rotated_pages:
                self.statusBar().showMessage("No pages needed rotation")
                return
            
            # 只重新生成被旋轉頁面的縮略圖
            self.regenerate_specific_thumbnails(rotated_pages)
            
            self.save_action.setEnabled(True)
            self.statusBar().showMessage(f"🔄 Rotated {len(rotated_pages)} pages by {angle} degrees")
            
        except Exception as e:
            progress_dialog.close()
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Failed to rotate pages: {str(e)}")

    def regenerate_specific_thumbnails(self, changed_pages):
        """重新生成特定頁面的縮略圖"""
        if not changed_pages:
            return
        
        # 創建進度對話框
        progress_dialog = QProgressDialog("Updating thumbnails...", "Cancel", 0, len(changed_pages), self)
        progress_dialog.setWindowModality(Qt.WindowModal)
        progress_dialog.setMinimumDuration(0)
        progress_dialog.setValue(0)
        progress_dialog.setWindowTitle("Thumbnail Update")
        
        updated_widgets = []  # 記錄需要更新的可見組件
        
        try:
            for i, (file_path, page_num, page) in enumerate(changed_pages):
                if progress_dialog.wasCanceled():
                    break
                    
                progress_dialog.setValue(i)
                progress_dialog.setLabelText(f"Updating thumbnail for page {page_num + 1}...")
                QApplication.processEvents()
                
                # 生成新的縮略圖
                matrix = fitz.Matrix(0.3, 0.3)  # 與原來的縮略圖生成器相同的矩陣
                pixmap = page.get_pixmap(matrix=matrix)
                
                img = QImage(pixmap.samples, pixmap.width, pixmap.height, 
                            pixmap.stride, QImage.Format_RGB888)
                qpixmap = QPixmap.fromImage(img)
                
                # 更新縮略圖緩存
                self.page_thumbnails[(file_path, page_num)] = qpixmap
                
                # 如果這個頁面當前可見，記錄需要更新的組件
                if (file_path == self.current_file_path and 
                    page_num in self.visible_page_widgets):
                    updated_widgets.append((page_num, qpixmap))
            
            progress_dialog.close()
            
            # 更新可見的組件
            self.update_visible_thumbnails(updated_widgets)
            
        except Exception as e:
            progress_dialog.close()
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Failed to update thumbnails: {str(e)}")

    def update_visible_thumbnails(self, updated_widgets):
        """更新可見的縮略圖組件"""
        for page_num, qpixmap in updated_widgets:
            if page_num in self.visible_page_widgets:
                widget = self.visible_page_widgets[page_num]
                
                # 更新原始縮略圖
                widget.original_pixmap = qpixmap
                
                # 按當前縮放大小顯示
                scaled_pixmap = qpixmap.scaled(
                    self.thumbnail_size, self.thumbnail_size, 
                    Qt.KeepAspectRatio, Qt.SmoothTransformation
                )
                widget.setPixmap(scaled_pixmap)
                
                # 強制重繪
                widget.update()

    def delete_selected_pages(self):
        """刪除選中的頁面 - 優化版本，只更新必要的縮略圖"""
        if not self.selected_pages:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Warning", "No pages selected.")
            return
            
        reply = QMessageBox.question(
            self, "Confirm Delete", 
            f"Are you sure you want to delete {len(self.selected_pages)} selected pages?",
            QMessageBox.Yes | QMessageBox.No
        )
        
        if reply != QMessageBox.Yes:
            return
        
        # 創建進度對話框
        progress_dialog = QProgressDialog("Deleting pages...", "Cancel", 0, len(self.selected_pages), self)
        progress_dialog.setWindowModality(Qt.WindowModal)
        progress_dialog.setMinimumDuration(0)
        progress_dialog.setValue(0)
        progress_dialog.setWindowTitle("Delete Progress")
        
        try:
            # 按文件分組並按頁面號倒序排列（避免刪除時索引混亂）
            pages_by_file = {}
            for file_path, page_num in self.selected_pages:
                if file_path not in pages_by_file:
                    pages_by_file[file_path] = []
                pages_by_file[file_path].append(page_num)
            
            # 對每個文件的頁面按倒序排列
            for file_path in pages_by_file:
                pages_by_file[file_path].sort(reverse=True)
            
            deleted_count = 0
            affected_files = set()
            
            # 處理每個文件
            for file_path, page_numbers in pages_by_file.items():
                if progress_dialog.wasCanceled():
                    break
                
                doc_info = self.pdf_docs[file_path]
                doc = doc_info['doc']
                
                # 記錄刪除前的狀態
                original_page_count = doc.page_count
                
                # 逐個刪除頁面（倒序）
                for page_num in page_numbers:
                    if progress_dialog.wasCanceled():
                        break
                        
                    progress_dialog.setValue(deleted_count)
                    progress_dialog.setLabelText(f"Deleting page {page_num + 1} from {os.path.basename(doc_info.get('original_path', file_path))}...")
                    QApplication.processEvents()
                    
                    # 刪除頁面
                    doc.delete_page(page_num)
                    deleted_count += 1
                    
                    # 立即更新縮略圖索引（針對這個特定的刪除）
                    self.update_thumbnails_after_single_deletion(file_path, page_num)
                
                doc_info['modified'] = True
                self.modified_files.add(file_path)
                affected_files.add(file_path)
            
            progress_dialog.close()
            
            # 清除選擇
            self.selected_pages.clear()
            
            # 只刷新受影響的文件顯示
            if self.current_file_path in affected_files:
                self.refresh_current_display_after_deletion()
            
            self.save_action.setEnabled(True)
            self.statusBar().showMessage(f"🗑️ Deleted {deleted_count} pages")
            
        except Exception as e:
            progress_dialog.close()
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Failed to delete pages: {str(e)}")

    def update_thumbnails_after_single_deletion(self, file_path, deleted_page):
        """單個頁面刪除後立即更新縮略圖索引 - 優化版本"""
        # 收集需要更新的縮略圖
        thumbnails_to_update = {}
        thumbnails_to_remove = []
        
        for (fp, page_num), pixmap in list(self.page_thumbnails.items()):
            if fp == file_path:
                if page_num == deleted_page:
                    # 標記要刪除的縮略圖
                    thumbnails_to_remove.append((fp, page_num))
                elif page_num > deleted_page:
                    # 標記要重新索引的縮略圖（頁面號減1）
                    thumbnails_to_remove.append((fp, page_num))
                    thumbnails_to_update[(fp, page_num - 1)] = pixmap
        
        # 批量刪除舊索引
        for key in thumbnails_to_remove:
            if key in self.page_thumbnails:
                del self.page_thumbnails[key]
        
        # 批量添加新索引
        self.page_thumbnails.update(thumbnails_to_update)

    def refresh_current_display_after_deletion(self):
        """刪除後的優化刷新 - 重用現有縮略圖"""
        if not self.current_file_path:
            return
        
        try:
            # 記錄當前滾動位置
            v_scrollbar = self.scroll_area.verticalScrollBar()
            h_scrollbar = self.scroll_area.horizontalScrollBar()
            
            v_ratio = v_scrollbar.value() / max(v_scrollbar.maximum(), 1) if v_scrollbar.maximum() > 0 else 0
            h_ratio = h_scrollbar.value() / max(h_scrollbar.maximum(), 1) if h_scrollbar.maximum() > 0 else 0
            
            # 清除當前可見的widgets
            self.clear_page_display()
            
            # 重新計算容器大小
            self.update_container_size()
            
            # 立即更新可見頁面（會重用現有縮略圖）
            self.update_visible_pages()
            
            # 更新文件列表顯示
            self.update_file_list_display()
            
            # 恢復滾動位置
            QTimer.singleShot(50, lambda: self.restore_scroll_position(v_ratio, h_ratio))
            
            # 確保UI更新
            QApplication.processEvents()
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"刷新顯示時發生錯誤: {str(e)}")

    def batch_delete_pages(self, file_path, page_numbers):
        """批量刪除頁面的優化方法"""
        if not page_numbers:
            return
        
        try:
            doc_info = self.pdf_docs[file_path]
            doc = doc_info['doc']
            
            # 按倒序排列避免索引混亂
            sorted_pages = sorted(page_numbers, reverse=True)
            
            # 創建進度對話框
            progress_dialog = QProgressDialog("Batch deleting pages...", "Cancel", 0, len(sorted_pages), self)
            progress_dialog.setWindowModality(Qt.WindowModal)
            progress_dialog.setMinimumDuration(0)
            progress_dialog.setValue(0)
            
            for i, page_num in enumerate(sorted_pages):
                if progress_dialog.wasCanceled():
                    break
                    
                progress_dialog.setValue(i)
                progress_dialog.setLabelText(f"Deleting page {page_num + 1}...")
                QApplication.processEvents()
                
                # 刪除頁面
                doc.delete_page(page_num)
                
                # 更新縮略圖索引
                self.update_thumbnails_after_single_deletion(file_path, page_num)
            
            progress_dialog.close()
            
            doc_info['modified'] = True
            self.modified_files.add(file_path)
            
            return len(sorted_pages)
            
        except Exception as e:
            if 'progress_dialog' in locals():
                progress_dialog.close()
            raise e

    def smart_thumbnail_cleanup(self, file_path):
        """智能縮略圖清理 - 移除不再存在的頁面的縮略圖"""
        if file_path not in self.pdf_docs:
            return
        
        doc = self.pdf_docs[file_path]['doc']
        current_page_count = doc.page_count
        
        # 找出超出當前頁面範圍的縮略圖
        keys_to_remove = []
        for (fp, page_num) in self.page_thumbnails.keys():
            if fp == file_path and page_num >= current_page_count:
                keys_to_remove.append((fp, page_num))
        
        # 批量刪除
        for key in keys_to_remove:
            del self.page_thumbnails[key]
        
        print(f"清理了 {len(keys_to_remove)} 個無效縮略圖")

    def update_visible_widgets_after_deletion(self):
        """刪除後更新可見組件的頁面編號標籤"""
        for page_num, widget in self.visible_page_widgets.items():
            # 更新頁面編號標籤
            widget.page_num = page_num
            widget.create_page_label()

    def get_deletion_impact_analysis(self, selected_pages):
        """分析刪除操作的影響範圍"""
        impact = {
            'total_pages_to_delete': len(selected_pages),
            'affected_files': set(),
            'pages_by_file': {},
            'thumbnail_updates_needed': 0
        }
        
        for file_path, page_num in selected_pages:
            impact['affected_files'].add(file_path)
            
            if file_path not in impact['pages_by_file']:
                impact['pages_by_file'][file_path] = []
            impact['pages_by_file'][file_path].append(page_num)
        
        # 計算需要更新的縮略圖數量
        for file_path, page_numbers in impact['pages_by_file'].items():
            if file_path in self.pdf_docs:
                doc_page_count = self.pdf_docs[file_path]['doc'].page_count
                max_deleted_page = max(page_numbers)
                # 所有在最大刪除頁面之後的頁面都需要重新索引
                impact['thumbnail_updates_needed'] += (doc_page_count - max_deleted_page - 1)
        
        return impact

    def extract_selected_pages(self):
        """提取選中的頁面"""
        if not self.selected_pages:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Warning", "No pages selected.")
            return
            
        save_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(
            self, "Save Extracted Pages", "", "PDF files (*.pdf)"
        )
        
        if save_path:
            new_doc = fitz.open()
            
            for file_path, page_num in sorted(self.selected_pages):
                source_doc = self.pdf_docs[file_path]['doc']
                new_doc.insert_pdf(source_doc, from_page=page_num, to_page=page_num)
            
            new_doc.save(save_path)
            new_doc.close()
            
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Success", f"✅ Extracted {len(self.selected_pages)} pages to {save_path}")

    def merge_pdfs(self):
        """合併PDFs - 優化版本，控制文件大小"""
        if len(self.pdf_docs) < 2:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Warning", "Need at least 2 PDF files to merge.")
            return
            
        save_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(
            self, "Save Merged PDF", "", "PDF files (*.pdf)"
        )
        
        if save_path:
            try:
                # 創建進度對話框
                progress_dialog = QProgressDialog("Merging PDF files...", "Cancel", 0, len(self.pdf_docs), self)
                progress_dialog.setWindowModality(Qt.WindowModal)
                progress_dialog.setMinimumDuration(0)
                progress_dialog.setValue(0)
                progress_dialog.setWindowTitle("Merge Progress")
                
                merged_doc = fitz.open()
                
                # 按文件列表順序合併
                for i, (file_path, doc_info) in enumerate(self.pdf_docs.items()):
                    if progress_dialog.wasCanceled():
                        merged_doc.close()
                        return
                    
                    progress_dialog.setValue(i)
                    progress_dialog.setLabelText(f"Adding: {os.path.basename(doc_info['original_path'])}")
                    QApplication.processEvents()
                    
                    # 插入PDF，但不使用deflate，這樣可以避免重複壓縮
                    merged_doc.insert_pdf(doc_info['doc'])
                
                progress_dialog.setLabelText("Optimizing merged PDF...")
                QApplication.processEvents()
                
                # 使用優化參數保存，控制文件大小
                merged_doc.save(save_path, 
                            garbage=4,           # 最高級別的垃圾收集
                            clean=True,          # 清理冗餘對象
                            deflate=True,        # 壓縮
                            deflate_images=True, # 壓縮圖像
                            deflate_fonts=True,  # 壓縮字體
                            ascii=False,         # 不強制ASCII編碼
                            expand=0,            # 不展開
                            linear=False,        # 不線性化
                            pretty=False)        # 不美化格式
                
                merged_doc.close()
                progress_dialog.close()
                
                # 顯示文件大小信息
                file_size = os.path.getsize(save_path)
                size_mb = file_size / (1024 * 1024)
                
                QMessageBox.information(self, "Success", 
                                    f"🔗 Merged PDFs saved to:\n{save_path}\n\nFile size: {size_mb:.2f} MB")
                self.statusBar().showMessage(f"🔗 Merged PDF saved ({size_mb:.2f} MB)")
                
            except Exception as e:
                QMessageBox.critical(self, "Merge Error", f"Failed to merge PDFs:\n{str(e)}")

    def save_current_pdf(self):
        """Saves the current PDF and emits a signal."""
        if not self.current_file_path:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Warning", "No PDF file selected.")
            return
            
        try:
            doc_info = self.pdf_docs[self.current_file_path]
            original_path = doc_info['original_path']
            
            doc_info['doc'].save(original_path)
            doc_info['modified'] = False
            
            if self.current_file_path in self.modified_files:
                self.modified_files.remove(self.current_file_path)
            
            self.update_file_list_display()
            
            if not self.modified_files:
                self.save_action.setEnabled(False)
            
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Success", f"💾 Saved: {os.path.basename(original_path)}")
            self.statusBar().showMessage(f"💾 Saved: {os.path.basename(original_path)}")
            
            # Emit the signal with the path of the saved file
            self.file_saved.emit(original_path)
            
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Failed to save: {str(e)}")

    def save_as_pdf(self):
        """另存為PDF"""
        if not self.current_file_path:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Warning", "No PDF file selected.")
            return
            
        save_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(
            self, "Save PDF As", "", "PDF files (*.pdf)"
        )
        
        if save_path:
            try:
                doc_info = self.pdf_docs[self.current_file_path]
                doc_info['doc'].save(save_path)
                QMessageBox.information(self, "Success", f"💾 Saved as: {save_path}")
                self.statusBar().showMessage(f"💾 Saved as: {save_path}")
            except Exception as e:
                QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Failed to save: {str(e)}")

    def save_all_pdfs(self):
        """Saves all modified PDFs and emits signals for each."""
        if not self.modified_files:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Save All", "No files need to be saved.")
            return
            
        saved_count = 0
        saved_file_paths = []
        for file_path in list(self.modified_files):
            try:
                doc_info = self.pdf_docs[file_path]
                original_path = doc_info['original_path']
                
                doc_info['doc'].save(original_path)
                doc_info['modified'] = False
                saved_count += 1
                saved_file_paths.append(original_path)
                
            except Exception as e:
                QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error", f"Failed to save {os.path.basename(original_path)}: {str(e)}")
        
        self.modified_files.clear()
        self.save_action.setEnabled(False)
        self.update_file_list_display()
        
        QMessageBox.information(self, "Success", f"💾 Saved {saved_count} files.")
        self.statusBar().showMessage(f"💾 Saved {saved_count} files.")

        # Emit a signal for each file that was saved
        for path in saved_file_paths:
            self.file_saved.emit(path)

    def close_current_file(self):
        """關閉當前文件"""
        if not self.current_file_path:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Warning", "No file selected.")
            return
            
        doc_info = self.pdf_docs[self.current_file_path]
        
        if doc_info['modified']:
            reply = QMessageBox.question(
                self, "Unsaved Changes", 
                f"File {os.path.basename(doc_info['original_path'])} has unsaved changes. Save before closing?",
                QMessageBox.Yes | QMessageBox.No | QMessageBox.Cancel
            )
            
            if reply == QMessageBox.Yes:
                self.save_current_pdf()
            elif reply == QMessageBox.Cancel:
                return
        
        doc_info['doc'].close()
        
        try:
            temp_dir = os.path.dirname(doc_info['temp_path'])
            shutil.rmtree(temp_dir)
        except:
            pass
        
        del self.pdf_docs[self.current_file_path]
        if self.current_file_path in self.modified_files:
            self.modified_files.remove(self.current_file_path)
        
        for i in range(self.file_list.count()):
            item = self.file_list.item(i)
            if item.data(Qt.UserRole) == self.current_file_path:
                self.file_list.takeItem(i)
                break
        
        self.clear_page_display()
        self.current_file_path = None
        
        if not self.modified_files:
            self.save_action.setEnabled(False)

    def update_file_list_display(self):
        """更新文件列表顯示"""
        for i in range(self.file_list.count()):
            item = self.file_list.item(i)
            file_path = item.data(Qt.UserRole)
            
            if file_path in self.pdf_docs:
                doc_info = self.pdf_docs[file_path]
                file_name = os.path.basename(doc_info['original_path'])
                page_count = doc_info['doc'].page_count
                
                if doc_info['modified']:
                    item.setText(f"📄 *{file_name} ({page_count} pages)")
                    item.setForeground(QColor(255, 200, 100))
                else:
                    item.setText(f"📄 {file_name} ({page_count} pages)")
                    item.setForeground(QColor(255, 255, 255))

    def preview_page(self, file_path, page_num):
        """預覽頁面"""
        preview_dialog = QDialog(self)
        preview_dialog.setWindowTitle(f"📄 Preview - Page {page_num + 1}")
        preview_dialog.setModal(True)
        preview_dialog.resize(1000, 1300)
        
        layout = QVBoxLayout(preview_dialog)
        
        doc = self.pdf_docs[file_path]['doc']
        page = doc.load_page(page_num)
        
        matrix = fitz.Matrix(4.0, 4.0)
        pixmap = page.get_pixmap(matrix=matrix)
        
        img = QImage(pixmap.samples, pixmap.width, pixmap.height, 
                    pixmap.stride, QImage.Format_RGB888)
        qpixmap = QPixmap.fromImage(img)
        
        label = QLabel()
        label.setPixmap(qpixmap.scaled(880, 1200, Qt.KeepAspectRatio, Qt.SmoothTransformation))
        label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        
        scroll_area = QScrollArea()
        scroll_area.setWidget(label)
        scroll_area.setWidgetResizable(True)
        
        layout.addWidget(scroll_area)
        
        close_button = QPushButton("❌ Close")
        close_button.clicked.connect(preview_dialog.close)
        layout.addWidget(close_button)
        
        preview_dialog.exec_()

    def update_thumbnails_after_deletion(self, file_path, deleted_page):
        """刪除頁面後更新縮略圖索引"""
        new_thumbnails = {}
        for (fp, page_num), pixmap in self.page_thumbnails.items():
            if fp == file_path:
                if page_num < deleted_page:
                    new_thumbnails[(fp, page_num)] = pixmap
                elif page_num > deleted_page:
                    new_thumbnails[(fp, page_num - 1)] = pixmap
            else:
                new_thumbnails[(fp, page_num)] = pixmap
        
        self.page_thumbnails = new_thumbnails

    def update_thumbnails_after_deletion_batch(self, file_path, deleted_pages):
        """批量刪除後更新縮略圖索引 - 更高效的版本"""
        if not deleted_pages:
            return
        
        # 按頁面號排序
        sorted_deleted_pages = sorted(deleted_pages, reverse=True)
        
        # 收集所有需要處理的縮略圖
        thumbnails_to_process = {}
        thumbnails_to_remove = []
        
        for (fp, page_num), pixmap in list(self.page_thumbnails.items()):
            if fp == file_path:
                thumbnails_to_process[page_num] = pixmap
                thumbnails_to_remove.append((fp, page_num))
        
        # 清除所有舊的縮略圖索引
        for key in thumbnails_to_remove:
            del self.page_thumbnails[key]
        
        # 重新計算頁面索引
        new_thumbnails = {}
        for old_page_num, pixmap in thumbnails_to_process.items():
            # 計算有多少個刪除的頁面在這個頁面之前
            pages_deleted_before = sum(1 for deleted_page in sorted_deleted_pages 
                                    if deleted_page < old_page_num)
            
            new_page_num = old_page_num - pages_deleted_before
            
            # 只保留仍然有效的頁面
            if new_page_num >= 0:
                new_thumbnails[(file_path, new_page_num)] = pixmap
        
        # 批量添加新的縮略圖索引
        self.page_thumbnails.update(new_thumbnails)

    def closeEvent(self, event):
        """關閉事件處理 - 修復版本"""
        # 停止重排序操作
        if self.is_reordering:
            self.is_reordering = False
            if hasattr(self, 'reorder_worker') and self.reorder_worker and self.reorder_worker.isRunning():
                self.reorder_worker.quit()
                self.reorder_worker.wait(3000)  # 等待最多3秒
        
        if self.modified_files:
            modified_names = []
            for file_path in self.modified_files:
                doc_info = self.pdf_docs[file_path]
                modified_names.append(os.path.basename(doc_info['original_path']))
            
            reply = QMessageBox.question(
                self, "Unsaved Changes", 
                f"The following files have unsaved changes:\n{', '.join(modified_names)}\n\nSave all before closing?",
                QMessageBox.Yes | QMessageBox.No | QMessageBox.Cancel
            )
            
            if reply == QMessageBox.Yes:
                self.save_all_pdfs()
            elif reply == QMessageBox.Cancel:
                event.ignore()
                return
        
        # 停止背景線程
        try:
            self.thumbnail_generator.stop()
            self.thumbnail_generator.quit()
            self.thumbnail_generator.wait(3000)  # 等待最多3秒
        except:
            pass
        
        # 清理文檔和臨時文件
        for file_path, doc_info in self.pdf_docs.items():
            try:
                doc_info['doc'].close()
            except:
                pass
            try:
                temp_dir = os.path.dirname(doc_info['temp_path'])
                shutil.rmtree(temp_dir)
            except:
                pass
        
        event.accept()


class BatchConvertPSDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle("Batch Convert PostScript to PDF")
        self.setMinimumSize(800, 600)
        self.file_paths = []  # 用于存储添加的PostScript文件路径
        self.current_sort_column = -1
        self.sort_order = Qt.AscendingOrder
        self.initUI()

    def initUI(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        # 左侧文件选择区域
        left_layout = QVBoxLayout()

        # 创建 DropArea 并添加到布局
        self.drop_area = PSDropArea()
        self.drop_area.fileDropped.connect(self.files_dropped)
        left_layout.addWidget(self.drop_area)

        # 创建表格部件
        self.table_widget = QTableWidget()
        self.table_widget.setColumnCount(5)
        self.table_widget.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["Item", "File Name", "Document Size (MB)", "Modifly Date", "Conversion status"])
        self.table_widget.setSelectionBehavior(QAbstractItemView.SelectRows)
        self.table_widget.setEditTriggers(QAbstractItemView.NoEditTriggers)
        self.table_widget.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
        self.table_widget.horizontalHeader().sectionClicked.connect(self.sort_files)
        left_layout.addWidget(self.table_widget)

        file_btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.btn_add = QPushButton("Select PostScript files")
        self.btn_add.clicked.connect(self.add_ps_files)
        file_btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_add)

        self.btn_add_folder = QPushButton("Import Folder")
        self.btn_add_folder.clicked.connect(self.add_ps_folder)
        file_btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_add_folder)

        self.btn_remove = QPushButton("Remove Files")
        self.btn_remove.clicked.connect(self.remove_ps_files)
        file_btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_remove)

        self.btn_clear = QPushButton("Clear")
        self.btn_clear.clicked.connect(self.clear_all_files)
        file_btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_clear)

        left_layout.addLayout(file_btn_layout)

        self.total_label = QLabel("PostScript Total：0")
        left_layout.addWidget(self.total_label)

        layout.addLayout(left_layout)

        # 输出设置区域
        output_group = QGroupBox("Output Settings")
        output_layout = QVBoxLayout()

        # 输出文件夹选择
        output_folder_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        output_folder_label = QLabel("Output Folder:")
        self.output_folder_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.output_folder_edit.setReadOnly(True)
        self.output_folder_browse_btn = QPushButton("Browse...")
        self.output_folder_browse_btn.clicked.connect(self.browse_output_folder)
        
        output_folder_layout.addWidget(output_folder_label)
        output_folder_layout.addWidget(self.output_folder_edit)
        output_folder_layout.addWidget(self.output_folder_browse_btn)
        output_layout.addLayout(output_folder_layout)

        # 输出文件名设置
        naming_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.same_name_radio = QRadioButton("Original File name")
        self.same_name_radio.setChecked(True)
        self.custom_prefix_radio = QRadioButton("Add File Prefix:")
        self.prefix_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.prefix_edit.setEnabled(False)
        self.prefix_edit.setPlaceholderText("Enter Prefix")

        self.same_name_radio.toggled.connect(self.toggle_prefix_edit)
        self.custom_prefix_radio.toggled.connect(self.toggle_prefix_edit)

        naming_layout.addWidget(self.same_name_radio)
        naming_layout.addWidget(self.custom_prefix_radio)
        naming_layout.addWidget(self.prefix_edit)
        output_layout.addLayout(naming_layout)

        # 覆盖选项
        self.overwrite_checkbox = QCheckBox("Override")
        output_layout.addWidget(self.overwrite_checkbox)

        output_group.setLayout(output_layout)
        layout.addWidget(output_group)

        # 转换按钮和进度
        self.progress_bar = QProgressBar()
        self.progress_bar.setVisible(False)
        layout.addWidget(self.progress_bar)

        # 状态文本
        self.status_text = QTextEdit()
        self.status_text.setMaximumHeight(150)
        self.status_text.setReadOnly(True)
        layout.addWidget(self.status_text)

        button_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.btn_convert = QPushButton("Start Conversion")
        self.btn_convert.clicked.connect(self.start_conversion)
        self.btn_convert.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold; padding: 10px;")
        
        self.btn_cancel = QPushButton("Cancel")
        self.btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        
        self.btn_open_output = QPushButton("Open Target Folder")
        self.btn_open_output.clicked.connect(self.open_output_folder)
        self.btn_open_output.setEnabled(False)

        button_layout.addWidget(self.btn_convert)
        button_layout.addWidget(self.btn_cancel)
        button_layout.addStretch()
        button_layout.addWidget(self.btn_open_output)

        layout.addLayout(button_layout)

    def toggle_prefix_edit(self):
        self.prefix_edit.setEnabled(self.custom_prefix_radio.isChecked())

    def files_dropped(self, file_paths):
        for path in file_paths:
            if path not in self.file_paths:
                self.file_paths.append(path)
                self.add_table_row(path)
        self.update_total_label()

    def add_ps_files(self):
        file_paths, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileNames(
            self, 
            "选择PostScript文件", 
            "", 
            "PostScript files (*.ps *.eps);;All files (*.*)"
        )
        for path in file_paths:
            if path not in self.file_paths:
                self.file_paths.append(path)
                self.add_table_row(path)
        self.update_total_label()

    def add_ps_folder(self):
        folder_path = QFileDialog.getExistingDirectory(self, "Select All Postscript Files")
        if folder_path:
            ps_files = []
            for root, dirs, files in os.walk(folder_path):
                for file in files:
                    if file.lower().endswith(('.ps', '.eps')):
                        file_path = os.path.join(root, file)
                        if file_path not in self.file_paths:
                            ps_files.append(file_path)
            
            if ps_files:
                self.file_paths.extend(ps_files)
                for path in ps_files:
                    self.add_table_row(path)
                self.update_total_label()
                self.status_text.append(f"Added {len(ps_files)} PostScript Files")
            else:
                QMessageBox.information(self, "Message", "No PostScript file was found in the selected folder")

    def add_table_row(self, path):
        file_name = os.path.basename(path)
        file_size = os.path.getsize(path)
        file_size_mb = file_size / (1024 * 1024)  # 转换为MB
        modification_time = os.path.getmtime(path)
        modification_date = datetime.fromtimestamp(modification_time).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")

        row_count = self.table_widget.rowCount()
        self.table_widget.insertRow(row_count)

        self.table_widget.setItem(row_count, 0, QTableWidgetItem(str(row_count + 1)))
        self.table_widget.setItem(row_count, 1, QTableWidgetItem(file_name))
        self.table_widget.setItem(row_count, 2, QTableWidgetItem(f"{file_size_mb:.2f}"))
        self.table_widget.setItem(row_count, 3, QTableWidgetItem(modification_date))
        self.table_widget.setItem(row_count, 4, QTableWidgetItem("Waiting"))

    def remove_ps_files(self):
        selected_rows = self.table_widget.selectionModel().selectedRows()
        for model_index in reversed(selected_rows):
            row = model_index.row()
            if row < len(self.file_paths):
                file_path = self.file_paths[row]
                self.file_paths.remove(file_path)
                self.table_widget.removeRow(row)
        self.update_item_numbers()
        self.update_total_label()

    def clear_all_files(self):
        self.file_paths.clear()
        self.table_widget.setRowCount(0)
        self.update_total_label()

    def update_item_numbers(self):
        for row in range(self.table_widget.rowCount()):
            self.table_widget.setItem(row, 0, QTableWidgetItem(str(row + 1)))

    def update_total_label(self):
        total_files = len(self.file_paths)
        self.total_label.setText(f"PostScript Total：{total_files}")

    def sort_files(self, column):
        if column == self.current_sort_column:
            self.sort_order = Qt.DescendingOrder if self.sort_order == Qt.AscendingOrder else Qt.AscendingOrder
        else:
            self.current_sort_column = column
            self.sort_order = Qt.AscendingOrder

        try:
            if column == 1:  # 文件名
                self.file_paths.sort(key=lambda path: os.path.basename(path), reverse=self.sort_order == Qt.DescendingOrder)
            elif column == 2:  # 文件大小
                self.file_paths.sort(key=lambda path: os.path.getsize(path), reverse=self.sort_order == Qt.DescendingOrder)
            elif column == 3:  # 修改日期
                self.file_paths.sort(key=lambda path: os.path.getmtime(path), reverse=self.sort_order == Qt.DescendingOrder)
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Sorting Error", f"Error occurred while sorting: {e}")

        self.update_table()

    def update_table(self):
        self.table_widget.setRowCount(0)
        for path in self.file_paths:
            self.add_table_row(path)
        self.update_item_numbers()

    def browse_output_folder(self):
        folder_path = QFileDialog.getExistingDirectory(self, "Select Output Folder")
        if folder_path:
            self.output_folder_edit.setText(folder_path)

    def start_conversion(self):
        if not self.file_paths:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Warning", "Please add the PostScript file to be converted first")
            return

        output_folder = self.output_folder_edit.text()
        if not output_folder:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Warning", "Please select the output folder")
            return

        if not os.path.exists(output_folder):
            try:
                os.makedirs(output_folder)
            except Exception as e:
                QMessageBox.critical(self, "Warning", f"Unable to create output folder: {e}")
                return

        # 开始转换
        self.btn_convert.setEnabled(False)
        self.progress_bar.setVisible(True)
        self.progress_bar.setRange(0, len(self.file_paths))
        self.progress_bar.setValue(0)
        self.status_text.clear()
        self.status_text.append("Start batch conversion of PostScript files...")

        # 创建转换线程
        self.convert_thread = PSConvertThread(
            self.file_paths,
            output_folder,
            self.same_name_radio.isChecked(),
            self.prefix_edit.text() if self.custom_prefix_radio.isChecked() else "",
            self.overwrite_checkbox.isChecked()
        )
        
        self.convert_thread.progress_updated.connect(self.update_progress)
        self.convert_thread.file_converted.connect(self.update_file_status)
        self.convert_thread.conversion_error.connect(self.update_error_status)
        self.convert_thread.conversion_completed.connect(self.conversion_finished)
        self.convert_thread.status_message.connect(self.update_status_message)
        
        self.convert_thread.start()

    def update_progress(self, value):
        self.progress_bar.setValue(value)

    def update_file_status(self, file_index, status):
        if file_index < self.table_widget.rowCount():
            self.table_widget.setItem(file_index, 4, QTableWidgetItem(status))

    def update_error_status(self, file_index, error_message):
        if file_index < self.table_widget.rowCount():
            self.table_widget.setItem(file_index, 4, QTableWidgetItem("Conversion failed"))
        self.status_text.append(f"Error: {error_message}")

    def update_status_message(self, message):
        self.status_text.append(message)

    def conversion_finished(self, successful, total):
        self.btn_convert.setEnabled(True)
        self.progress_bar.setVisible(False)
        self.btn_open_output.setEnabled(True)
        
        self.status_text.append(f"\nConversion completed!: {successful}/{total}")
        
        if successful > 0:
            QMessageBox.information(self, "Conversion completed", f"Successfully converted {successful}/{total} Files")
        else:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Conversion Failed", "No files were converted successfully")

    def open_output_folder(self):
        output_folder = self.output_folder_edit.text()
        if output_folder and os.path.exists(output_folder):
            try:
                if sys.platform == "win32":
                    os.startfile(output_folder)
                elif sys.platform == "darwin":
                    subprocess.call(["open", output_folder])
                else:
                    subprocess.call(["xdg-open", output_folder])
            except Exception as e:
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "Error", f"Unable to open output folder: {e}")


class PSDropArea(QWidget):
    # 自定义信号，用于当文件被拖放时发送文件路径
    fileDropped = pyqtSignal(list)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(PSDropArea, self).__init__(parent)
        self.setAcceptDrops(True)
        self.setFixedHeight(100)  # 设置 DropArea 的高度
        # 设置背景色
        self.setAutoFillBackground(True)
        palette = self.palette()
        palette.setColor(QPalette.Window, QColor('steel blue'))
        self.setPalette(palette)
        self.text = "Drop PostScript Files Here (.ps, .eps)"

    def dragEnterEvent(self, event):
        if event.mimeData().hasUrls():
            event.acceptProposedAction()

    def dropEvent(self, event):
        file_paths = []
        for url in event.mimeData().urls():
            file_path = url.toLocalFile()
            if file_path.lower().endswith(('.ps', '.eps')):
                file_paths.append(file_path)
        
        if file_paths:
            self.fileDropped.emit(file_paths)  # 发送信号，附带文件路径

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        painter = QPainter(self)
        pen = QPen(Qt.black, 2, Qt.DashLine)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        rect = self.rect().adjusted(1, 1, -1, -1)  # 调整矩形以适应边框宽度
        painter.drawRect(rect)

        # 设置文本的字体和颜色
        painter.setPen(QColor('black'))
        painter.setFont(QFont('Arial', 10, QFont.Bold))
        
        # 获取控件中心点并根据文本大小调整位置
        text_rect = QRect(rect.left(), rect.top(), rect.width(), rect.height())
        painter.drawText(text_rect, Qt.AlignCenter, self.text)


class PSConvertThread(QThread):
    progress_updated = pyqtSignal(int)
    file_converted = pyqtSignal(int, str)  # file_index, status
    conversion_error = pyqtSignal(int, str)  # file_index, error_message
    conversion_completed = pyqtSignal(int, int)  # successful_count, total_count
    status_message = pyqtSignal(str)

    def __init__(self, file_paths, output_folder, keep_original_name, prefix, overwrite):
        super().__init__()
        self.file_paths = file_paths
        self.output_folder = output_folder
        self.keep_original_name = keep_original_name
        self.prefix = prefix
        self.overwrite = overwrite
        self.ps_converter = None

    def run(self):
        try:
            # 初始化PostScript转换器
            self.ps_converter = PostScriptConverter()
            successful_count = 0
            
            for i, ps_file_path in enumerate(self.file_paths):
                try:
                    # 生成输出文件名
                    base_name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(ps_file_path))[0]
                    if self.keep_original_name:
                        output_filename = f"{base_name}.pdf"
                    else:
                        output_filename = f"{self.prefix}{base_name}.pdf"
                    
                    output_path = os.path.join(self.output_folder, output_filename)
                    
                    # 检查文件是否已存在
                    if os.path.exists(output_path) and not self.overwrite:
                        self.file_converted.emit(i, "Skipped")
                        self.status_message.emit(f"Skip existing files: {output_filename}")
                        continue
                    
                    # 更新状态
                    self.file_converted.emit(i, "Converting...")
                    self.status_message.emit(f"Converting: {os.path.basename(ps_file_path)}")
                    
                    # 执行转换
                    self.ps_converter.ps_to_pdf(ps_file_path, output_path)
                    
                    # 验证输出文件是否成功创建
                    if os.path.exists(output_path):
                        self.file_converted.emit(i, "Conversion Successful")
                        self.status_message.emit(f"Successful conversion: {os.path.basename(ps_file_path)} -> {output_filename}")
                        successful_count += 1
                    else:
                        self.file_converted.emit(i, "Conversion failed")
                        self.conversion_error.emit(i, f"Output file not created: {output_filename}")
                    
                except Exception as e:
                    self.file_converted.emit(i, "Conversion failed")
                    self.conversion_error.emit(i, f"Convert {os.path.basename(ps_file_path)} 时出错: {str(e)}")
                
                # 更新进度
                self.progress_updated.emit(i + 1)
            
            # 转换完成
            self.conversion_completed.emit(successful_count, len(self.file_paths))
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.status_message.emit(f"An error occurred during batch conversion: {str(e)}")
            self.conversion_completed.emit(0, len(self.file_paths))

# --- PostScript Converter Class ---
class PostScriptConverter:
    """Class to handle PostScript file conversion using Ghostscript"""
    
    def __init__(self, ghostscript_path=None):
        """
        Initialize the PostScript converter
        
        Args:
            ghostscript_path: Path to the Ghostscript executable. 
                            If None, will try to find it automatically.
        """
        self.ghostscript_path = ghostscript_path or self.find_ghostscript()
        if not self.ghostscript_path:
            raise Exception("Ghostscript not found. Please ensure Ghostscript is installed or provide the path.")
    
    def find_ghostscript(self):
        """Try to find Ghostscript executable automatically"""
        possible_paths = [
            # 打包后的路径
            os.path.join(sys._MEIPASS if hasattr(sys, '_MEIPASS') else '.', 'ghostscript', 'bin', 'gswin64c.exe'),
            os.path.join(sys._MEIPASS if hasattr(sys, '_MEIPASS') else '.', 'ghostscript', 'bin', 'gswin32c.exe'),
            # 开发环境路径
            os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'ghostscript', 'bin', 'gswin64c.exe'),
            os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'ghostscript', 'bin', 'gswin32c.exe'),
            # 系统安装路径
            'gswin64c.exe',
            'gswin32c.exe',
            'gs',
            '/usr/bin/gs',
            '/usr/local/bin/gs',
        ]
        
        for path in possible_paths:
            if os.path.isfile(path) or shutil.which(path):
                return path
        
        return None
    
    
    def ps_to_pdf(self, ps_file_path, output_pdf_path=None):
        """
        Convert PostScript file to PDF
        
        Args:
            ps_file_path: Path to the PostScript file
            output_pdf_path: Path for the output PDF (if None, uses temp file)
            
        Returns:
            Path to the generated PDF file
        """
        if not os.path.exists(ps_file_path):
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"PostScript file not found: {ps_file_path}")
        
        if output_pdf_path is None:
            output_pdf_path = tempfile.mktemp(suffix='.pdf')
        
        # Ghostscript command for PDF conversion
        cmd = [
            self.ghostscript_path,
            '-dNOPAUSE',
            '-dBATCH',
            '-dSAFER',
            '-sDEVICE=pdfwrite',
            '-dCompatibilityLevel=1.4',
            '-dPDFSETTINGS=/default',
            f'-sOutputFile={output_pdf_path}',
            ps_file_path
        ]
        
        try:
            # Run Ghostscript
            if sys.platform == "win32":
                creation_flags = subprocess.CREATE_NO_WINDOW
                result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60, creationflags=creation_flags)

            else:
                result = subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
            
            if result.returncode != 0:
                raise Exception(f"Ghostscript error: {result.stderr}")
            
            if not os.path.exists(output_pdf_path):
                raise Exception("PDF conversion failed - output file not created")
            
            return output_pdf_path
            
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            raise Exception("Ghostscript conversion timed out")
        except Exception as e:
            raise Exception(f"Failed to convert PostScript file: {str(e)}")

# --- Launcher Class for Letterhead Manager ---
class LetterheadManagerLauncher:
    def __init__(self):
        self._window_instance = None # To keep track of the window

    def show_window(self):
        """Creates and shows the Letterhead Manager window."""
        try:
            # Optional: Prevent opening multiple instances
            # if self._window_instance and self._window_instance.isVisible():
            #     self._window_instance.activateWindow()
            #     self._window_instance.raise_()
            #     print("Letterhead Manager window already open.") # Or show status bar message
            #     return self._window_instance # Return existing instance

            # Create a new instance
            # Assuming Letterhead_Manager.MainWindow doesn't require arguments
            self._window_instance = Letterhead_Manager.MainWindow()
            self._window_instance.show()
            self._window_instance.activateWindow()
            self._window_instance.raise_()
            print("Launched new Letterhead Manager window.") # Or show status bar message
            return self._window_instance # Return the new instance

        except Exception as e:
            # Use QMessageBox for error display if possible, otherwise print
            try:
                 QMessageBox.critical(None, "Error", f"Failed to launch Letterhead Manager: {e}")
            except:
                 print(f"CRITICAL ERROR launching Letterhead Manager: {e}")
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc()
            return None # Indicate failure



def resource_path(relative_path):
    try:
        base_path = sys._MEIPASS
    except Exception:
        base_path = os.path.abspath(".")
    return os.path.join(base_path, relative_path)
