    def create_general_info_tab(self):
        # 創建一個QWidget作為標籤頁的主控件
        widget = QWidget()
        
        # 創建布局管理器
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # 頂部操作按鈕
        button_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        export_button = QPushButton("導出PDF摘要")
        export_button.clicked.connect(self.export_summary)
        button_layout.addStretch()
        button_layout.addWidget(export_button)
        layout.addLayout(button_layout)

        # 檔案屬性分組
        group_box_file = QGroupBox("檔案屬性")
        layout_file = QVBoxLayout()
        label_path = QLabel(f"<b>文件路徑:</b> {self.pdf_doc.name}")
        label_name = QLabel(f"<b>文件名:</b> {os.path.basename(self.pdf_doc.name)}")
        label_size = QLabel(f"<b>文件大小:</b> {self.format_file_size(os.path.getsize(self.pdf_doc.name))}")
        layout_file.addWidget(label_path)
        layout_file.addWidget(label_name)
        layout_file.addWidget(label_size)
        group_box_file.setLayout(layout_file)

        # 頁面資訊分組
        group_box_page = QGroupBox("頁面資訊")
        layout_page = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # 獲取第一頁尺寸
        page_size = self.pdf_doc[0].rect
        width_pt = page_size.width
        height_pt = page_size.height
        
        # 轉換點數為毫米和英寸
        width_mm = round(width_pt * 0.3528, 2)
        height_mm = round(height_pt * 0.3528, 2)
        width_inch = round(width_pt / 72, 2)
        height_inch = round(height_pt / 72, 2)
        
        # 判斷紙張類型
        paper_type = self.get_paper_type(width_mm, height_mm)
        
        label_page_size = QLabel(f"<b>頁面尺寸:</b> {width_pt} × {height_pt} 點 ({width_mm} × {height_mm} 毫米, {width_inch}\" × {height_inch}\" 英寸)")
        label_paper_type = QLabel(f"<b>紙張類型:</b> {paper_type}")
        label_page_count = QLabel(f"<b>頁數:</b> {self.pdf_doc.page_count}")
        label_orientation = QLabel(f"<b>方向:</b> {'直向' if height_pt > width_pt else '橫向'}")
        
        layout_page.addWidget(label_page_size)
        layout_page.addWidget(label_paper_type)
        layout_page.addWidget(label_page_count)
        layout_page.addWidget(label_orientation)
        group_box_page.setLayout(layout_page)

        # 文件元數據分組
        group_box_meta = QGroupBox("文件元數據")
        layout_meta = QVBoxLayout()
        info = self.pdf_doc.metadata
        
        # 獲取並格式化日期
        creation_date = self.format_pdf_date(info.get('creationDate', '未知'))
        mod_date = self.format_pdf_date(info.get('modDate', '未知'))
        
        label_title = QLabel(f"<b>標題:</b> {info.get('title', '未知')}")
        label_author = QLabel(f"<b>作者:</b> {info.get('author', '未知')}")
        label_subject = QLabel(f"<b>主題:</b> {info.get('subject', '未知')}")
        label_keywords = QLabel(f"<b>關鍵字:</b> {info.get('keywords', '未知')}")
        label_creation_date = QLabel(f"<b>創建日期:</b> {creation_date}")
        label_mod_date = QLabel(f"<b>修改日期:</b> {mod_date}")
        label_producer = QLabel(f"<b>製作者:</b> {info.get('producer', '未知')}")
        label_creator = QLabel(f"<b>創建程序:</b> {info.get('creator', '未知')}")
        
        layout_meta.addWidget(label_title)
        layout_meta.addWidget(label_author)
        layout_meta.addWidget(label_subject)
        layout_meta.addWidget(label_keywords)
        layout_meta.addWidget(label_creation_date)
        layout_meta.addWidget(label_mod_date)
        layout_meta.addWidget(label_producer)
        layout_meta.addWidget(label_creator)
        group_box_meta.setLayout(layout_meta)
        
        # 安全資訊分組
        group_box_security = QGroupBox("安全資訊")
        layout_security = QVBoxLayout()
        
        is_encrypted = "是" if self.pdf_doc.is_encrypted else "否"
        permissions = self.get_pdf_permissions()
        
        label_encrypted = QLabel(f"<b>加密狀態:</b> {is_encrypted}")
        label_permissions = QLabel(f"<b>權限設置:</b> {permissions}")
        
        layout_security.addWidget(label_encrypted)
        layout_security.addWidget(label_permissions)
        group_box_security.setLayout(layout_security)

        # 將分組框添加到布局
        layout.addWidget(group_box_file)
        layout.addWidget(group_box_page)
        layout.addWidget(group_box_meta)
        layout.addWidget(group_box_security)
        layout.addStretch()

        # 設置QWidget的布局
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        
        # 創建一個可滾動區域並設置其內容部件
        scroll_area = QScrollArea()
        scroll_area.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll_area.setWidget(widget)

        # 在標籤頁控件中添加新的標籤頁
        self.tab_widget.addTab(scroll_area, "一般資訊")

    def create_fonts_tab(self):
        # 創建一個QWidget作為標籤頁的主控件
        self.fonts_tab = QWidget()
        
        # 創建總布局管理器
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self.fonts_tab)
        
        # 頂部控件區域
        top_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        
        # 搜索框
        search_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        search_label = QLabel("搜索:")
        self.fonts_search = QLineEdit()
        self.fonts_search.setPlaceholderText("輸入字體名稱進行搜索...")
        self.fonts_search.textChanged.connect(self.filter_fonts)
        search_layout.addWidget(search_label)
        search_layout.addWidget(self.fonts_search)
        
        # 過濾選項
        filter_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.fonts_filter_embedded = QCheckBox("僅顯示嵌入字體")
        self.fonts_filter_embedded.stateChanged.connect(self.filter_fonts)
        self.show_hex_names = QCheckBox("顯示十六進制編碼")
        self.show_hex_names.stateChanged.connect(self.refresh_font_display)
        filter_layout.addWidget(self.fonts_filter_embedded)
        filter_layout.addWidget(self.show_hex_names)
        
        # 匯出按鈕
        export_button = QPushButton("匯出字體列表")
        export_button.clicked.connect(self.export_fonts)
        
        # 加入頂部布局
        top_layout.addLayout(search_layout, 3)
        top_layout.addLayout(filter_layout, 2)
        top_layout.addWidget(export_button, 1)
        layout.addLayout(top_layout)
        
        # 進度條
        self.fonts_progress = QProgressBar()
        self.fonts_progress.setValue(0)
        layout.addWidget(self.fonts_progress)
        
        # 字體信息表格
        self.fonts_table = QTableWidget()
        self.fonts_table.setColumnCount(6)
        self.fonts_table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["名稱", "類型", "字型", "嵌入", "子集", "使用次數"])
        header = self.fonts_table.horizontalHeader()
        header.setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.Stretch)  # 名稱列彈性調整
        header.setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView.ResizeToContents)
        header.setSectionResizeMode(2, QHeaderView.ResizeToContents)
        header.setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView.ResizeToContents)
        header.setSectionResizeMode(4, QHeaderView.ResizeToContents)
        header.setSectionResizeMode(5, QHeaderView.ResizeToContents)
        
        # 設置表格字體為支持中文的字體
        font = QFont("Microsoft YaHei", 8)
        self.fonts_table.setFont(font)
        
        # 添加表格到布局
        layout.addWidget(self.fonts_table)
        
        # 底部資訊區域
        bottom_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.fonts_status_label = QLabel("準備載入字體資訊...")
        show_encoding_help = QPushButton("字體編碼幫助")
        show_encoding_help.clicked.connect(self.show_encoding_help)
        bottom_layout.addWidget(self.fonts_status_label)
        bottom_layout.addStretch()
        bottom_layout.addWidget(show_encoding_help)
        layout.addLayout(bottom_layout)
        
        # 添加標籤頁
        self.tab_widget.addTab(self.fonts_tab, "字體訊息")

    def refresh_font_display(self):
        """根據是否顯示十六進制編碼重新整理字體表格"""
        if not hasattr(self, 'font_list') or not self.font_list:
            return
        
        # 重新套用當前過濾器，這會刷新表格
        self.filter_fonts()

    def create_images_tab(self):
        # 創建一個QWidget作為標籤頁的主控件
        self.images_tab = QWidget()
        
        # 創建總布局管理器
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self.images_tab)
        
        # 頂部控件區域
        top_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        
        # 過濾選項
        filter_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        filter_label = QLabel("頁面:")
        self.images_page_filter = QComboBox()
        self.images_page_filter.addItem("所有頁面")
        for i in range(1, self.pdf_doc.page_count + 1):
            self.images_page_filter.addItem(f"第 {i} 頁")
        self.images_page_filter.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.filter_images)
        filter_layout.addWidget(filter_label)
        filter_layout.addWidget(self.images_page_filter)
        
        # 匯出按鈕
        export_button = QPushButton("匯出圖像列表")
        export_button.clicked.connect(self.export_images)
        
        # 加入頂部布局
        top_layout.addLayout(filter_layout, 3)
        top_layout.addStretch(2)
        top_layout.addWidget(export_button, 1)
        layout.addLayout(top_layout)
        
        # 進度條
        self.images_progress = QProgressBar()
        self.images_progress.setValue(0)
        layout.addWidget(self.images_progress)
        
        # 圖像信息表格
        self.images_table = QTableWidget()
        self.images_table.setColumnCount(7)
        self.images_table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["頁碼", "編號", "寬度", "高度", "位元深度", "色彩空間", "大小(KB)"])
        header = self.images_table.horizontalHeader()
        header.setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView.ResizeToContents)
        header.setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView.ResizeToContents)
        header.setSectionResizeMode(2, QHeaderView.ResizeToContents)
        header.setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView.ResizeToContents)
        header.setSectionResizeMode(4, QHeaderView.ResizeToContents)
        header.setSectionResizeMode(5, QHeaderView.Stretch)
        header.setSectionResizeMode(6, QHeaderView.ResizeToContents)
        
        # 設置表格字體為支持中文的字體
        font = QFont("Microsoft YaHei", 10)
        self.images_table.setFont(font)
        
        # 添加表格到布局
        layout.addWidget(self.images_table)
        
        # 底部資訊區域
        bottom_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.images_status_label = QLabel("準備載入圖像資訊...")
        bottom_layout.addWidget(self.images_status_label)
        layout.addLayout(bottom_layout)
        
        # 添加標籤頁
        self.tab_widget.addTab(self.images_tab, "圖像訊息")

    def create_text_tab(self):
        # 創建一個QWidget作為標籤頁的主控件
        self.text_tab = QWidget()
        
        # 創建總布局管理器
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self.text_tab)
        
        # 頂部控件區域
        top_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        
        # 頁面選擇
        page_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        page_label = QLabel("提取頁面:")
        self.text_page_select = QComboBox()
        self.text_page_select.addItem("所有頁面")
        for i in range(1, self.pdf_doc.page_count + 1):
            self.text_page_select.addItem(f"第 {i} 頁")
        page_layout.addWidget(page_label)
        page_layout.addWidget(self.text_page_select)
        
        # 提取按鈕
        extract_button = QPushButton("提取文本")
        extract_button.clicked.connect(self.extract_text)
        
        # 匯出按鈕
        export_button = QPushButton("導出為文本檔")
        export_button.clicked.connect(self.export_text)
        
        # 加入頂部布局
        top_layout.addLayout(page_layout, 3)
        top_layout.addWidget(extract_button, 1)
        top_layout.addWidget(export_button, 1)
        layout.addLayout(top_layout)
        
        # 進度條
        self.text_progress = QProgressBar()
        self.text_progress.setValue(0)
        layout.addWidget(self.text_progress)
        
        # 文本顯示區域
        self.text_edit = QTextEdit()
        self.text_edit.setReadOnly(True)
        self.text_edit.setFont(QFont("Microsoft YaHei", 10))
        self.text_edit.setPlaceholderText("點擊「提取文本」按鈕開始提取文本內容...")
        layout.addWidget(self.text_edit)
        
        # 添加標籤頁
        self.tab_widget.addTab(self.text_tab, "文本提取")

    def tab_changed(self, index):
        # 檢查是否已加載該標籤頁
        if index not in self.loaded_tabs:
            if index == 1:  # 字體標籤頁
                self.load_fonts_data()
            elif index == 2:  # 圖像標籤頁
                self.load_images_data()
            
            # 標記為已加載
            self.loaded_tabs[index] = True

    def load_fonts_data(self):
        # 創建並啟動字體加載線程
        self.fonts_thread = LoadFontsThread(self.pdf_doc)
        self.fonts_thread.update_signal.connect(self.populate_fonts_table)
        self.fonts_thread.progress_signal.connect(self.update_fonts_progress)
        self.fonts_status_label.setText("正在載入字體資訊...")
        self.fonts_thread.start()

    def load_images_data(self):
        # 創建並啟動圖像加載線程
        self.images_thread = LoadImagesThread(self.pdf_doc)
        self.images_thread.update_signal.connect(self.populate_images_table)
        self.images_thread.progress_signal.connect(self.update_images_progress)
        self.images_status_label.setText("正在載入圖像資訊...")
        self.images_thread.start()

    def extract_text(self):
        # 獲取所選頁面
        selected_index = self.text_page_select.currentIndex()
        pages = None  # 默認提取所有頁面
        
        if selected_index > 0:  # 不是"所有頁面"選項
            pages = [selected_index - 1]  # 將頁碼轉換為從0開始的索引
        
        # 創建並啟動文本提取線程
        self.text_thread = ExtractTextThread(self.pdf_doc, pages)
        self.text_thread.update_signal.connect(self.display_extracted_text)
        self.text_thread.progress_signal.connect(self.update_text_progress)
        self.text_edit.setPlainText("正在提取文本...")
        self.text_thread.start()

    def update_fonts_progress(self, value):
        self.fonts_progress.setValue(value)
        if value == 100:
            self.fonts_status_label.setText("字體資訊載入完成")

    def update_images_progress(self, value):
        self.images_progress.setValue(value)
        if value == 100:
            self.images_status_label.setText("圖像資訊載入完成")

    def update_text_progress(self, value):
        self.text_progress.setValue(value)

    def populate_fonts_table(self, font_list):
        self.font_list = font_list  # 保存完整列表以便過濾
        self.fonts_table.setRowCount(len(font_list))
        
        for row, font in enumerate(font_list):
            name_item = QTableWidgetItem(font["name"])
            type_item = QTableWidgetItem(font["type"])
            family_item = QTableWidgetItem(font["family"])
            embedded_item = QTableWidgetItem(font["embedded"])
            subset_item = QTableWidgetItem(font["subset"])
            count_item = QTableWidgetItem(str(font["count"]))
            
            # 設置嵌入字體的顏色
            if font["embedded"] == "是":
                embedded_item.setForeground(QColor("#4CAF50"))  # 暗色模式下的綠色
            else:
                embedded_item.setForeground(QColor("#F44336"))  # 暗色模式下的紅色
            
            self.fonts_table.setItem(row, 0, name_item)
            self.fonts_table.setItem(row, 1, type_item)
            self.fonts_table.setItem(row, 2, family_item)
            self.fonts_table.setItem(row, 3, embedded_item)
            self.fonts_table.setItem(row, 4, subset_item)
            self.fonts_table.setItem(row, 5, count_item)
        
        # 更新底部狀態信息
        self.fonts_status_label.setText(f"共 {len(font_list)} 個字體")

    def populate_images_table(self, image_list):
        self.image_list = image_list  # 保存完整列表以便過濾
        self.images_table.setRowCount(len(image_list))
        
        total_size = 0
        for row, img in enumerate(image_list):
            page_item = QTableWidgetItem(str(img["page"]))
            index_item = QTableWidgetItem(str(img["index"]))
            width_item = QTableWidgetItem(str(img["width"]))
            height_item = QTableWidgetItem(str(img["height"]))
            bpc_item = QTableWidgetItem(str(img["bpc"]))
            colorspace_item = QTableWidgetItem(str(img["colorspace"]))
            size_item = QTableWidgetItem(f"{img['size_kb']}")
            
            total_size += img["size_kb"]
            
            self.images_table.setItem(row, 0, page_item)
            self.images_table.setItem(row, 1, index_item)
            self.images_table.setItem(row, 2, width_item)
            self.images_table.setItem(row, 3, height_item)
            self.images_table.setItem(row, 4, bpc_item)
            self.images_table.setItem(row, 5, colorspace_item)
            self.images_table.setItem(row, 6, size_item)
        
        self.images_status_label.setText(f"圖像總數: {len(image_list)} | 總大小: {self.format_file_size(total_size * 1024)}")

    def display_extracted_text(self, text):
        self.text_edit.setPlainText(text)

    def filter_fonts(self):
        search_text = self.fonts_search.text().lower()
        show_only_embedded = self.fonts_filter_embedded.isChecked()
        show_hex = self.show_hex_names.isChecked() if hasattr(self, 'show_hex_names') else False
        
        filtered_fonts = []
        for font in self.font_list:
            name_match = search_text in font["name"].lower()
            embedded_match = True if not show_only_embedded else font["embedded"] == "是"
            
            if name_match and embedded_match:
                filtered_fonts.append(font)
        
        self.fonts_table.setRowCount(len(filtered_fonts))
        for row, font in enumerate(filtered_fonts):
            # 處理字體名稱顯示
            font_name = font["name"]
            if show_hex:
                # 將名稱轉換為十六進制表示
                hex_name = ' '.join([f"{ord(c):02X}" for c in font_name])
                name_display = f"{font_name} [HEX: {hex_name}]"
            else:
                name_display = font_name
                
            name_item = QTableWidgetItem(name_display)
            type_item = QTableWidgetItem(font["type"])
            family_item = QTableWidgetItem(font["family"])
            embedded_item = QTableWidgetItem(font["embedded"])
            subset_item = QTableWidgetItem(font["subset"])
            count_item = QTableWidgetItem(str(font["count"]))
            
            # 設置嵌入字體的顏色
            if font["embedded"] == "是":
                embedded_item.setForeground(QColor("#4CAF50"))  # 暗色模式下的綠色
            else:
                embedded_item.setForeground(QColor("#F44336"))  # 暗色模式下的紅色
            
            self.fonts_table.setItem(row, 0, name_item)
            self.fonts_table.setItem(row, 1, type_item)
            self.fonts_table.setItem(row, 2, family_item)
            self.fonts_table.setItem(row, 3, embedded_item)
            self.fonts_table.setItem(row, 4, subset_item)
            self.fonts_table.setItem(row, 5, count_item)
        
        self.fonts_status_label.setText(f"顯示 {len(filtered_fonts)}/{len(self.font_list)} 個字體")

    def show_encoding_help(self):
        """顯示字體編碼幫助對話框"""
        help_text = """
    <h3>字體名稱亂碼問題幫助</h3>
    <p>PDF檔案中的字體名稱可能因為編碼問題顯示為亂碼，特別是包含中文或日文等非ASCII字符的字體名稱。</p>

    <h4>可能原因：</h4>
    <ul>
    <li>字體名稱使用了非UTF-8編碼（如Big5、GBK或Shift-JIS等）</li>
    <li>字體名稱被截斷或損壞</li>
    <li>PDF產生器使用了自定義的字體命名方式</li>
    </ul>

    <h4>解決方法：</h4>
    <ul>
    <li>勾選「顯示十六進制編碼」可查看字體名稱的原始編碼</li>
    <li>如需在其他程式中使用這些字體，可根據十六進制碼嘗試不同的編碼轉換</li>
    <li>一般常見編碼：
        <ul>
        <li>繁體中文：Big5</li>
        <li>簡體中文：GBK/GB2312</li>
        <li>日文：Shift-JIS</li>
        </ul>
    </li>
    </ul>

    <p>注意：程式已嘗試自動修復常見的編碼問題，但部分複雜情況可能需要手動處理。</p>
    """
        msg_box = QMessageBox(self)
        msg_box.setWindowTitle("字體編碼幫助")
        msg_box.setIcon(QMessageBox.Information)
        msg_box.setTextFormat(Qt.RichText)
        msg_box.setText(help_text)
        msg_box.exec_()

    def filter_images(self):
        selected_index = self.images_page_filter.currentIndex()
        
        if selected_index == 0:  # "所有頁面"
            filtered_images = self.image_list
        else:
            page_num = selected_index  # 頁碼從1開始
            filtered_images = [img for img in self.image_list if img["page"] == page_num]
        
        self.images_table.setRowCount(len(filtered_images))
        total_size = 0
        
        for row, img in enumerate(filtered_images):
            page_item = QTableWidgetItem(str(img["page"]))
            index_item = QTableWidgetItem(str(img["index"]))
            width_item = QTableWidgetItem(str(img["width"]))
            height_item = QTableWidgetItem(str(img["height"]))
            bpc_item = QTableWidgetItem(str(img["bpc"]))
            colorspace_item = QTableWidgetItem(str(img["colorspace"]))
            size_item = QTableWidgetItem(f"{img['size_kb']}")
            
            total_size += img["size_kb"]
            
            self.images_table.setItem(row, 0, page_item)
            self.images_table.setItem(row, 1, index_item)
            self.images_table.setItem(row, 2, width_item)
            self.images_table.setItem(row, 3, height_item)
            self.images_table.setItem(row, 4, bpc_item)
            self.images_table.setItem(row, 5, colorspace_item)
            self.images_table.setItem(row, 6, size_item)
        
        if selected_index == 0:
            self.images_status_label.setText(f"圖像總數: {len(filtered_images)} | 總大小: {self.format_file_size(total_size * 1024)}")
        else:
            self.images_status_label.setText(f"第 {selected_index} 頁圖像數: {len(filtered_images)} | 總大小: {self.format_file_size(total_size * 1024)}")

    def export_summary(self):
        file_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "保存PDF摘要", "", "文本文件 (*.txt)")
        if not file_path:
            return
            
        try:
            with open(file_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                f.write(f"PDF檔案摘要\n")
                f.write(f"{'=' * 40}\n\n")
                
                # 檔案屬性
                f.write(f"檔案屬性:\n")
                f.write(f"{'-' * 40}\n")
                f.write(f"文件路徑: {self.pdf_doc.name}\n")
                f.write(f"文件名: {os.path.basename(self.pdf_doc.name)}\n")
                f.write(f"文件大小: {self.format_file_size(os.path.getsize(self.pdf_doc.name))}\n\n")
                
                # 頁面資訊
                page_size = self.pdf_doc[0].rect
                width_pt = page_size.width
                height_pt = page_size.height
                width_mm = round(width_pt * 0.3528, 2)
                height_mm = round(height_pt * 0.3528, 2)
                width_inch = round(width_pt / 72, 2)
                height_inch = round(height_pt / 72, 2)
                paper_type = self.get_paper_type(width_mm, height_mm)
                
                f.write(f"頁面資訊:\n")
                f.write(f"{'-' * 40}\n")
                f.write(f"頁面尺寸: {width_pt} × {height_pt} 點 ({width_mm} × {height_mm} 毫米, {width_inch}\" × {height_inch}\" 英寸)\n")
                f.write(f"紙張類型: {paper_type}\n")
                f.write(f"頁數: {self.pdf_doc.page_count}\n")
                f.write(f"方向: {'直向' if height_pt > width_pt else '橫向'}\n\n")
                
                # 文件元數據
                info = self.pdf_doc.metadata
                creation_date = self.format_pdf_date(info.get('creationDate', '未知'))
                mod_date = self.format_pdf_date(info.get('modDate', '未知'))
                
                f.write(f"文件元數據:\n")
                f.write(f"{'-' * 40}\n")
                f.write(f"標題: {info.get('title', '未知')}\n")
                f.write(f"作者: {info.get('author', '未知')}\n")
                f.write(f"主題: {info.get('subject', '未知')}\n")
                f.write(f"關鍵字: {info.get('keywords', '未知')}\n")
                f.write(f"創建日期: {creation_date}\n")
                f.write(f"修改日期: {mod_date}\n")
                f.write(f"製作者: {info.get('producer', '未知')}\n")
                f.write(f"創建程序: {info.get('creator', '未知')}\n\n")
                
                # 安全資訊
                is_encrypted = "是" if self.pdf_doc.is_encrypted else "否"
                permissions = self.get_pdf_permissions()
                
                f.write(f"安全資訊:\n")
                f.write(f"{'-' * 40}\n")
                f.write(f"加密狀態: {is_encrypted}\n")
                f.write(f"權限設置: {permissions}\n")
                
            QMessageBox.information(self, "導出成功", f"PDF摘要已成功保存到:\n{file_path}")
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "導出錯誤", f"保存PDF摘要時發生錯誤:\n{str(e)}")

    def export_fonts(self):
        if not hasattr(self, 'font_list') or not self.font_list:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "導出錯誤", "還沒有加載字體資訊，請先切換到字體標籤頁加載資料。")
            return
            
        file_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "保存字體列表", "", "CSV檔案 (*.csv)")
        if not file_path:
            return
            
        try:
            with open(file_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                # 寫入標題行
                f.write("名稱,類型,字型,嵌入,子集,使用次數,名稱_HEX\n")
                
                # 獲取當前過濾後的字體
                search_text = self.fonts_search.text().lower()
                show_only_embedded = self.fonts_filter_embedded.isChecked()
                
                for font in self.font_list:
                    name_match = search_text in font["name"].lower()
                    embedded_match = True if not show_only_embedded else font["embedded"] == "是"
                    
                    if name_match and embedded_match:
                        # 為每個字體名稱添加十六進制表示
                        hex_name = ' '.join([f"{ord(c):02X}" for c in font["name"]])
                        f.write(f"\"{font['name']}\",\"{font['type']}\",\"{font['family']}\",{font['embedded']},{font['subset']},{font['count']},\"{hex_name}\"\n")
                
            QMessageBox.information(self, "導出成功", f"字體列表已成功保存到:\n{file_path}")
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "導出錯誤", f"保存字體列表時發生錯誤:\n{str(e)}")

    def export_images(self):
        if not hasattr(self, 'image_list') or not self.image_list:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "導出錯誤", "還沒有加載圖像資訊，請先切換到圖像標籤頁加載資料。")
            return
            
        file_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "保存圖像列表", "", "CSV檔案 (*.csv)")
        if not file_path:
            return
            
        try:
            with open(file_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                # 寫入標題行
                f.write("頁碼,編號,寬度,高度,位元深度,色彩空間,大小(KB)\n")
                
                # 獲取當前過濾後的圖像
                selected_index = self.images_page_filter.currentIndex()
                
                if selected_index == 0:  # "所有頁面"
                    filtered_images = self.image_list
                else:
                    page_num = selected_index  # 頁碼從1開始
                    filtered_images = [img for img in self.image_list if img["page"] == page_num]
                
                for img in filtered_images:
                    f.write(f"{img['page']},{img['index']},{img['width']},{img['height']},{img['bpc']},\"{img['colorspace']}\",{img['size_kb']}\n")
                
            QMessageBox.information(self, "導出成功", f"圖像列表已成功保存到:\n{file_path}")
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "導出錯誤", f"保存圖像列表時發生錯誤:\n{str(e)}")

    def export_text(self):
        if self.text_edit.toPlainText() == "" or self.text_edit.toPlainText() == "正在提取文本...":
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "導出錯誤", "還沒有提取文本，請先點擊「提取文本」按鈕。")
            return
            
        file_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "保存文本內容", "", "文本文件 (*.txt)")
        if not file_path:
            return
            
        try:
            with open(file_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                f.write(self.text_edit.toPlainText())
                
            QMessageBox.information(self, "導出成功", f"文本內容已成功保存到:\n{file_path}")
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "導出錯誤", f"保存文本內容時發生錯誤:\n{str(e)}")

    def format_file_size(self, size_bytes):
        if size_bytes == 0:
            return "0B"
        size_name = ("B", "KB", "MB", "GB", "TB", "PB", "EB", "ZB", "YB")
        i = int(math.floor(math.log(size_bytes, 1024)))
        p = math.pow(1024, i)
        s = round(size_bytes / p, 2)
        return f"{s} {size_name[i]}"

    def format_pdf_date(self, date_str):
        if date_str == '未知':
            return date_str
            
        try:
            # 嘗試解析典型的PDF日期格式 D:YYYYMMDDHHmmSSOHH'mm'
            if date_str.startswith('D:'):
                date_str = date_str[2:]  # 移除 'D:' 前綴
                
                # 提取基本部分
                year = date_str[0:4]
                month = date_str[4:6]
                day = date_str[6:8]
                
                # 檢查是否有時間部分
                if len(date_str) >= 14:
                    hour = date_str[8:10]
                    minute = date_str[10:12]
                    second = date_str[12:14]
                    return f"{year}-{month}-{day} {hour}:{minute}:{second}"
                else:
                    return f"{year}-{month}-{day}"
            return date_str
        except:
            return date_str

    def get_paper_type(self, width_mm, height_mm):
        # 確保長邊為寬度
        w, h = (width_mm, height_mm) if width_mm >= height_mm else (height_mm, width_mm)
        
        # 常見紙張尺寸（毫米）
        paper_sizes = {
            'A0': (841, 1189),
            'A1': (594, 841),
            'A2': (420, 594),
            'A3': (297, 420),
            'A4': (210, 297),
            'A5': (148, 210),
            'B4': (250, 353),
            'B5': (176, 250),
            'Letter': (216, 279),
            'Legal': (216, 356),
            'Tabloid': (279, 432)
        }
        
        # 設定容許誤差（毫米）
        tolerance = 3
        
        for name, (pw, ph) in paper_sizes.items():
            if (abs(w - pw) <= tolerance and abs(h - ph) <= tolerance):
                return name
                
        return f"自定義 ({width_mm} × {height_mm} mm)"

    def get_pdf_permissions(self):
        if not self.pdf_doc.is_encrypted:
            return "完全訪問（未加密文件）"
            
        permissions = []
        try:
            # 嘗試獲取權限信息
            if hasattr(self.pdf_doc, 'allow_printing') and self.pdf_doc.allow_printing:
                permissions.append("允許列印")
            if hasattr(self.pdf_doc, 'allow_copy') and self.pdf_doc.allow_copy:
                permissions.append("允許複製內容")
            if hasattr(self.pdf_doc, 'allow_editing') and self.pdf_doc.allow_editing:
                permissions.append("允許編輯")
            if hasattr(self.pdf_doc, 'allow_annotations') and self.pdf_doc.allow_annotations:
                permissions.append("允許註釋")
            if not permissions:
                permissions.append("僅允許閱讀")
        except:
            permissions.append("無法獲取權限信息")
            
        return "、".join(permissions)

class SortWidget(QDockWidget):
    sort_applied = pyqtSignal(list)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__("Page Sorting", parent)
        self.setAllowedAreas(Qt.LeftDockWidgetArea | Qt.RightDockWidgetArea)

        widget = QWidget(self)
        layout = QVBoxLayout(widget)

        self.page_list = QListWidget()
        self.page_list.itemDoubleClicked.connect(self.jump_to_page)
        self.page_list.setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView.ExtendedSelection)
        layout.addWidget(self.page_list)

        button_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.move_up_button = QPushButton("Up")
        self.move_up_button.clicked.connect(self.move_item_up)
        button_layout.addWidget(self.move_up_button)

        self.move_down_button = QPushButton("Down")
        self.move_down_button.clicked.connect(self.move_item_down)
        button_layout.addWidget(self.move_down_button)

        self.apply_button = QPushButton("Apply")
        self.apply_button.clicked.connect(self.apply_sort)
        button_layout.addWidget(self.apply_button)

        layout.addLayout(button_layout)
        self.setWidget(widget)

    def update_page_list(self, page_count):
        self.page_list.clear()
        for i in range(page_count):
            self.page_list.addItem(f"Page {i+1}")

    def move_item_up(self):
        row = self.page_list.currentRow()
        if row > 0:
            item = self.page_list.takeItem(row)
            self.page_list.insertItem(row - 1, item)
            self.page_list.setCurrentRow(row - 1)

    def move_item_down(self):
        row = self.page_list.currentRow()
        if row < self.page_list.count() - 1:
            item = self.page_list.takeItem(row)
            self.page_list.insertItem(row + 1, item)
            self.page_list.setCurrentRow(row + 1)

    def apply_sort(self):
        order = []
        for i in range(self.page_list.count()):
            item_text = self.page_list.item(i).text()
            page_num = int(item_text.split()[1]) - 1
            order.append(page_num)
        self.sort_applied.emit(order)

    def jump_to_page(self, item):
        page_num = int(item.text().split()[1]) - 1
        self.parent().jump_to_page(page_num)

class DropArea(QWidget):
    # 自定義信號，用於當文件被拖放時發送文件路徑
    fileDropped = pyqtSignal(list)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super(DropArea, self).__init__(parent)
        self.setAcceptDrops(True)
        self.setFixedHeight(100)  # 設置 DropArea 的高度
        # 設置背景色
        self.setAutoFillBackground(True)
        palette = self.palette()
        palette.setColor(QPalette.Window, QColor('medium sea green'))
        self.setPalette(palette)
        self.text = "Drop PDF Files Here"


    def dragEnterEvent(self, event):
        if event.mimeData().hasUrls():
            event.acceptProposedAction()

    def dropEvent(self, event):
        file_paths = [url.toLocalFile() for url in event.mimeData().urls() if url.toString().lower().endswith('.pdf')]
        self.fileDropped.emit(file_paths)  # 發送信號，附帶文件路徑

    def paintEvent(self, event):
        painter = QPainter(self)
        pen = QPen(Qt.black, 2, Qt.DashLine)
        painter.setPen(pen)
        rect = self.rect().adjusted(1, 1, -1, -1)  # 調整矩形以適應邊框寬度
        painter.drawRect(rect)

        # 設置文本的字體和顏色
        painter.setPen(QColor('black'))
        painter.setFont(QFont('Arial', 10, QFont.Bold))
        
        # 獲取控件中心點並根據文本大小調整位置
        text_rect = QRect(rect.left(), rect.top(), rect.width(), rect.height())
        painter.drawText(text_rect, Qt.AlignCenter, self.text)


class SearchWidget(QDockWidget):
    search_requested = pyqtSignal(str)
    jump_to_page_requested = pyqtSignal(int, bool)  # 添加參數指示是否需要高亮

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__("Searching Tools", parent)
        self.setAllowedAreas(Qt.LeftDockWidgetArea | Qt.RightDockWidgetArea)

        widget = QWidget()
        layout = QVBoxLayout(widget)

        self.search_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.search_edit.setPlaceholderText("Enter search text")
        self.search_edit.returnPressed.connect(self.search)
        layout.addWidget(self.search_edit)

        self.search_button = QPushButton("Search")
        self.search_button.clicked.connect(self.search)
        layout.addWidget(self.search_button)

        # 添加高亮選項的勾選框，默認不勾選
        self.highlight_checkbox = QCheckBox("Highlight search results")
        self.highlight_checkbox.setChecked(False)  # 默認不勾選
        layout.addWidget(self.highlight_checkbox)

        # 新增進度條
        self.progress_bar = QProgressBar()
        self.progress_bar.setVisible(False)  # 初始隱藏
        self.progress_bar.setTextVisible(True)
        self.progress_bar.setFormat("Searching... %p%")
        layout.addWidget(self.progress_bar)

        self.result_list = QListWidget()
        self.result_list.itemDoubleClicked.connect(self.jump_to_result)
        self.result_list.setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView.ExtendedSelection)
        layout.addWidget(self.result_list)

        self.result_count_label = QLabel()
        self.result_count_label.setStyleSheet("color: white;")
        layout.addWidget(self.result_count_label)

        self.extract_ALL_button = QPushButton("Extract All Pages")
        self.extract_ALL_button.clicked.connect(self.extract_ALL_pages)
        layout.addWidget(self.extract_ALL_button)

        self.delete_ALL_button = QPushButton("Delete All Pages")
        self.delete_ALL_button.clicked.connect(self.delete_ALL_pages)
        layout.addWidget(self.delete_ALL_button)

        self.clear_button = QPushButton("Clear Result")
        self.clear_button.clicked.connect(self.clear_search)
        layout.addWidget(self.clear_button)

        self.setWidget(widget)

    def showEvent(self, event):
        super().showEvent(event)
        # 使用 QTimer.singleShot 確保焦點設置在 UI 完全顯示後執行
        QTimer.singleShot(0, self.search_edit.setFocus)

    def search(self):
        self.result_list.clear()
        text = self.search_edit.text()
        if text:
            # 在開始搜索前顯示並重置進度條
            self.progress_bar.setValue(0)
            self.progress_bar.setVisible(True)
            self.search_requested.emit(text)
        else:
            self.result_count_label.setText("No input for search.")

    def update_results(self, results):
        self.result_list.clear()
        for page_num, _ in results:
            self.result_list.addItem(f"Page {page_num + 1}")
        self.result_count_label.setText(f"Found {len(results)} results")
        self.progress_bar.setVisible(False) # 搜索完成後隱藏進度條

        # 更新頁面，根據高亮選項決定是否顯示高亮
        if self.parent():
            # 先清除當前所有高亮
            self.parent().clear_highlights()
            
            # 如果勾選了高亮選項，則重新顯示當前頁面以應用高亮
            if self.highlight_checkbox.isChecked():
                self.parent().show_page(self.parent().current_page)

    def clear_result_list(self):
        """清空搜索結果列表和相關顯示內容。"""
        try:
            self.result_list.clear()
            self.result_count_label.setText("")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"清除結果列表時出錯: {e}")

    def extract_pages(self):
        try:
            pages_to_extract = [int(item.text().split()[1]) - 1 for item in self.result_list.selectedItems()]
            if pages_to_extract and self.parent():
                self.parent().extract_pages_by_number(pages_to_extract)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"提取頁面時發生錯誤: {e}")

    def delete_pages(self):
        try:
            pages_to_delete = [int(item.text().split()[1]) - 1 for item in self.result_list.selectedItems()]
            if pages_to_delete and self.parent():
                self.parent().delete_pages_by_number(pages_to_delete)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"刪除頁面時發生錯誤: {e}")

    def jump_to_result(self, item):
        try:
            page_num = int(item.text().split()[1]) - 1
            if self.parent():
                # 如果父窗口有 jump_to_page 方法，使用該方法
                if hasattr(self.parent(), 'jump_to_page_with_highlight'):
                    # 傳遞頁碼和是否高亮的標誌
                    self.parent().jump_to_page_with_highlight(page_num, self.highlight_checkbox.isChecked())
                else:
                    # 向後兼容：如果沒有新方法，使用舊方法
                    self.parent().jump_to_page(page_num)
                    
                    # 如果不需要高亮，則手動清除高亮
                    if not self.highlight_checkbox.isChecked() and hasattr(self.parent(), 'clear_highlights'):
                        self.parent().clear_highlights()
                        self.parent().show_page(page_num)  # 重新顯示頁面
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"跳轉到結果頁面時發生錯誤: {e}")

    def clear_search(self):
        try:
            self.search_edit.clear()
            self.result_list.clear()
            self.result_count_label.setText("")
            self.progress_bar.setVisible(False) # 清除時也隱藏進度條
            
            # 先檢查父對象是否存在，然後再調用其方法
            if self.parent():
                if hasattr(self.parent(), 'clear_highlights'):
                    self.parent().clear_highlights()
                # 確保 search_results 存在後再清除
                if hasattr(self.parent(), 'search_results'):
                    self.parent().search_results.clear()
                
                # 重新顯示當前頁面以刷新視圖
                if hasattr(self.parent(), 'show_page') and hasattr(self.parent(), 'current_page'):
                    self.parent().show_page(self.parent().current_page)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"清除搜索時發生錯誤: {e}")

    def contextMenuEvent(self, event):
        selected_items = self.result_list.selectedItems()
        if selected_items:
            menu = QMenu(self)
            menu.setStyleSheet("""
                QMenu {
                    background-color: #272727; /* 深灰色背景 */
                    color: white; /* 白色文字 */
                    border: 1px solid #666666; /* 浅灰色边框 */
                    border-radius: 10px; /* 菜单边框圆角 */
                }
                QMenu::item {
                    padding: 5px 20px; /* 菜单项内边距 */
                    margin: 2px 2px; /* 菜单项外边距 */
                }
                QMenu::item:selected {
                    background-color: #02F78E; /* 选中时的背景色 */
                    color: black; /* 选中时的文字颜色 */
                    border-radius: 5px; /* 选中项的边框圆角 */
                    padding: 5px 20px; /* 选中项的内边距 */
                }
            """)
            extract_action = menu.addAction("Extract Pages")
            delete_action = menu.addAction("Delete Pages")
            action = menu.exec_(event.globalPos())
            
            if action == extract_action:
                self.extract_pages()
            elif action == delete_action:
                self.delete_pages()

    def extract_ALL_pages(self):
        try:
            # 獲取所有搜索結果頁面編號
            all_items = []
            for i in range(self.result_list.count()):
                all_items.append(self.result_list.item(i))
                
            pages_to_extract = [int(item.text().split()[1]) - 1 for item in all_items]
            
            if pages_to_extract and self.parent():
                self.parent().extract_pages_by_number(pages_to_extract)
            else:
                if self.parent():
                    QMessageBox.information(self, "Information", "No pages to extract.")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"提取所有頁面時發生錯誤: {e}")

    def delete_ALL_pages(self):
        try:
            # 獲取所有搜索結果頁面編號
            all_items = []
            for i in range(self.result_list.count()):
                all_items.append(self.result_list.item(i))
                
            pages_to_delete = [int(item.text().split()[1]) - 1 for item in all_items]
            
            if pages_to_delete and self.parent():
                self.parent().delete_pages_by_number(pages_to_delete)
            else:
                if self.parent():
                    QMessageBox.information(self, "Information", "No pages to delete.")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"刪除所有頁面時發生錯誤: {e}")
            
class MergePDFsDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle("合併PDF文件")
        self.setMinimumSize(600, 300)
        self.file_paths = []  # 用於存儲添加的PDF文件路徑
        self.sort_column = 0
        self.sort_order = Qt.AscendingOrder
        self.initUI()

    def initUI(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        # 創建 DropArea 並添加到佈局
        self.drop_area = DropArea()
        self.drop_area.fileDropped.connect(self.files_dropped)
        layout.addWidget(self.drop_area)

        # 創建表格部件
        self.table_widget = QTableWidget()
        self.table_widget.setColumnCount(5)
        self.table_widget.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(["項目", "文件名", "頁數", "大小 (MB)", "修改日期"])
        self.table_widget.horizontalHeader().sectionClicked.connect(self.sort_files)
        self.table_widget.setSelectionBehavior(QAbstractItemView.SelectRows)
        self.table_widget.setEditTriggers(QAbstractItemView.NoEditTriggers)
        self.table_widget.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
        layout.addWidget(self.table_widget)

        btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        self.btn_add = QPushButton("載入PDF")
        self.btn_add.clicked.connect(self.add_pdf)
        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_add)

        self.btn_remove = QPushButton("移除PDF")
        self.btn_remove.clicked.connect(self.remove_pdf)
        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_remove)

        self.btn_up = QPushButton("上移")
        self.btn_up.clicked.connect(self.move_up)
        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_up)

        self.btn_down = QPushButton("下移")
        self.btn_down.clicked.connect(self.move_down)
        btn_layout.addWidget(self.btn_down)

        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)

        # 創建標籤以顯示PDF文件總數
        self.total_label = QLabel("PDF文件總數：0")
        layout.addWidget(self.total_label)

        self.btn_merge = QPushButton("Merge PDF")
        self.btn_merge.clicked.connect(self.merge_pdfs)
        layout.addWidget(self.btn_merge)

    def files_dropped(self, file_paths):
        for path in file_paths:
            if path not in self.file_paths:
                self.file_paths.append(path)
                self.add_table_row(path)
        self.update_total_label()

    def add_pdf(self):
        file_paths, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileNames(self, "選擇PDF文件", "", "PDF files (*.pdf)")
        for path in file_paths:
            if path not in self.file_paths:
                self.file_paths.append(path)
                self.add_table_row(path)
        self.update_total_label()

    def add_table_row(self, path):
        file_name = os.path.basename(path)
        file_size = os.path.getsize(path)
        file_size_mb = file_size / (1024 * 1024)  # 轉換為MB
        with fitz.open(path) as doc:
            page_count = doc.page_count

        modification_time = os.path.getmtime(path)
        modification_date = datetime.fromtimestamp(modification_time).strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")

        row_count = self.table_widget.rowCount()
        self.table_widget.insertRow(row_count)

        self.table_widget.setItem(row_count, 0, QTableWidgetItem(str(row_count + 1)))
        self.table_widget.setItem(row_count, 1, QTableWidgetItem(file_name))
        self.table_widget.setItem(row_count, 2, QTableWidgetItem(str(page_count)))
        self.table_widget.setItem(row_count, 3, QTableWidgetItem(f"{file_size_mb:.2f}"))
        self.table_widget.setItem(row_count, 4, QTableWidgetItem(modification_date))

    def remove_pdf(self):
        selected_rows = self.table_widget.selectionModel().selectedRows()
        for model_index in reversed(selected_rows):
            row = model_index.row()
            file_path = self.file_paths[row]
            self.file_paths.remove(file_path)
            self.table_widget.removeRow(row)
        self.update_item_numbers()
        self.update_total_label()

    def move_up(self):
        current_row = self.table_widget.currentRow()
        if current_row > 0:
            self.file_paths.insert(current_row - 1, self.file_paths.pop(current_row))
            self.table_widget.insertRow(current_row - 1)
            for col in range(self.table_widget.columnCount()):
                self.table_widget.setItem(current_row - 1, col, self.table_widget.takeItem(current_row + 1, col))
            self.table_widget.removeRow(current_row + 1)
            self.table_widget.selectRow(current_row - 1)
            self.update_item_numbers()

    def move_down(self):
        current_row = self.table_widget.currentRow()
        if current_row < self.table_widget.rowCount() - 1:
            self.file_paths.insert(current_row + 1, self.file_paths.pop(current_row))
            self.table_widget.insertRow(current_row + 2)
            for col in range(self.table_widget.columnCount()):
                self.table_widget.setItem(current_row + 2, col, self.table_widget.takeItem(current_row, col))
            self.table_widget.removeRow(current_row)
            self.table_widget.selectRow(current_row + 1)
            self.update_item_numbers()

    def sort_files(self, column):
        if self.sort_column == column:
            self.sort_order = Qt.AscendingOrder if self.sort_order == Qt.DescendingOrder else Qt.DescendingOrder
        else:
            self.sort_column = column
            self.sort_order = Qt.AscendingOrder

        if column == 1:
            self.file_paths.sort(key=os.path.basename, reverse=self.sort_order == Qt.DescendingOrder)
        elif column == 2:
            self.file_paths.sort(key=lambda path: fitz.open(path).page_count, reverse=self.sort_order == Qt.DescendingOrder)
        elif column == 3:
            self.file_paths.sort(key=os.path.getsize, reverse=self.sort_order == Qt.DescendingOrder)
        elif column == 4:
            self.file_paths.sort(key=os.path.getmtime, reverse=self.sort_order == Qt.DescendingOrder)

        self.table_widget.sortItems(column, self.sort_order)

        self.table_widget.clearContents()
        self.table_widget.setRowCount(0)
        for path in self.file_paths:
            self.add_table_row(path)
            self.update_sort_indicator()
            self.update_item_numbers()

    def update_sort_indicator(self):
        if self.sort_order == Qt.AscendingOrder:
            self.table_widget.horizontalHeader().setSortIndicator(self.sort_column, Qt.AscendingOrder)
        else:
            self.table_widget.horizontalHeader().setSortIndicator(self.sort_column, Qt.DescendingOrder)

    def update_total_label(self):
        total_files = len(self.file_paths)
        self.total_label.setText(f"PDF文件總數：{total_files}")

    def update_item_numbers(self):
        for row in range(self.table_widget.rowCount()):
            self.table_widget.item(row, 0).setText(str(row + 1))

    def merge_pdfs(self):
        if self.file_paths:
            merged_doc = fitz.open()
            for path in self.file_paths:
                with fitz.open(path) as doc:
                    merged_doc.insert_pdf(doc)
            save_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "保存合併後的PDF文件", "", "PDF files (*.pdf)")
            if save_path:
                merged_doc.save(save_path)
                QMessageBox.information(self, "合併完成", "PDF文件合併完成！")
        else:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "無文件", "請先添加要合併的PDF文件。")

class ExtractPagesDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle("Extract Page Options")
        self.setMinimumSize(400, 200)
  
        layout = QVBoxLayout()

        # Create button group for exclusive options
        self.button_group = QButtonGroup()

        # Predefined options group
        predefined_group = QGroupBox("預定義選項")
        predefined_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.radio_odd_pages = QRadioButton("提取單數頁面")
        self.radio_even_pages = QRadioButton("提取雙數頁面")
        self.button_group.addButton(self.radio_odd_pages)
        self.button_group.addButton(self.radio_even_pages)
        predefined_layout.addWidget(self.radio_odd_pages)
        predefined_layout.addWidget(self.radio_even_pages)
        predefined_group.setLayout(predefined_layout)
        layout.addWidget(predefined_group)

        # Multiple pages option group
        multiple_group = QGroupBox("提取倍數頁面")
        multiple_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.radio_multiple_pages = QRadioButton("提取[N]為倍數的頁面")
        self.multiple_pages_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.multiple_pages_edit.setPlaceholderText("輸入倍數")
        self.multiple_pages_edit.setEnabled(False)
        self.radio_multiple_pages.toggled.connect(self.multiple_pages_edit.setEnabled)
        self.button_group.addButton(self.radio_multiple_pages)
        multiple_layout.addWidget(self.radio_multiple_pages)
        multiple_layout.addWidget(self.multiple_pages_edit)
        multiple_group.setLayout(multiple_layout)
        layout.addWidget(multiple_group)

        # Custom pages option group
        custom_group = QGroupBox("自定義頁面")
        custom_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.radio_custom_pages = QRadioButton("自定義頁面")
        self.custom_pages_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.custom_pages_edit.setPlaceholderText("e.g., 1,3,5-7")
        self.custom_pages_edit.setEnabled(False)
        self.radio_custom_pages.toggled.connect(self.custom_pages_edit.setEnabled)
        self.button_group.addButton(self.radio_custom_pages)
        custom_layout.addWidget(self.radio_custom_pages)
        custom_layout.addWidget(self.custom_pages_edit)
        custom_group.setLayout(custom_layout)
        layout.addWidget(custom_group)

        # Complex option group
        complex_group = QGroupBox("複雜選項")
        complex_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.radio_complex_option = QRadioButton("從第 N 頁開始，每 X 頁提取 Z 頁")
        self.n_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.n_edit.setPlaceholderText("N: 起始頁")
        self.x_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.x_edit.setPlaceholderText("X: 間隔頁數")
        self.z_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.z_edit.setPlaceholderText("Z: 提取頁數")
        self.n_edit.setEnabled(False)
        self.x_edit.setEnabled(False)
        self.z_edit.setEnabled(False)
        self.radio_complex_option.toggled.connect(self.toggle_complex_option_enabled)
        self.button_group.addButton(self.radio_complex_option)
        complex_layout.addWidget(self.radio_complex_option)
        complex_layout.addWidget(self.n_edit)
        complex_layout.addWidget(self.x_edit)
        complex_layout.addWidget(self.z_edit)
        complex_group.setLayout(complex_layout)
        layout.addWidget(complex_group)

        # Confirm button
        confirm_button = QPushButton("確認")
        confirm_button.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        layout.addWidget(confirm_button, alignment=Qt.AlignRight)

        self.setLayout(layout)

    def toggle_complex_option_enabled(self, checked):
        self.n_edit.setEnabled(checked)
        self.x_edit.setEnabled(checked)
        self.z_edit.setEnabled(checked)

    def get_extract_options(self):
        if self.radio_odd_pages.isChecked():
            return "odd"
        elif self.radio_even_pages.isChecked():
            return "even"
        elif self.radio_multiple_pages.isChecked() and self.multiple_pages_edit.text().isdigit():
            return "multiple", int(self.multiple_pages_edit.text())
        elif self.radio_custom_pages.isChecked():
            return "custom", self.custom_pages_edit.text()
        elif self.radio_complex_option.isChecked():
            try:
                n = int(self.n_edit.text()) - 1  # Convert to zero-indexed
                x = int(self.x_edit.text())
                z = int(self.z_edit.text())
                return "complex", n, x, z
            except ValueError:
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "Input Error", "Please enter valid integers for N, X, and Z.")
                return None
        return None

class PDFConverter(QThread):
    progress_updated = pyqtSignal(int)
    
    def __init__(self, pdf_path, docx_path):
        super().__init__()
        self.pdf_path = pdf_path
        self.docx_path = docx_path

    def run(self):
        try:
            cv = Converter(self.pdf_path)
            cv.convert(self.docx_path, start=0, end=None)
            cv.close()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"轉換失敗：{e}")

class DeletePagesDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
