        self.n_edit.setEnabled(checked)
        self.x_edit.setEnabled(checked)
        self.z_edit.setEnabled(checked)

    def get_delete_options(self):
        if self.radio_odd_pages.isChecked():
            return "odd"
        elif self.radio_even_pages.isChecked():
            return "even"
        elif self.radio_multiple_pages.isChecked() and self.multiple_pages_edit.text().isdigit():
            return "multiple", int(self.multiple_pages_edit.text())
        elif self.radio_custom_pages.isChecked():
            return "custom", self.custom_pages_edit.text()
        elif self.radio_complex_option.isChecked():
            try:
                n = int(self.n_edit.text()) - 1  # Convert to zero-indexed
                x = int(self.x_edit.text())
                z = int(self.z_edit.text())
                return "complex", n, x, z
            except ValueError:
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "Input Error", "Please enter valid integers for N, X, and Z.")
                return None
        return None

class TextExtractorDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle("視覺化提取PDF區域文字")
        self.setMinimumSize(800, 700) #

        self.doc = None
        self.current_page_num = 0
        self.zoom_level = 1.5
        self.pdf_rect = None
        self.current_pixmap = None

        self.initUI()

    def initUI(self):
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        splitter = QSplitter(Qt.Horizontal) #

        # --- 左側控制面板 ---
        control_panel = QFrame()
        control_panel.setFrameShape(QFrame.StyledPanel)
        control_panel.setMinimumWidth(280) #
        control_layout = QVBoxLayout(control_panel)

        # 1. 檔案選擇
        file_group = QGroupBox("1. 選擇檔案")
        file_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.pdf_path_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.pdf_path_edit.setReadOnly(True)
        self.pdf_path_edit.setPlaceholderText("請點擊按鈕選擇PDF檔案")
        pdf_path_button = QPushButton("瀏覽...")
        pdf_path_button.clicked.connect(self.select_pdf_file)
        file_layout.addWidget(self.pdf_path_edit)
        file_layout.addWidget(pdf_path_button)
        file_group.setLayout(file_layout)
        control_layout.addWidget(file_group)

        # 2. 頁面與縮放控制
        control_group = QGroupBox("2. 預覽控制")
        control_grid = QGridLayout()

        self.prev_page_button = QPushButton("<")
        self.prev_page_button.clicked.connect(self.show_prev_page)
        self.page_edit = QLineEdit("1")
        self.page_edit.setValidator(QIntValidator(1, 9999))
        self.page_edit.returnPressed.connect(self.goto_page)
        self.total_pages_label = QLabel("/ 1")
        self.next_page_button = QPushButton(">")
        self.next_page_button.clicked.connect(self.show_next_page)
        
        control_grid.addWidget(QLabel("頁碼:"), 0, 0)
        control_grid.addWidget(self.prev_page_button, 0, 1)
        control_grid.addWidget(self.page_edit, 0, 2)
        control_grid.addWidget(self.total_pages_label, 0, 3)
        control_grid.addWidget(self.next_page_button, 0, 4)

        self.zoom_out_button = QPushButton("-")
        self.zoom_out_button.clicked.connect(self.zoom_out)
        self.zoom_label = QLabel(f"{int(self.zoom_level * 100)}%")
        self.zoom_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.zoom_in_button = QPushButton("+")
        self.zoom_in_button.clicked.connect(self.zoom_in)

        control_grid.addWidget(QLabel("縮放:"), 1, 0)
        control_grid.addWidget(self.zoom_out_button, 1, 1)
        control_grid.addWidget(self.zoom_label, 1, 2, 1, 2)
        control_grid.addWidget(self.zoom_in_button, 1, 4)

        control_group.setLayout(control_grid)
        control_layout.addWidget(control_group)

        # 3. 選擇提取頁面
        extract_pages_group = QGroupBox("3. 選擇提取頁面範圍")
        extract_pages_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.radio_all_pages = QRadioButton("所有頁面")
        self.radio_current_page = QRadioButton("僅目前預覽頁面")
        self.radio_custom_pages = QRadioButton("自訂頁面:")
        self.custom_pages_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.custom_pages_edit.setPlaceholderText("例如: 1,3,5-8")
        self.custom_pages_edit.setEnabled(False)
        self.radio_all_pages.setChecked(True)
        self.radio_custom_pages.toggled.connect(self.custom_pages_edit.setEnabled)
        extract_pages_layout.addWidget(self.radio_all_pages)
        extract_pages_layout.addWidget(self.radio_current_page)
        extract_pages_layout.addWidget(self.radio_custom_pages)
        extract_pages_layout.addWidget(self.custom_pages_edit)
        extract_pages_group.setLayout(extract_pages_layout)
        control_layout.addWidget(extract_pages_group)

        # 4. 執行提取
        extract_button_group = QGroupBox("4. 執行提取")
        extract_button_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.extract_button = QPushButton("提取到 Excel")
        self.extract_button.clicked.connect(self.extract_to_excel)
        extract_button_layout.addWidget(self.extract_button)
        extract_button_group.setLayout(extract_button_layout)
        control_layout.addWidget(extract_button_group)

        control_layout.addStretch()
        splitter.addWidget(control_panel)

        # --- 右側預覽區域 ---
        preview_group = QGroupBox("在下方區域用滑鼠框選目標")
        preview_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        self.preview_label = PDFImageLabel()
        self.preview_label.selection_finished.connect(self.on_selection_finished)
        self.preview_label.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.preview_label.setStyleSheet("background-color: #555; border: 1px solid #888;")
        
        scroll_area = QScrollArea()
        scroll_area.setWidgetResizable(False) 
        scroll_area.setWidget(self.preview_label)
        
        preview_layout.addWidget(scroll_area)
        preview_group.setLayout(preview_layout)
        splitter.addWidget(preview_group)

        splitter.setSizes([300, 500])
        main_layout.addWidget(splitter)
        self.setLayout(main_layout)

    def select_pdf_file(self):
        file_path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "選擇PDF文件", "", "PDF files (*.pdf)")
        if file_path:
            self.pdf_path_edit.setText(file_path)
            try:
                if self.doc:
                    self.doc.close()
                self.doc = fitz.open(file_path)
                self.total_pages_label.setText(f"/ {self.doc.page_count}")
                self.page_edit.setValidator(QIntValidator(1, self.doc.page_count))
                self.current_page_num = 0
                self.pdf_rect = None
                self.page_edit.setText("1")
                self.show_page_preview()
            except Exception as e:
                QMessageBox.critical(self, "錯誤", f"無法開啟PDF文件: {e}")
                self.doc = None
    
    def zoom_in(self):
        self.zoom_level = min(5.0, self.zoom_level + 0.25)
        self.zoom_label.setText(f"{int(self.zoom_level * 100)}%")
        self.show_page_preview()

    def zoom_out(self):
        self.zoom_level = max(0.5, self.zoom_level - 0.25)
        self.zoom_label.setText(f"{int(self.zoom_level * 100)}%")
        self.show_page_preview()

    def goto_page(self):
        if not self.doc: return
        try:
            page_num = int(self.page_edit.text()) - 1
            if 0 <= page_num < self.doc.page_count:
                self.current_page_num = page_num
                self.show_page_preview()
            else:
                self.page_edit.setText(str(self.current_page_num + 1))
        except ValueError:
            self.page_edit.setText(str(self.current_page_num + 1))

    def show_prev_page(self):
        if self.doc and self.current_page_num > 0:
            self.current_page_num -= 1
            self.page_edit.setText(str(self.current_page_num + 1))
            self.show_page_preview()

    def show_next_page(self):
        if self.doc and self.current_page_num < self.doc.page_count - 1:
            self.current_page_num += 1
            self.page_edit.setText(str(self.current_page_num + 1))
            self.show_page_preview()

    def show_page_preview(self):
        if not self.doc: return
        try:
            page = self.doc.load_page(self.current_page_num)
            mat = fitz.Matrix(self.zoom_level, self.zoom_level)
            pix = page.get_pixmap(matrix=mat)
            img = QImage(pix.samples, pix.width, pix.height, pix.stride, QImage.Format_RGB888)
            self.current_pixmap = QPixmap.fromImage(img)
            self.redraw_preview_with_selection()
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "錯誤", f"無法顯示頁面: {e}")

    def redraw_preview_with_selection(self):
        """重新繪製預覽圖並在上面疊加當前的選取框"""
        if not self.current_pixmap:
            return
        
        pixmap_to_show = self.current_pixmap.copy()

        if self.pdf_rect:
            matrix = fitz.Matrix(self.zoom_level, self.zoom_level)
            pixmap_rect = self.pdf_rect * matrix

            screen_qrect = QRect(
                int(pixmap_rect.x0),
                int(pixmap_rect.y0),
                int(pixmap_rect.width),
                int(pixmap_rect.height)
            )

            painter = QPainter(pixmap_to_show)
            pen = QPen(QColor(255, 0, 0), 2, Qt.SolidLine)
            painter.setPen(pen)
            brush = QBrush(QColor(255, 0, 0, 70))
            painter.setBrush(brush)
            painter.drawRect(screen_qrect)
            painter.end()

        self.preview_label.setPixmap(pixmap_to_show)
        self.preview_label.adjustSize()

    def on_selection_finished(self, selection_qrect):
        """當使用者在PDFImageLabel上完成框選時觸發"""
        if not self.current_pixmap:
            return

        label_size = self.preview_label.size()
        pixmap_size = self.current_pixmap.size()
        offset_x = (label_size.width() - pixmap_size.width()) / 2
        offset_y = (label_size.height() - pixmap_size.height()) / 2
        
        pixmap_qrect = selection_qrect.translated(-int(offset_x), -int(offset_y))

        inverse_matrix = ~fitz.Matrix(self.zoom_level, self.zoom_level)

        # --- **關鍵修正** ---
        # 使用 x() + width() 和 y() + height() 來獲取正確的幾何座標點，
        # 而不是使用 .right() 和 .bottom()。
        self.pdf_rect = fitz.Rect(pixmap_qrect.x(), pixmap_qrect.y(),
                                  pixmap_qrect.x() + pixmap_qrect.width(),
                                  pixmap_qrect.y() + pixmap_qrect.height()) * inverse_matrix
        # --- **結束修正** ---

        QMessageBox.information(self, "選取完成", "已選取區域。請選擇頁面範圍並點擊提取。")
        self.redraw_preview_with_selection()

    def extract_to_excel(self):
        if not self.doc:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "錯誤", "請先選擇一個PDF檔案。")
            return
        if not self.pdf_rect:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "錯誤", "請先在預覽圖上用滑鼠框選要提取的區域。")
            return

        pages_to_process = []
        if self.radio_all_pages.isChecked():
            pages_to_process = range(self.doc.page_count)
        elif self.radio_current_page.isChecked():
            pages_to_process = [self.current_page_num]
        elif self.radio_custom_pages.isChecked():
            try:
                pages_to_process = self.parse_page_range(self.custom_pages_edit.text())
            except ValueError as e:
                QMessageBox.critical(self, "頁碼錯誤", str(e))
                return
        
        if not pages_to_process:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "錯誤", "沒有有效的頁面可供提取。")
            return

        try:
            pdf_path = self.pdf_path_edit.text()
            excel_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "保存Excel文件", os.path.splitext(pdf_path)[0] + "_extracted.xlsx", "Excel Files (*.xlsx)")
            if not excel_path:
                return

            workbook = Workbook()
            sheet = workbook.active
            sheet.title = "提取的文字"

            progress = QProgressDialog("正在提取文字...", "取消", 0, len(pages_to_process), self)
            progress.setWindowModality(Qt.WindowModal)

            all_rows_data = []
            max_columns = 0
            for i, page_num in enumerate(pages_to_process):
                progress.setValue(i)
                if progress.wasCanceled():
                    break
                
                page = self.doc.load_page(page_num)
                text = page.get_text("text", clip=self.pdf_rect, sort=True).strip()
                if text:
                    row_data = [f"第 {page_num + 1} 頁"]
                    lines = text.splitlines()
                    for line in lines:
                        if line.strip():
                            row_data.append(line.strip())
                    
                    all_rows_data.append(row_data)
                    if len(row_data) > max_columns:
                        max_columns = len(row_data)

            progress.setValue(len(pages_to_process))
            
            if not all_rows_data:
                QMessageBox.information(self, "提示", "在指定的區域和頁面範圍內沒有提取到任何文字。")
                return
            
            header = ["頁碼"] + [f"欄位_{i}" for i in range(1, max_columns)]
            sheet.append(header)

            for row_data in all_rows_data:
                sheet.append(row_data)
            
            for i in range(1, sheet.max_column + 1):
                col_letter = get_column_letter(i)
                max_length = 0
                for cell in sheet[col_letter]:
                    try:
                        if len(str(cell.value)) > max_length:
                            max_length = len(str(cell.value))
                    except:
                        pass
                adjusted_width = (max_length + 2)
                sheet.column_dimensions[col_letter].width = adjusted_width

            workbook.save(excel_path)
            QMessageBox.information(self, "成功", f"提取的文字已保存到：{excel_path}")
            
        except Exception as e:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "錯誤", f"提取文本失敗：{e}")

    def parse_page_range(self, range_str):
        if not range_str: return []
        indices = set()
        parts = range_str.split(',')
        for part in parts:
            part = part.strip()
            if '-' in part:
                start, end = map(int, part.split('-'))
                if start > end or start < 1 or end > self.doc.page_count:
                    raise ValueError(f"無效的頁碼範圍: {part}")
                indices.update(range(start - 1, end))
            else:
                page = int(part)
                if page < 1 or page > self.doc.page_count:
                    raise ValueError(f"無效的頁碼: {page}")
                indices.add(page - 1)
        return sorted(list(indices))

    def closeEvent(self, event):
        if self.doc:
            self.doc.close()
        super().closeEvent(event)


class InsertPagesDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None, current_pdf_page_count=0):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle("插入頁面")
        self.setMinimumSize(450, 400) # 稍微增加高度以容納新選項
        
        # 用於儲存最終結果的屬性
        self.output_details = None
        # 從主視窗傳入總頁數，用於驗證
        self.total_pages = current_pdf_page_count

        self.setup_ui()

    def setup_ui(self):
        # 主佈局
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        
        # 1. 建立共用的檔案選擇元件 (僅用於前兩個分頁)
        self.pdf_path_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.pdf_path_edit.setReadOnly(True)
        self.pdf_path_edit.setPlaceholderText("請選擇要插入的 PDF 檔案")
        
        browse_button = QPushButton("瀏覽...")
        browse_button.clicked.connect(self.browse_pdf)

        self.file_selection_widget = QWidget() # 將檔案選擇元件放入一個容器
        file_selection_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.file_selection_widget)
        file_selection_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        file_selection_layout.addWidget(QLabel("選擇PDF文件:"))
        file_selection_layout.addWidget(self.pdf_path_edit)
        file_selection_layout.addWidget(browse_button)
        main_layout.addWidget(self.file_selection_widget)

        # 2. 建立 Tab Widget
        self.tab_widget = QTabWidget()
        self.tab_widget.currentChanged.connect(self.on_tab_changed) # 當分頁切換時，控制檔案選擇元件的顯隱
        main_layout.addWidget(self.tab_widget)

        # 建立三個分頁
        self.single_tab = QWidget()
        self.setup_single_tab()
        self.tab_widget.addTab(self.single_tab, "單次插入 (FROM FILE)")

        self.repeat_tab = QWidget()
        self.setup_repeat_tab()
        self.tab_widget.addTab(self.repeat_tab, "重複插入 (FROM FILE)")

        # --- 新增 ---
        self.blank_page_tab = QWidget()
        self.setup_blank_page_tab()
        self.tab_widget.addTab(self.blank_page_tab, "插入白頁 (BLANK PAGE)")
        # --- 結束新增 ---

        # 3. 建立統一的 OK/Cancel 按鈕
        self.button_box = QDialogButtonBox(QDialogButtonBox.Ok | QDialogButtonBox.Cancel)
        self.button_box.accepted.connect(self.accept)
        self.button_box.rejected.connect(self.reject)
        main_layout.addWidget(self.button_box)

        self.on_tab_changed(0) # 初始化顯隱狀態

    def on_tab_changed(self, index):
        """根據當前分頁，決定是否顯示檔案選擇框"""
        # 只有前兩個分頁需要選擇外部PDF檔案
        self.file_selection_widget.setVisible(index < 2)

    def setup_single_tab(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self.single_tab)
        layout.setSpacing(15)
        self.page_range_edit_single = QLineEdit()
        self.page_range_edit_single.setPlaceholderText("例如: 1, 3, 5-7 (留空表示所有頁面)")
        layout.addWidget(QLabel("要插入的頁碼範圍 (留空=全部):"))
        layout.addWidget(self.page_range_edit_single)
        self.insert_pos_combo = QComboBox()
        self.insert_pos_combo.addItems(["開頭", "結尾", "指定位置"])
        self.insert_pos_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.toggle_page_input)
        self.insert_page_spinbox = QSpinBox()
        self.insert_page_spinbox.setMinimum(1)
        self.insert_page_spinbox.setMaximum(self.total_pages + 1)
        self.insert_page_spinbox.setEnabled(False)
        insert_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        insert_layout.addWidget(QLabel("插入到目標文件的位置:"))
        insert_layout.addWidget(self.insert_pos_combo)
        insert_layout.addWidget(self.insert_page_spinbox)
        layout.addLayout(insert_layout)
        layout.addStretch()

    def setup_repeat_tab(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self.repeat_tab)
        layout.setSpacing(15)
        self.page_range_edit_repeat = QLineEdit()
        self.page_range_edit_repeat.setPlaceholderText("例如: 1 (通常為單頁，如水印)")
        layout.addWidget(QLabel("要重複插入的頁碼 (建議單頁):"))
        layout.addWidget(self.page_range_edit_repeat)
        self.interval_spin = QSpinBox()
        self.interval_spin.setMinimum(1)
        self.interval_spin.setMaximum(99999)
        layout.addWidget(QLabel("每隔 N 頁插入一次:"))
        layout.addWidget(self.interval_spin)
        layout.addStretch()

    def setup_blank_page_tab(self):
        """設定「插入白頁」分頁的UI"""
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self.blank_page_tab)
        layout.setSpacing(15)
        
        # 插入數量
        self.blank_page_count_spinbox = QSpinBox()
        self.blank_page_count_spinbox.setMinimum(1)
        self.blank_page_count_spinbox.setMaximum(1000)
        layout.addWidget(QLabel("插入白頁數量:"))
        layout.addWidget(self.blank_page_count_spinbox)
        
        # 頁面尺寸
        self.blank_page_size_combo = QComboBox()
        self.blank_page_size_combo.addItems(["與當前頁面相同", "A4", "A3", "Letter"])
        layout.addWidget(QLabel("頁面尺寸:"))
        layout.addWidget(self.blank_page_size_combo)
        
        # 頁面方向
        self.blank_page_orientation_combo = QComboBox()
        self.blank_page_orientation_combo.addItems(["直向 (Portrait)", "橫向 (Landscape)"])
        layout.addWidget(QLabel("頁面方向:"))
        layout.addWidget(self.blank_page_orientation_combo)
        
        # 插入位置 (與 single_tab 邏輯相同)
        self.blank_insert_pos_combo = QComboBox()
        self.blank_insert_pos_combo.addItems(["開頭", "結尾", "指定位置"])
        self.blank_insert_pos_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.toggle_blank_page_input)
        self.blank_insert_page_spinbox = QSpinBox()
        self.blank_insert_page_spinbox.setMinimum(1)
        self.blank_insert_page_spinbox.setMaximum(self.total_pages + 1)
        self.blank_insert_page_spinbox.setEnabled(False)
        
        blank_insert_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        blank_insert_layout.addWidget(QLabel("插入到目標文件的位置:"))
        blank_insert_layout.addWidget(self.blank_insert_pos_combo)
        blank_insert_layout.addWidget(self.blank_insert_page_spinbox)
        layout.addLayout(blank_insert_layout)
        
        layout.addStretch()

    def browse_pdf(self):
        file_path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "選擇要插入的PDF文件", "", "PDF files (*.pdf)")
        if file_path:
            self.pdf_path_edit.setText(file_path)

    def toggle_page_input(self):
        is_specific_pos = self.insert_pos_combo.currentText() == "指定位置"
        self.insert_page_spinbox.setEnabled(is_specific_pos)
    
    def toggle_blank_page_input(self):
        is_specific_pos = self.blank_insert_pos_combo.currentText() == "指定位置"
        self.blank_insert_page_spinbox.setEnabled(is_specific_pos)

    def accept(self):
        """重寫 accept 方法，在關閉對話框前進行驗證。"""
        current_tab_index = self.tab_widget.currentIndex()

        # 如果是需要檔案的分頁，進行通用檔案驗證
        if current_tab_index < 2:
            pdf_path = self.pdf_path_edit.text()
            if not pdf_path or not os.path.exists(pdf_path):
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "驗證失敗", "請選擇一個有效的 PDF 檔案。")
                return

        # 根據當前分頁進行特定驗證
        if current_tab_index == 0: # 單次插入
            self.output_details = {
                'mode': 'single',
                'pdf_path': self.pdf_path_edit.text(),
                'page_range': self.page_range_edit_single.text().strip(),
                'insert_position': self.insert_pos_combo.currentText(),
                'insert_page': self.insert_page_spinbox.value()
            }
        elif current_tab_index == 1: # 重複插入
            page_range_str = self.page_range_edit_repeat.text().strip()
            if not page_range_str:
                QMessageBox.warning(self, "驗證失敗", "請指定要重複插入的頁碼。")
                return
            self.output_details = {
                'mode': 'repeat',
                'pdf_path': self.pdf_path_edit.text(),
                'page_range': page_range_str,
                'interval': self.interval_spin.value()
            }
        elif current_tab_index == 2: # 插入白頁
            self.output_details = {
                'mode': 'blank',
                'count': self.blank_page_count_spinbox.value(),
                'size': self.blank_page_size_combo.currentText(),
                'orientation': self.blank_page_orientation_combo.currentText(),
                'insert_position': self.blank_insert_pos_combo.currentText(),
                'insert_page': self.blank_insert_page_spinbox.value()
            }
        
        super().accept()

    def get_insert_details(self):
        return self.output_details
    
class PrintOptionsWidget(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.initUI()
        self.populate_printers()  # Populate the printer combo box

    def initUI(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        layout.setSpacing(10)

        # Setting up Printer Selection
        self.setupPrinterSelection(layout)
        layout.addStretch(1)  # 添加弹性空间

        # 设置纸张大小选项
        self.setupPaperSize(layout)
        layout.addStretch(1)  # 添加弹性空间

        # 设置打印方向选项
        self.setupOrientation(layout)
        layout.addStretch(1)  # 添加弹性空间

        # 设置偏移量选项
        self.setupOffsets(layout)
        layout.addStretch(1)  # 添加弹性空间

        # 设置打印份数选项
        self.setupCopies(layout)
        layout.addStretch(1)  # 添加弹性空间

        # 设置打印页数选项
        self.setupPageRange(layout)
        layout.addStretch(1)  # 添加弹性空间

        # 添加自定义打印比例选项
        self.setupScaleFactor(layout)
        layout.addStretch(1)  # 添加弹性空间

        # 设置双面打印选项
        self.setupDuplex(layout)
        layout.addStretch(1)  # 添加弹性空间

        # 设置全页打印选项（无边距）
        self.setupFitToMargin(layout)
        layout.addStretch(5)  # 添加弹性空间

        # 设置打印按钮
        self.setupPrintButton(layout)
        layout.addStretch(1)

        # 应用布局
        self.setLayout(layout)

    def setupPrinterSelection(self, layout):
        group_box = QGroupBox("Printer Selection")
        group_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        printer_label = QLabel("Select Printer:")
        self.printer_combo = QComboBox()
        group_layout.addWidget(printer_label)
        group_layout.addWidget(self.printer_combo)

        # 添加打印机偏好设置按钮
        self.printer_preferences_button = QPushButton("Printer Preferences")
        self.printer_preferences_button.clicked.connect(self.open_printer_preferences)
        group_layout.addWidget(self.printer_preferences_button)

        group_box.setLayout(group_layout)
        layout.addWidget(group_box)

    def populate_printers(self):
        available_printers = QPrinterInfo.availablePrinters()
        default_printer = QPrinterInfo.defaultPrinter()

        # 添加打印機到下拉選單並標記預設打印機
        for printer in available_printers:
            self.printer_combo.addItem(printer.printerName())
        
        # 設置預設打印機
        index = self.printer_combo.findText(default_printer.printerName())
        if index >= 0:
            self.printer_combo.setCurrentIndex(index)

    def setupPaperSize(self, layout):
        group_box = QGroupBox("Paper Options")
        group_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        paper_size_label = QLabel("Paper Size:")
        self.paper_size_combo = QComboBox()
        self.paper_size_combo.addItems(["A4", "A3", "A5", "Letter"])
        group_layout.addWidget(paper_size_label)
        group_layout.addWidget(self.paper_size_combo)
        group_box.setLayout(group_layout)
        layout.addWidget(group_box)

    def setupOrientation(self, layout):
        group_box = QGroupBox("Orientation")
        group_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        orientation_label = QLabel("Orientation:")
        self.portrait_radio = QRadioButton("Portrait")
        self.landscape_radio = QRadioButton("Landscape")
        self.auto_orientation_radio = QRadioButton("Auto")  # 新增自动选项
        self.auto_orientation_radio.setChecked(True)  # 默认选中自动
        orientation_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        orientation_layout.addWidget(self.portrait_radio)
        orientation_layout.addWidget(self.landscape_radio)
        orientation_layout.addWidget(self.auto_orientation_radio)
        group_layout.addWidget(orientation_label)
        group_layout.addLayout(orientation_layout)
        group_box.setLayout(group_layout)
        layout.addWidget(group_box)

    def set_orientation_based_on_pdf(self, pdf_doc):
        if self.auto_orientation_radio.isChecked():
            # 获取第一页的尺寸
            first_page = pdf_doc[0]
            width, height = first_page.rect.width, first_page.rect.height
            
            if width > height:
                self.landscape_radio.setChecked(True)
            else:
                self.portrait_radio.setChecked(True)

    def get_orientation(self):
        if self.auto_orientation_radio.isChecked():
            # 如果选择了自动，返回 None，稍后再根据 PDF 尺寸决定
            return None
        elif self.landscape_radio.isChecked():
            return QPrinter.Landscape
        else:
            return QPrinter.Portrait

    def setupOffsets(self, layout):
        group_box = QGroupBox("Margin Shift")
        group_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        offsets_label = QLabel("Margin Shift (mm):")
        offsets_layout = QGridLayout()
        self.top_offset_spin = QDoubleSpinBox()
        self.bottom_offset_spin = QDoubleSpinBox()
        self.left_offset_spin = QDoubleSpinBox()
        self.right_offset_spin = QDoubleSpinBox()

        # 允许负值
        self.top_offset_spin.setMinimum(-9999)
        self.bottom_offset_spin.setMinimum(-9999)
        self.left_offset_spin.setMinimum(-9999)
        self.right_offset_spin.setMinimum(-9999)

        offsets_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Top:"), 0, 0)
        offsets_layout.addWidget(self.top_offset_spin, 0, 1)
        offsets_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Bottom:"), 1, 0)
        offsets_layout.addWidget(self.bottom_offset_spin, 1, 1)
        offsets_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Left:"), 0, 2)
        offsets_layout.addWidget(self.left_offset_spin, 0, 3)
        offsets_layout.addWidget(QLabel("Right:"), 1, 2)
        offsets_layout.addWidget(self.right_offset_spin, 1, 3)
        group_layout.addWidget(offsets_label)
        group_layout.addLayout(offsets_layout)
        group_box.setLayout(group_layout)
        layout.addWidget(group_box)

    def setupCopies(self, layout):
        group_box = QGroupBox("Number of Copies")
        group_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        copies_label = QLabel("Copies:")
        self.copies_spin = QSpinBox()
        self.copies_spin.setMinimum(1)
        group_layout.addWidget(copies_label)
        group_layout.addWidget(self.copies_spin)
        group_box.setLayout(group_layout)
        layout.addWidget(group_box)

    def setupFitToMargin(self, layout):
        self.fit_to_margin_checkbox = QCheckBox("Fit to Printer Margin")
        layout.addWidget(self.fit_to_margin_checkbox)

    def setupDuplex(self, layout):
        self.duplex_checkbox = QCheckBox("Enable Duplex Printing")
        layout.addWidget(self.duplex_checkbox)

    def setupPageRange(self, layout):
        group_box = QGroupBox("Page Range")
        group_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        
        # 添加單選按鈕組
        self.page_range_group = QButtonGroup()
        
        # 打印所有頁面選項
        self.all_pages_radio = QRadioButton("All Pages")
        self.all_pages_radio.setChecked(True)  # 默認選中
        self.page_range_group.addButton(self.all_pages_radio)
        group_layout.addWidget(self.all_pages_radio)
        
        # 打印當前頁面選項
        self.current_page_radio = QRadioButton("Current Page")
        self.page_range_group.addButton(self.current_page_radio)
        group_layout.addWidget(self.current_page_radio)
        
        # 自定義頁面範圍選項
        self.custom_range_radio = QRadioButton("Custom Range:")
        self.page_range_group.addButton(self.custom_range_radio)
        
        # 自定義範圍輸入框
        custom_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        custom_layout.addWidget(self.custom_range_radio)
        
        self.page_range_edit = QLineEdit()
        self.page_range_edit.setPlaceholderText("e.g., 1,3,5-7")
        self.page_range_edit.setEnabled(False)  # 初始狀態禁用
        custom_layout.addWidget(self.page_range_edit)
        
        group_layout.addLayout(custom_layout)
        
        # 連接信號以啟用/禁用輸入框
        self.custom_range_radio.toggled.connect(self.page_range_edit.setEnabled)
        
        group_box.setLayout(group_layout)
        layout.addWidget(group_box)

    def setupScaleFactor(self, layout):
        group_box = QGroupBox("Scale Factor")
        group_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        scale_factor_label = QLabel("Scale (%):")
        self.scale_factor_spin = QSpinBox()
        self.scale_factor_spin.setRange(10, 400)  # Allow scaling from 10% to 400%
        self.scale_factor_spin.setValue(100)  # Default to 100%
        group_layout.addWidget(scale_factor_label)
        group_layout.addWidget(self.scale_factor_spin)
        group_box.setLayout(group_layout)
        layout.addWidget(group_box)

    def setupPrintButton(self, layout):
        self.print_button = QPushButton("Print")
        layout.addWidget(self.print_button)

    def open_printer_preferences(self):
        selected_printer_name = self.printer_combo.currentText()
        if selected_printer_name:
            printer = QPrinter()
            printer.setPrinterName(selected_printer_name)
            dialog = QPrintDialog(printer, self)
            dialog.exec_()

    def get_page_range_selection(self):
        """獲取當前選擇的頁面範圍類型"""
        if self.all_pages_radio.isChecked():
            return "all"
        elif self.current_page_radio.isChecked():
            return "current"
        elif self.custom_range_radio.isChecked():
            return "custom"
        return "all"  # 默認值

class PDFViewer(QMainWindow):
    ZOOM_IN_FACTOR = 1.25  # 定义放大因子
    ZOOM_OUT_FACTOR = 1 / ZOOM_IN_FACTOR  # 定义缩小因子
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.letterhead_launcher = LetterheadManagerLauncher() # Create instance of the launcher
        self.config_file = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), 'config.json')
        self.settings = self.load_settings()
        self.doc = None
        self.sort_widget = None
        self.current_page = 0
        self.zoom_ratio = 1.0
        self.min_zoom = 0.25
        self.max_zoom = 4.0
        self.original_pdf_path = None  # 原始PDF文件路徑
        self.temp_pdf_path = None  # 臨時PDF文件路徑
        self.setAcceptDrops(True)  # 啟用拖放功能
        self.search_widget = None
        self.search_results = []  
        self.open_windows = []  # Keep track of open windows 
        self.is_modified = False 
        self.letterhead_launcher = LetterheadManagerLauncher()
        # PostScript related attributes
        self.ps_converter = None
        self.is_postscript_mode = False
        self.original_ps_path = None
        self.temp_ps_pdf_path = None
        self.initUI()
        self.apply_settings()

    def launch_letterhead_manager(self):
        """Launches the Letterhead_Manager window using the launcher class."""
        # Call the launcher's method to show the window
        window = self.letterhead_launcher.show_window()
        if window:
            self.statusBar().showMessage("Launched Letterhead Manager.", 3000)
        else:
            # Error message is handled within the launcher now
            self.statusBar().showMessage("Failed to launch Letterhead Manager.", 3000)

    def open_postscript(self):
        """Open PostScript file and convert to PDF for viewing"""
        initial_dir = self.last_directory if hasattr(self, 'last_directory') else ''
        path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
            self, 
            "選擇 PostScript 文件", 
            initial_dir, 
            "PostScript files (*.ps *.eps);;All files (*.*)"
        )
        
        if path:
            try:
                self.load_postscript(path)
                self.last_directory = os.path.dirname(path)
            except Exception as e:
                self.show_error(f"無法打開 PostScript 文件: {e}")

    def load_postscript(self, ps_path):
        """Load PostScript file by converting it to PDF"""
        try:
            if not self.ps_converter:
                self.ps_converter = PostScriptConverter()
            
            self.progress_bar.setVisible(True)
            self.progress_bar.setRange(0, 0)
            QApplication.processEvents()
            
            # --- Start of Modification ---
            # 1. 直接將 PS 轉為暫存 PDF
            temp_pdf_path = tempfile.mktemp(suffix='.pdf')
            converted_pdf_path = self.ps_converter.ps_to_pdf(ps_path, temp_pdf_path)

            # 2. 直接打開轉換後的 PDF，不再呼叫 self.load_pdf()
            self.doc = fitz.open(converted_pdf_path)
            self.current_page = 0
            self.zoom_ratio = 1.0
            
            # 3. 設置 PostScript 模式的相關屬性
            self.is_postscript_mode = True
            self.original_pdf_path = None # 清除 PDF 路徑
            self.original_ps_path = ps_path
            self.temp_ps_pdf_path = converted_pdf_path # 保存暫存路徑以便後續清理
            self.is_modified = False

            # 4. 更新 UI
            self.show_page(self.current_page)
            self.setWindowTitle(f'PDFDocuEdit Pro - {os.path.basename(ps_path)} (PostScript)')
            file_info = f"PostScript 文件: {ps_path} | 頁數: {self.doc.page_count}"
            self.file_info_label.setText(file_info)
            self.page_num_edit.setText(str(self.current_page + 1))
            self.update_ui_for_postscript_mode()
            # --- End of Modification ---
            
        except Exception as e:
            raise Exception(f"轉換 PostScript 文件時發生錯誤：{e}")
        finally:
            self.progress_bar.setVisible(False)

    def update_ui_for_postscript_mode(self):
        """Update UI elements when in PostScript mode"""
        if self.is_postscript_mode:
            # Disable editing functions that don't make sense for PostScript
            editing_buttons = [
                self.btn_insert_pages,
                self.btn_delete_pages,
                self.btn_extract_pages,
                self.btn_sort_pages,
                self.btn_split
            ]
            
            for button in editing_buttons:
                button.setEnabled(False)
                button.setToolTip("編輯功能在 PostScript 模式下不可用")
        else:
            # Re-enable all buttons when not in PostScript mode
            editing_buttons = [
                self.btn_insert_pages,
                self.btn_delete_pages,
                self.btn_extract_pages,
                self.btn_sort_pages,
                self.btn_split
            ]
            
            for button in editing_buttons:
                button.setEnabled(True)
                button.setToolTip("")

    def convert_ps_to_pdf(self):
        """Convert the current PostScript file to PDF and save it"""
        if not self.is_postscript_mode or not self.original_ps_path:
            self.show_warning("當前沒有載入 PostScript 文件")
            return
        
        try:
            # Choose save location
            initial_name = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(self.original_ps_path))[0] + '.pdf'
            save_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(
                self, 
                "保存為 PDF", 
                initial_name,
                "PDF files (*.pdf)"
            )
            
            if not save_path:
                return
            
            # Show progress
            self.progress_bar.setVisible(True)
            self.progress_bar.setRange(0, 0)
            QApplication.processEvents()
            
            # Convert PostScript to PDF
            self.ps_converter.ps_to_pdf(self.original_ps_path, save_path)
            
            self.show_success(f"PostScript 文件已成功轉換為 PDF：{save_path}")
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.show_error(f"轉換失敗：{e}")
        finally:
            self.progress_bar.setVisible(False)

    def show_sort_widget(self):
        if not self.doc:
            self.show_warning("請先打開一個PDF文件。")
            return

        if self.sort_widget is None:
            # SortWidget 尚未创建，创建并显示
            self.sort_widget = SortWidget(self)
            self.sort_widget.sort_applied.connect(self.apply_page_sorting)
            self.addDockWidget(Qt.RightDockWidgetArea, self.sort_widget)

        # 显示 SortWidget
        self.sort_widget.update_page_list(self.doc.page_count)
        self.sort_widget.show()

    def apply_page_sorting(self, order):
        if self.doc:
            self.doc.select(order)
            self.show_page(0)  # Show the first page after sorting
            self.show_success("Page order has been applied!")
        else:
            self.show_warning("Please open a PDF file first.")
        self.is_modified = True

    def merge_pdfs(self):
        merge_dialog = MergePDFsDialog(self)
        merge_dialog.exec_()  # 显示合并对话框

    def update_file_info(self, path):
        # 設置文件信息標籤
        if self.doc:
            file_info = f"文件路徑: {path} | 頁數: {self.doc.page_count}"
            self.file_info_label.setText(file_info)
        else:
            self.file_info_label.setText("未加載文件")


    def open_text_extractor(self):
        extractor_dialog = TextExtractorDialog(self)
        extractor_dialog.exec_()

    def contextMenuEvent(self, event):
        local_pos = self.image_widget.mapFromGlobal(event.globalPos())

        # 檢查是否點擊在image_label內部
        if self.image_label.geometry().contains(local_pos):
            menu = QMenu(self)
            menu.setStyleSheet("""
                QMenu {
                    background-color: #272727; /* 深灰色背景 */
                    color: white; /* 白色文字 */
                    border: 1px solid #666666; /* 浅灰色边框 */
                    border-radius: 10px; /* 菜单边框圆角 */
                }
                QMenu::item {
                    padding: 5px 20px; /* 菜单项内边距 */
                    margin: 2px 2px; /* 菜单项外边距 */
                }
                QMenu::item:selected {
                    background-color: #02F78E; /* 选中时的背景色 */
                    color: black; /* 选中时的文字颜色 */
                    border-radius: 5px; /* 选中项的边框圆角 */
                    padding: 5px 20px; /* 选中项的内边距 */
                }
            """)

            insert_action = menu.addAction('Insert Page')
            insert_action.triggered.connect(self.insert_pages_current)

            delete_action = menu.addAction("Delete Page")
            delete_action.triggered.connect(self.delete_pages_current)

            extract_action = menu.addAction("Extract Page")
            extract_action.triggered.connect(self.extract_pages_current)

            rotate_action = menu.addAction("Rotate")
            rotate_action.triggered.connect(self.rotate_pages_current)

            Search_action = menu.addAction("Search")
            Search_action.triggered.connect(self.show_search_widget)

            pdf_info_action = menu.addAction("PDF Info")
            pdf_info_action.triggered.connect(self.show_pdf_info)

            menu.exec_(event.globalPos())

    def insert_pages_current(self):
        if not self.doc:
            self.show_warning("請先打開一個PDF文件。")
            return

        insert_pdf_path, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(self, "選擇要插入的PDF文件", "", "PDF files (*.pdf)")
        if insert_pdf_path:
            try:
                page_range, ok = QInputDialog.getText(self, "頁碼範圍", "輸入要插入的頁碼範圍 (例如: 1,3,5-7):")
                if ok:
                    pages_to_insert = self.parse_page_range(page_range)
                    with fitz.open(insert_pdf_path) as insert_doc:
                        self.doc.insert_pdf(insert_doc, from_page=pages_to_insert[0], to_page=pages_to_insert[-1], start_at=self.current_page)
                    self.show_page(self.current_page)
                    self.show_success("頁面插入成功！")
            except Exception as e:
                self.show_error(str(e))
        self.is_modified = True

    def delete_pages_current(self):
        if not self.doc:
            self.show_warning("請先打開一個PDF文件。")
            return

        try:
            self.doc.delete_page(self.current_page)
            self.current_page = max(0, self.current_page - 1)  # Adjust current page index
            self.show_page(self.current_page)
            self.show_success("頁面已刪除！")
        except Exception as e:
            self.show_error(str(e))
        self.is_modified = True

    def extract_pages_current(self):
        if not self.doc:
            self.show_warning("請先打開一個PDF文件。")
            return

        extracted_doc = fitz.open()
        extracted_doc.insert_pdf(self.doc, from_page=self.current_page, to_page=self.current_page)
        save_path, _ = QFileDialog.getSaveFileName(self, "保存提取的頁面", "", "PDF files (*.pdf)")
        if save_path:
            extracted_doc.save(save_path)
            self.show_success("頁面已提取并保存！")
        self.is_modified = True

    def rotate_pages_current(self):
        if not self.doc:
            self.show_warning("請先打開一個PDF文件。")
            return

        angle, ok = QInputDialog.getInt(self, "旋轉角度", "輸入旋轉角度 (90, 180, 270):", min=0, max=360, step=90)
        if ok:
            try:
                page = self.doc.load_page(self.current_page)
                page.set_rotation(angle)
                self.show_page(self.current_page)
                self.show_success("頁面已旋轉！")
            except Exception as e:
                self.show_error(str(e))
        self.is_modified = True    
        
    def initUI(self):
        self.setWindowTitle('PDFDocuEdit Pro - 無檔案')
        
        # 獲取屏幕大小
        screen = QDesktopWidget().screenNumber(QDesktopWidget().cursor().pos())
        screen_size = QDesktopWidget().screenGeometry(screen)
        
        # 設置窗口大小為屏幕大小的75%
        width = int(screen_size.width() * 0.75)
        height = int(screen_size.height() * 0.75)
        
        # 居中顯示
        x = (screen_size.width() - width) // 2
        y = (screen_size.height() - height) // 2
        
        self.setGeometry(x, y, width, height)

        # 設置最小大小，以確保UI元素能夠正常顯示
        self.setMinimumSize(800, 600)

        shortcut_first_page = QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+Home"), self)
        shortcut_first_page.activated.connect(self.goto_first_page)

        shortcut_last_page = QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+End"), self)
        shortcut_last_page.activated.connect(self.goto_last_page)

        shortcut_prev_page = QShortcut(QKeySequence("Page Up"), self)
        shortcut_prev_page.activated.connect(self.show_prev_page)

        shortcut_next_page = QShortcut(QKeySequence("Page Down"), self)
        shortcut_next_page.activated.connect(self.show_next_page)

        search_shortcut = QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+F"), self)
        search_shortcut.activated.connect(self.toggle_search_widget)

        close_shortcut = QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+W"), self)
        close_shortcut.activated.connect(self.close)

        zoom_in_shortcut = QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl++"), self)
        zoom_in_shortcut.activated.connect(self.zoom_in)

        zoom_out_shortcut = QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+-"), self)
        zoom_out_shortcut.activated.connect(self.zoom_out)

        # 旋轉 PDF 向左（逆時針）
        rotate_left_shortcut = QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+{"), self)
        rotate_left_shortcut.activated.connect(self.rotate_pdf_left)

        # 旋轉 PDF 向右（順時針）
        rotate_right_shortcut = QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+}"), self)
        rotate_right_shortcut.activated.connect(self.rotate_pdf_right)

        # Create shortcuts for left and right arrow keys

        self.shortcut_right = QShortcut(QKeySequence(Qt.Key_Right), self)

        # Connect the shortcuts to their respective handlers
        self.shortcut_left.activated.connect(self.show_prev_page)
        self.shortcut_right.activated.connect(self.show_next_page)
